## A股作手 Swing Industry Correlation And Position Discipline Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `为什么你做波段总亏钱？因为没搞懂“行业联动性”和“仓位纪律”这 2 个核心`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-TpR-TnBvjI`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- Main speaker / core figure: not named in the transcript; NotebookLM identifies the speaker as a senior trader sharing prior swing-trading and rolling-position experience.
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed the title and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `Mastering Swing Trading through Industry Correlation and Position Discipline`.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频把波段交易定义成一套`确定性降成本系统`: 先选择高成长、高波动、但联动节奏不完全同步的行业和核心标的，再用行业龙头作为参照物做滚动交易；全程以`50%底仓不动`、`先买再卖看股数`、`先卖再买看金额`和`做T失败强制平仓`来防止波段变成补仓、赌方向或卖飞后的心态失控。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `波段的目的不是暴利, 而是确定性降成本`.
   - 视频把波段的核心收益来源放在反复滚动后的成本下降，而不是一次抓满主升浪。
   - A trade that does not lower cost or improve position quality is not a successful swing operation in this model.

2. `行业联动性是波段选择的核心`.
   - 需要选择有成长性、波动性和资金关注度的行业。
   - 行业之间不能完全同步，否则没有左右切换和节奏差。

3. `相关但不完全同步 > 完全同步 > 完全无关`.
   - 完全同步的组合会同涨同跌，无法通过轮动降成本。
   - 完全无关的组合可能导致资金长期不在风口。
   - 最适合的是同属成长风格、但节奏上有差异的方向。

4. `选行业核心, 不选偏门杂毛`.
   - 波段并不需要寻找冷门小票。
   - 更适合选择行业里有代表性、流动性和跟随性的核心标的。

5. `A动B跟: 用龙头A做参照物, 交易核心B的时间差`.
   - 龙头A快速拉升时, B如果尚未充分反应, 可以成为滚动买入对象。
   - A减速或不再创新高时, B的加仓部分应快速退出。

6. `50%底仓是心理和仓位护栏`.
   - 常规情况下至少一半仓位不动。
   - 它让交易者既不会因为卖飞而心态崩坏，也不会因为满仓被套而失去滚动空间。

7. `先买再卖看股数`.
   - 如果先买入做T，后续卖出时以同等股数为准。
   - 目的不是扩大仓位，而是在不破坏底仓结构的前提下赚取价差。

8. `先卖再买看金额`.
   - 如果先卖出规避风险或高抛，后续买回时以同等金额为准。
   - 目的在于增加股数、降低持仓成本，而不是只让账户现金看起来变多。

9. `确定性高于空间`.
   - 视频强调可重复的小确定性，胜过不确定的大空间。
   - 当A已经停顿、B还在冲时，规则要求尊重参照物，而不是贪最后一段。

10. `做T失败不能转成补仓`.
    - 为做T买入的仓位，如果没有按预期冲高，必须在成本价附近或小亏范围内处理。
    - 一旦把失败T仓转成长持仓，系统就从降成本变成被动加仓。

11. `炸板风险要用概率处理`.
    - NotebookLM transcript extraction identifies a rule: 持仓股炸板且不能回封时，先减掉一半仓位，次日集合竞价或开盘低位买回。
    - The point is not predicting the exact next-day path; the point is using common negative premium risk to protect cost and liquidity.

### Hard Rule Table

| Rule | Observe | Trigger | Action | Disabled / Invalid When | Common Misuse |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 50%底仓死守 | 持仓股数 / 账户仓位 | 常规波动中 | 至少一半仓位不动 | 极端特殊情况或系统性风险另行处理 | 一跌就全割, 一涨就全卖, 后续心态失衡 |
| 行业相关性筛选 | 行业走势 / 板块节奏 | 多个成长行业存在轮动节奏差 | 建立可相互观察的行业池 | 行业完全同步或完全无资金关注 | 把同一产业链高度同步标的误当分散 |
| 核心标的过滤 | 行业内辨识度 / 流动性 / 跟随性 | 行业具备成长性和波动空间 | 只选代表性核心标的B | 偏门、无流动性、无行业代表性 | 低位杂毛看似便宜, 实际无法跟随 |
| A动B跟买入 | 龙头A分时 / 核心B分时 | A直线拉升且B尚未充分反应 | 快速加仓B的可滚动部分 | A只是小幅波动, B已充分补涨, 相关性失效 | 反应太慢后追高B |
| A停B出卖出 | 龙头A分时 | A减速或不再创新高 | 卖出B的当日加仓部分 | A继续强趋势且B仍未补涨时需重新确认 | 看到B继续涨就贪拿, 让做T变被套 |
| 先买再卖看股数 | 交易记录 / 持仓股数 | 先买入做T | 后续卖出同等股数 | 没有底仓, 无法完成A股滚动卖出 | 卖出股数不匹配, 仓位越滚越乱 |
| 先卖再买看金额 | 交易记录 / 现金金额 | 先卖出避险或高抛 | 后续用同等金额买回 | 逻辑已坏或不应买回 | 只盯买回价格, 忘记目标是增股降本 |
| 炸板减半 | 持仓股分时 / 封板状态 | 涨停炸板且不能回封 | 先减掉约一半仓位 | 能强势回封或原交易逻辑另有明确信号 | 幻想尾盘能封死, 承受次日低开 |
| 次日买回降本 | 集合竞价 / 开盘 | 炸板次日出现低开或可买回窗口 | 买回前一日卖出的部分 | 逻辑已彻底破坏或风险继续扩大 | 为追求最低点而迟疑, 失去增股机会 |
| T仓强制平仓 | 当日加仓成本线 / 分时 | 加仓后未按预期冲高或反向下跌 | 在成本附近或小亏内处理 | 无 | 把失败T仓留成长期补仓 |
| 盘后成本复盘 | 当日交易流水 / 持仓成本 | 每个交易日结束 | 计算成本是否真实下降、股数是否增加 | 无 | 只看浮盈浮亏, 不检查系统目标 |

### Trading System

#### Market / Sector Ecology

Positive environment:

- Market has active high-growth sectors.
- The sector has enough波动 and public recognition to create tradable intraday reactions.
- Several related sectors are active but not perfectly synchronized.
- Core leaders can create visible intraday reference signals.

Negative environment:

- No clear active sector or no liquidity.
- Candidate industries are perfectly synchronized, removing rotation value.
- Sector movement is too scattered to identify a reference leader.
- The trader cannot execute fast enough to complete the rolling leg.

Manual rule:

- Do not start with "which stock will rise most"; start with "which sector pair can create repeated cost-reduction windows."

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- In a high-growth or high-volatility direction.
- Industry core or representative company, not an obscure follower.
- Has visible liquidity and intraday response.
- Can be paired with a clear reference leader A.
- Has enough existing position to support rolling operations.

Negative filters:

- Cheap but non-core.
- Same-industry or same-chain exposure that moves exactly together with existing holdings.
- A stock that cannot be sold or bought back according to the intended rolling sequence.
- A name whose relationship with A is imagined rather than repeatedly observable.

#### Entry

Allowed entry:

1. Identify reference leader A and tradable core B before the trade.
2. Watch A's intraday acceleration.
3. If A clearly launches and B has not fully reacted, add the rolling portion in B.
4. Record the exact quantity if this is a `先买再卖` operation.

Do not enter when:

- A only moves slightly and the signal is not decisive.
- B has already completed the follow move.
- The trade cannot be exited the same day from existing shares.
- The reason for entry is "I want to lower cost" rather than a real A-to-B trigger.

#### Exit

Normal exit:

- If A slows, stops making intraday highs, or loses the reference role, sell the B rolling portion.
- If the T trade fails, exit near cost or with a small loss; do not convert it into补仓.

Risk exit:

- If a held name炸板 and cannot回封, reduce roughly half according to the transcript's extracted rule.
- The next-day buyback is a cost-reduction tool only if the thesis has not fully broken.

#### Position Management

Core constraints:

- Keep at least 50% base position under normal conditions.
- The rolling portion is a tool, not a new long-term bet.
- `先买再卖`: quantity equality.
- `先卖再买`: capital equality.

Manual adaptation:

- Beginners should reduce rolling frequency until they can record every operation's quantity, amount, and post-trade cost.
- The model is execution-heavy; without fast order handling and clear reference signals, smaller size is safer for learning.

#### Review / Feedback

Every completed roll should answer:

- Did cost decrease?
- Did share count increase when using `先卖再买`?
- Did cash increase or cost fall when using `先买再卖`?
- Did the 50% base position remain intact?
- Did any failed T become hidden补仓?

### Daily Execution Flow

#### 盘前: Build The Correlation Map

1. List active high-growth sectors.
2. Remove sectors that are too synchronized with each other.
3. For each sector, choose:
   - reference leader A,
   - tradable core target B,
   - expected A-to-B response pattern.
4. Mark current base position and rolling capacity.

#### 盘中: Execute Only On Confirmed A-to-B Triggers

1. Watch reference leader A first.
2. If A launches and B lags, add B's rolling portion.
3. If A slows or stops making highs, sell the rolling portion.
4. If the trade moves against the plan, exit the rolling portion near cost or small loss.

#### 尾盘: Risk And Position Cleaning

1. Check whether any failed board cannot回封.
2. Apply the extracted rule of reducing roughly half when炸板 cannot repair.
3. Confirm no rolling add-on has turned into accidental overnight exposure.
4. Confirm base position remains around the intended 50% floor.

#### 盘后: Cost And Rule Audit

1. Calculate whether total holding cost actually declined.
2. Check whether quantity / amount equality rules were followed.
3. Review whether A's signal was strong enough or merely imagined.
4. Update the sector-pair map: which pairs had real time lag, which moved together.
5. Record every case where greed delayed exit.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- Select high-growth sectors with imperfect synchronization.
- Use core leaders as reference signals.
- Keep a 50% base position.
- Apply quantity equality for `先买再卖`.
- Apply amount equality for `先卖再买`.
- Separate rolling position from long holding position.
- Audit post-trade cost and share count.

Hard to copy:

- Very fast intraday reaction speed.
- Sensing exactly when leader A is slowing.
- Judging whether B has "not fully reacted" or is already late.
- Emotional calm after selling too early.
- Experience distinguishing temporary炸板 from true failure.

Manual adaptation:

- Copy the accounting rules and process first.
- Treat speed and盘感 as skills to train, not assumptions the beginner already owns.

### Common Misuse

1. Turning `做T` into补仓.
   - The most dangerous failure is refusing to exit the rolling add-on when it fails.

2. Confusing correlation with duplication.
   - Holding two stocks that always move together does not create rotation value.

3. Treating any leader move as a signal.
   - A small or unclear move in A is not enough.

4. Using B as a junk follower.
   - The model requires core B, not random low-position stocks.

5. Violating the accounting rule.
   - If the trader cannot state the buy quantity, sell quantity, sell amount, and buyback amount, the operation is not controlled.

6. Overusing the model in poor liquidity.
   - Without active sector movement, rolling becomes random noise.

7. Mistaking cost reduction for risk removal.
   - Lower cost helps psychology and flexibility, but does not fix a broken thesis.

### Comparison With Existing Frameworks

#### Reinforces Trader Probability Discipline

- Both frameworks prioritize repeatable positive expectancy over single-trade prediction.
- This video adds a concrete micro-accounting layer: quantity equality, amount equality, and cost audit.

#### Reinforces Dahuzi Mechanical Systems

- Both replace vague feeling with visible triggers and fixed rules.
- Dahuzi's market-breadth model gates when to buy; this framework gates how to roll once a qualified sector/holding exists.
- Dahuzi's ten-day-line framework explicitly avoids doing T in that model; do not mix it with this rolling-position model unless the trade mode is clearly separated.

#### Reinforces Chen Xiaoqun Mainline/Core Thinking

- Both reject杂毛 and emphasize core targets.
- Key difference: Chen Xiaoqun tends to trade the market's core leader directly in a short-term mainline system; this video often uses leader A as the signal and trades core B's lag.
- The conflict is solved by mode labeling: if B is a real core with stable response, it can be a rolling target; if B is just a weak follower, skip.

#### Connects To Baozong And Qianniuge Sector Frameworks

- Baozong and Qianniuge help classify institutional/growth sectors and volume cycles.
- This video operates one level lower: once active growth sectors exist, it explains how to use sector correlation and position accounting to lower cost.

#### Connects To Beijing Chaojia / Limit-Up Risk Control

- The炸板减半 rule is a risk-control bridge to limit-up failure thinking.
- It should not become a reason to chase boards; in this framework it is mainly a holding-risk and buyback-cost rule.

#### Aligns With Aizai Bingchuan

- Follow the screen, not prediction.
- If wrong, cut the wrong part quickly.
- Do not let subjective hope override objective price behavior.

### Review Questions

1. Did I choose sectors with real growth activity and enough volatility?
2. Are my sector holdings too synchronized to create rotation value?
3. Who is reference leader A, and why is it qualified as the signal?
4. Is B truly an industry core target, or only a cheap follower?
5. Did A clearly launch, or did I buy B from imagination?
6. When A slowed, did I exit B's rolling portion without greed?
7. Did I keep at least 50% base position intact?
8. For `先买再卖`, did the sold quantity equal the bought quantity?
9. For `先卖再买`, did the buyback amount equal the sold amount?
10. Did today's operation actually lower cost or increase share count?
11. Did any failed T position become hidden补仓?
12. If a holding炸板 and could not回封, did I follow the risk plan?
13. Did I chase the lowest buyback point and miss the intended cost window?
14. Is the thesis still alive, or am I only using a lower cost to comfort myself?
15. Which industry-pair relationship was proven today, and which one failed?

### Future Distillation Targets

When adding future videos on this speaker or related swing / rolling-position systems, look for:

- More exact definitions of `行业联动性`.
- Examples of valid and invalid industry pairs.
- How to choose reference leader A and tradable core B.
- Whether the 50% base position is fixed or situation-dependent.
- Exact handling of炸板后次日买回 when the stock opens high instead of low.
- How this system changes in institution-led trends, hot-money phases, and low-liquidity markets.
- Practical journal templates for tracking quantity equality, amount equality, and cost reduction.

## 2. Ai Zai Bingchuan Quote Framework

## Ai Zai Bingchuan Quote Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `游资教父“爱在冰川”（相城采莲路）81条语录完整版：上集`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FKKQMtZ5xW0`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Publish date shown by YouTube: `4 Jun 2025`
- Duration shown by YouTube: `12:47`
- Core figure identified by NotebookLM transcript: `爱在冰川` / `相城采莲路`
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. YouTube's own page showed no public captions in the player, but NotebookLM successfully imported `1 source` and generated a source summary.
- Coverage note: although the video title says `81条语录完整版：上集`, NotebookLM's transcript-based index could confirm items `1-43`; item `44` was visible as a heading-like fragment but not confirmable as a complete rule. Do not treat this note as the full 81-item set.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

爱在冰川这条上集的核心不是单一买法，而是一套短线投机的生存框架：承认预判的不可靠，以市场确认和资金流向做跟随；在新旧题材轮转、龙头与板块共生、炸板率、人心变化、业绩雷区和监管规则之间寻找大概率；一旦题材退潮、个股走弱或自我节奏不对，先止损和休息。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `预判基本靠蒙，跟随基本靠等`.
   - 交易者可以有假设，但不能把假设当现实。
   - 真正的执行依据是盘面确认、资金选择和强弱反馈。
   - 配套动作是：做对了死拿，做错了咔嚓。

2. `投机写在人性里`.
   - A股短线机会的底层不是公式，而是人性中的贪婪、恐惧、从众和反身性。
   - 因此短线复盘必须看人心数据：涨停、炸板、连板、成交、板块扩散、亏钱效应。

3. `周期靠轮转，仓位跟周期`.
   - 研究大周期和小周期的交替，不是为了预测每一天，而是为了知道该进攻、轻仓还是休息。
   - 周期差到极点时，新一波机会可能在酝酿；但酝酿不等于立刻买。

4. `题材炒新不炒旧`.
   - 新周期出现后，老热点容易被资金抛弃。
   - 指数反弹初期老题材可能先轮动补涨，但随后能成为主线的通常是新东西。

5. `北向资金优先于技术形态`.
   - 在权重、指数和创业板核心资产的判断里，先看北向资金流入力度，再看技术图形。
   - 北向资金没有配合时，单独的金叉、超跌和形态不能构成强依据。

6. `主线需要中军连续上推`.
   - 题材不是靠口号成立，而是要有大成交中军连续推升，形成赚钱效应。
   - 对主线应当先大胆假设，再小心求证。

7. `龙头要让所有人都能上车`.
   - 真正的龙头通常不是一直一字封死不给机会，而是有流动性、有换手、有承接、有辨识度。
   - 它能容纳不同资金上车，并通过分歧换手继续凝聚人气。

8. `龙头吃板块溢价，也被板块反噬`.
   - 龙头上涨会带动板块，板块走强又给龙头溢价。
   - 当板块集体杀跌，妖股会受到负反馈，不能幻想龙头永远独活。

9. `炸板率是人心指标`.
   - 炸板率不是冷数据，而是市场承接、恐高和信心变化的量化表现。
   - 高炸板率通常意味着次日接力困难，除非已经进入极端冰点后的修复阶段。

10. `4月20-30日几乎不做`.
    - 年报密集期末端容易集中暴露业绩雷，晚披露业绩往往质量差。
    - 这是本框架里少见的硬时间避险规则。

11. `下跌后的上涨多为缩量`.
    - 企稳后的上涨不一定需要立刻放量；缩量上涨可能说明筹码锁定和分歧减少。
    - 异常放量反而可能是阶段转折或兑现信号。

12. `任何时候觉得不对就休息`.
    - 休息不是失败，而是防止节奏错位扩大亏损。
    - 连续亏损、盘面看不懂、炸板率恶化、日期进入风险窗口时，休息优先级高于寻找机会。

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Core view:

- The A-share short-term market is a human-nature market. 投机、抱团、恐慌、追新 and反身性 will keep recurring.
- Market opportunities often brew when conditions look terrible, but the correct response is not immediate bottom-fishing; wait for the new cycle to reveal itself.
- Logic is often completed by price. When funds choose a direction and prices rise, explanations and narratives are strengthened after the fact.

Positive ecology:

- New theme appears after old theme fatigue.
- Large成交中军 pushes continuously.
- 龙头 has liquidity and lets broad capital participate.
- 炸板率 is controlled, next-day premium still exists.
- 北向资金 or other incremental money supports index/core assets.

Negative ecology:

- Old hot theme is in decline and traders still expect a second wave without proof.
- High炸板率 signals deteriorating接力.
- Board collapses create negative feedback into the leader.
- Date enters late April earnings minefield.
- Trader cannot explain exit before entry.

Manual rule:

- If market ecology is unclear, write down the hypothesis and wait for confirmation. If the ecology is bad and you feel something is wrong, rest.

#### Theme Cycle

Cycle logic:

1. Old themes rotate first during index rebound.
2. New themes then gradually show strength.
3. If a new theme has large成交中军 and赚钱效应, it may become the next mainline.
4. If the new theme cannot create a core leader or sector diffusion, it remains only a short-lived branch.

Theme filters:

- Fresh enough: not a fully exhausted old narrative.
- Simple enough: the market can understand it in one sentence.
- Strong enough: has large成交 anchors and visible赚钱效应.
- Timely enough: month-start themes may have better extension.
- Confirmed enough: not only a trader's private story.

#### Leader And Sector Relationship

True leader signals:

- Everyone can board: the stock provides liquidity and turnover, not just inaccessible one-word boards.
- It carries sector premium and can反哺 the sector.
- It has high traffic, high turnover, and scarcity.
- It can survive disagreement better than followers.
- It has反预期 potential even when it does not immediately limit up.

Leader risk signals:

- Sector followers and中军 collapse first.
- Board premium disappears.
- The leader loses the ability to affect peers.
- It becomes a pure情怀 hold after the板块溢价 is gone.

Manual rule:

- If the sector kills and the leader no longer反哺 the sector, reduce leader expectations first; do not call it独立妖 unless the market proves it.

#### Entry Logic

Allowed entry premises:

1. The trade belongs to a visible new or current mainline.
2. There is a high-probability pool, such as yesterday's limit-up names or the strongest active sector.
3. A large成交中军 is confirming the theme.
4. The target is a true liquid leader or credible leader candidate.
5. The stop and take-profit plan are written before entry.

Lower-quality entry premises:

- Buying because the stock is low-priced or超跌 but not in a mainline.
- Buying an old theme because it feels familiar.
- Buying only because of a technical pattern while北向/资金流 is unfavorable.
- Buying after abnormal放量 in a move that has already run far.

#### Exit And Rest

Hard exits:

- Theme heat has clearly faded and stock is in a downtrend.
- Holding is justified only by hope or outside opinions.
- Sector kills and leader loses板块溢价.
- Stop-loss condition written before entry is triggered.

Rest triggers:

- You feel the market is wrong or unreadable.
- Consecutive losses appear.
- 炸板率 rises sharply.
- Late April earnings minefield arrives.
- Your mainline hypothesis cannot find中军 confirmation.

Manual rule:

- `休息肯定是对的` should be treated as a position rule, not a mood slogan.

### If/Then Rulebook

1. If a theme's heat fades and a stock enters a down path, then exit; do not wait for imagined repair.
2. If you are making a mainline hypothesis, then require a large成交中军 to keep pushing before acting with size.
3. If the market is extremely poor, then prepare the candidate list but wait for confirmation of the new cycle.
4. If the date is April 20-30, then default to lowest participation unless a rare, verified opportunity justifies risk.
5. If北向资金 supports index/core assets, then technical shapes have higher reliability; if it does not, downgrade the shape.
6. If a leader is so locked that nobody can board, then be careful calling it a true broad-market leader.
7. If a leader has liquidity, turnover, sector response, and scarcity, then it has stronger dragon quality.
8. If炸板率 rises sharply, then reduce next-day接力 expectation.
9. If炸板率 is extreme after a long decline, then watch for repair but still avoid blind size.
10. If an advance after decline keeps shrinking volume and price rises steadily, then do not reject it only because it lacks volume.
11. If abnormal volume appears after a long shrink-rise, then watch for end-of-move risk.
12. If监管规则 is clear, then rule transparency may become a speculative label; if rules become arbitrary, avoid the setup.
13. If a stock weakens relative to peers, then汰弱留强; do not hold weak names because of sunk cost.
14. If a妖股 fails to limit up but still has反预期 and sector heat, then observe its resilience; do not mechanically kill it without context.
15. If you feel tired, confused, angry, or eager to win back losses, then stop trading and review after close.

### Copyable Practices

- Keep a daily `new vs old theme` map.
- Mark whether today's strongest theme has a large成交中军.
- Track北向资金 when trading index/core-weight directions.
- Track daily炸板率 and next-day premium.
- Maintain a late-April risk calendar.
- Before entry, write target, invalidation, expected holding logic, and what will make you rest.
- After close, classify each trade as prediction, confirmation, or revenge.
- Build a watchlist from high-probability pools, especially yesterday's limit-up names and the strongest current sectors.
- Separate core leaders from followers by liquidity, sector response, and ability to let capital board.
- Treat `休息` as an executable action in the playbook.

### Easy Misuses

1. Turning `预判靠蒙` into never preparing.
   - Correct use: prepare hypotheses, then let the market confirm.

2. Turning `跟随靠等` into passive bag-holding.
   - Correct use: wait for confirmation before entry and cut immediately when invalidated.

3. Treating北向资金 as a universal signal.
   - Correct use: strongest for index/core-weight contexts; weaker for pure small-cap情绪局.

4. Chasing every new theme.
   - Correct use: only new themes with中军、赚钱效应 and sector diffusion deserve attention.

5. Calling an inaccessible一字板 the best leader.
   - Correct use: focus on broad participation and healthy turnover.

6. Assuming龙头 can ignore its sector forever.
   - Correct use: watch板块负反馈; when the sector kills, leader risk rises.

7. Misreading缩量上涨.
   - Correct use: context matters. Shrink-rise after decline can be healthy; high-level缩量加速 after huge profit may be dangerous.

8. Challenging监管 when rules are unclear.
   - Correct use: only clear, stable rules can create rule-game premium.

9. Ignoring annual report minefield.
   - Correct use: late April is a system-level risk window, not a stock-picking challenge.

10. Treating rest as emotional weakness.
    - Correct use: rest is capital preservation and rhythm repair.

### Alignment With Existing Frameworks

#### Reinforces 炒股养家

- Both emphasize crowd psychology, money-making effect, and following rather than predicting.
- 爱在冰川's炸板率 rule is a concrete data proxy for养家's `得人心者得天下`.
- Both treat risk diffusion as a reason to retreat.

#### Reinforces 游资成长共性

- Both reject random trading and insist on主流题材、市场合力、复盘 and quick correction.
- 爱在冰川 adds more explicit annual seasonality,北向资金 and regulatory-game observations.

#### Reinforces 龙头战法

- Both reject pre-naming leaders and require market selection.
- The phrase `所有人都能上得了车` complements the dragon framework's emphasis on liquidity and healthy participation.
- Potential tension: dragon-leader notes warn against high-level dangerous缩量, while爱在冰川 says下跌后的上涨 often shrinks volume. Resolve by context: bottom/repair shrink-rise can be healthy; high-level缩量加速 after many boards can be dangerous.

#### Reinforces 北京炒家

- 北京炒家 is more mechanical about boundary, filtering, and risk control.
- 爱在冰川 gives the ecology behind why certain filters matter:题材退潮 must cut,炸板率 predicts接力 difficulty, and rest is always valid.

#### Reinforces Limit-Up Certainty

- Both focus on high-probability pools and确定性.
- 爱在冰川's `从昨日涨停里找连板` connects with涨停作为流动性/时间/方向不确定性压缩器.
- The特停规则 point adds a regulatory-certainty dimension.

#### Reinforces Trader Probability Discipline

- `炒股就是干大概率的事` and `收益率成正态分布` directly match the probability discipline framework.
- Rest after confusion or loss protects against破产率 and emotional rule-breaking.

#### Reinforces Chen Xiaoqun

- 陈小群框架的主线核心龙头 can be read as a later operational extension of爱在冰川's ideas.
- Shared spine: do not predict, follow current主线; focus on core leader; correct fast.
- 爱在冰川 adds a broader market ecology layer:北向资金、新旧题材、炸板率、特停规则 and late-April calendar risk.

### Non-Copyable Edges

- Deep盘感 about which theme will receive market imagination.
- Emotional capacity to hold a true leader through disagreement.
- Ability to read对手盘 in real time.
- Large-network information flow or veteran intuition.
- The psychological toughness behind `做对了死拿，做错了咔嚓`.

Copy the procedures, not the mythology.

### Review Questions

1. 今天我是预判交易，还是跟随市场确认？
2. 当前主线是新题材，老题材补涨，还是没有主线？
3. 我假设的主线有没有大成交中军连续上推？
4. 目标是不是所有人都能上车的换手龙，而不是不可参与的一字幻觉？
5. 板块是否仍在给龙头溢价，还是已经对龙头形成负反馈？
6. 今日炸板率是否提示明天接力困难？
7. 北向资金是否支持我正在交易的指数/权重方向？
8. 今天是否处在4月20-30日这种财报雷区？
9. 我的止盈止损是否在买入前已经写清楚？
10. 手中弱股有没有及时汰弱留强？
11. 这笔交易是大概率池里的机会，还是低价/超跌安慰？
12. 我有没有把外盘、消息、他人观点当成自己系统的替代品？
13. 如果盘面感觉不对，我是否真的执行了休息？
14. 我有没有在亏损后试图更频繁地交易来打回来？
15. 这条规则能重复执行，还是只是在解释一次运气？

### Future Distillation Directions

- Find and distill the lower episode or remaining 81-item continuation if available.
- Verify whether item 44 and later items introduce a separate `股市赚钱本质` module.

- Build a separate `炸板率/接力环境` mechanical tracker if future videos provide thresholds.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条 Asking 视频的可沉淀核心是：短线交易不是靠孤立技术形态取胜，而是在大盘、板块和赚钱效应形成强势确定性时，提前用静态计划锁定市场高度认可的题材龙头，顺从全市场资金合力，并用`心态七分、技术三分`的纪律控制手脚；真正可复制的是借势、题材分级、计划复盘和四要素体系，不可直接复制的是对`临界点`、`盘感`和大资金主动引导的直觉。

### Source-Grounded Principles

1. `借势而为`.
   - The transcript emphasizes borrowing the force of the market, sector, or money-making demonstration effect.
   - A trade is weaker when it relies only on an isolated stock pattern.

2. `大局观高于技术`.
   - Similar-looking stocks can behave differently because the broad market context is different.
   - The source frames success as a problem of understanding the whole market, not only entries.

3. `只判强弱, 不预测多空`.
   - The transcript says the useful task is judging current market strength, not predicting the future.
   - Operation is allowed only after the trader can classify the environment as supportive.

4. `龙头看启动时机和带动性`.
   - A leader is not defined by board size or order volume alone.
   - It should rise at the key moment and pull related stocks with it.

5. `题材认可度重于基本面`.
   - A stock rises because funds flow in; performance or fundamentals may be only one reason funds use.
   - The more important short-term test is whether the market recognizes the theme.

6. `市场合力大于单路资金`.
   - A single hot-money route can ignite a move, but the durable run needs broader short-term capital participation.
   - The source uses this to separate "point fire" from real market合力.

7. `反舒服买入`.
   - If a very hot stock can be bought comfortably, the transcript warns that it may already be near exhaustion.
   - Strong scarcity and crowding are treated as a signal of strength; easy entry late in heat is suspicious.

8. `题材要分大中小`.
   - Daily review should classify themes by size and likely persistence.
   - The source does not provide a quantitative classification formula.

9. `静态选股优于盘中动态冲动`.
   - A plan made after review is preferred to chasing whatever moves intraday.
   - This is a control mechanism against randomness.

10. `操作体系必须有四要素`.
    - The transcript explicitly frames the system around stock selection, buying, selling, and position control.
    - Missing any one of these leaves the system incomplete.

11. `心态七分, 技术三分`.
    - The explicit ratio is used as a discipline priority: technical methods matter, but control of hands, patience, and execution matter more.

12. `下跌期不操作`.
    - When the market is in a falling phase, the transcript treats not operating as the best protection.
    - This is a hard defense rule, not a vague preference.

13. `盈利也要审计`.
    - Profitable trades should be reviewed to separate good buy point, correct mainstream capture, and luck.
    - This prevents the trader from turning luck into false confidence.

14. `主动发力吸引跟风` has a strict boundary.
    - The source mentions active force / attracting followers as putting capital in a favorable position.
    - It does not provide the minimum capital size or operational conditions; do not translate this into a small-account rule.

### Parameter Audit

| Parameter | Source Context | Can Become A Rule? | Audit Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `10万到10亿` | Title / growth result framing | No | Outcome narrative, not an operating parameter. |
| `200-300万 vs 1000万` | Order / sealing comparison used to explain大局观 over isolated technique | No | Context is clear enough as an example, but not a fixed threshold. |
| `7:3` | 心态 : 技术 importance | Yes | Use as resource-allocation emphasis: prioritize discipline and psychological control. |
| `四要素` | 选股、买入、卖出、仓位 | Yes | A complete system must cover all four parts. |
| `盘后 / 次日` | Review and next-day execution flow | Yes | Use as process rule: static review first, next-day plan execution. |
| `临界点` | Market / sector turning point | No | The source does not provide a quantitative signal. Treat as advanced judgment. |
| `大 / 中 / 小题材` | Theme classification | Partial | Classification is required, but exact standards are not provided. |
| `舒服买入` | Hot-stock exhaustion warning | Partial | Useful qualitative warning; no measurable transaction condition provided. |

### Hard Rules

| Rule | Trigger | Action | Forbidden | Risk Boundary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 借势规则 | Market, sector, or money-making effect is clearly strong | Participate in the strongest direction | Isolated stock picking against the environment | The source does not quantify "strong"; require your own tested thermometer. |
| 弱势禁令 | Market is in a falling phase | Do nothing / hold cash | Force trades or casual rebound fishing | Falling markets create补跌 risk; source says no operation is best. |
| 强弱优先 | Future direction is uncertain but current strength can be judged | Operate only when current strength is confirmed | Trade from forecasts | Prediction is not the operating edge in this source. |
| 龙头带动 | A stock rises at a key moment and related names respond | Treat it as a leader candidate | Define leader only by order size or first movement | No带动性 means no confirmed leader. |
| 题材认可 | Multiple candidates exist | Prefer the one with market-recognized theme | Buy because fundamentals look good but funds do not recognize it | Fundamentals alone do not create short-term upward force. |
| 合力验证 | A hot-money route or large order appears | Check whether broader short-term funds continue entering | Blindly follow a single route | Single money can ignite;合力 sustains. |
| 反舒服买入 | A very hot stock becomes easy and comfortable to buy | Become cautious or avoid chasing | Treat easy late entry as bargain | "Comfortable" is qualitative; do not turn it into an exact rule without review. |
| 题材分级 | Daily review after market close | Classify themes into small / medium / large and study persistence | Treat every hotspot equally | The source gives no scoring formula; keep this as a review task. |
| 静态选股 | Preparing for next session | Build a planned watchlist | Intraday random dynamic stock selection | Plan can still be wrong; execution must react to market strength. |
| 四要素完整 | Building or reviewing a system | Define selection, buy, sell, and position rules | Only optimize entry | Missing sell or position control breaks the system. |
| 盈利审计 | A trade makes money | Identify whether profit came from buy point, mainstream theme, or luck | Assume profit equals skill | Luck misread as skill creates future overconfidence. |
| 主动发力边界 | Considering "lead and attract followers" behavior | Treat as large-capital / advanced behavior unless proven otherwise | Small account tries to点火 or manipulate rhythm | Required capital size and conditions are not provided. |

### Anti-Rules

1. Do not treat `10万到10亿` as a roadmap or position-size target.
2. Do not define a leader by封单 size alone.
3. Do not buy a stock only because the K-line looks familiar.
4. Do not confuse market prediction with market strength classification.
5. Do not chase a hot stock simply because it becomes easy to buy.
6. Do not copy "active force to attract followers" without capital, liquidity, and compliance boundaries.
7. Do not call every daily hotspot a big theme.
8. Do not skip review just because a trade was profitable.
9. Do not build a system with entry rules but no sell or position rules.
10. Do not mistake single-route ignition for durable market合力.

### Trading System Skeleton

#### Market Ecology

Positive environment:

- The broad market is not in a falling phase.
- A clear money-making effect exists.
- A sector or theme is receiving recognition.
- Leader candidates show带动性.
- The trader can classify current strength without relying on prediction.

Negative environment:

- Market is falling.
- The trader cannot identify current strength.
- Hotspots are fragmented and unranked.
- A stock is rising alone without associated response.
- The trade depends on a single route, single order, or private belief.

Manual rule:

- First answer: what force am I borrowing? If no answer, do not trade.

#### Theme And Leader Selection

Positive filters:

- Theme is highly recognized by the market.
- Theme can be classified as small / medium / large in review.
- Stock has leader behavior: it rises first around a key moment and pulls related names.
- Stock is in the line of current money-making effect.
- Capital appears to be entering as合力, not only one route.

Negative filters:

- Good fundamentals but no funds flowing in.
- Isolated rise without sector response.
- A name that becomes easy to buy after already being very hot.
- A "leader" chosen only by order size.
- A trade picked intraday because it moved, outside the static plan.

#### Entry

Allowed entry logic:

1. Market / sector / money-making effect is supportive.
2. The target is a theme-recognized leader candidate.
3. The target has带动性 or broad资金合力.
4. The trade exists in the static plan or passes the same written criteria.

Avoid:

- Forced rebound trades in falling markets.
- Dynamic intraday chasing with no prior classification.
- Comfortable late entry in an already crowded hot stock.
- Following a single hot-money route without合力.

#### Exit

The source provides fewer concrete exit rules than entry / environment rules. Do not invent exact stops.

Confirmed exit / defense concepts:

- In a falling market, do not operate.
- For超跌反弹, the transcript says to grab once and leave; no exact holding time is provided.
- Sell and position rules must exist as part of the four-element system.
- If the reason for entry was market strength, loss of that strength should trigger review.

Missing:

- Fixed stop-loss percentage.
- Batch sell method.
- Time-based exit.
- Clear profit-taking formula.

#### Review Process

Daily review tasks:

- Identify market strength and whether a falling phase exists.
- Identify current money-making effect.
- Classify themes by size and persistence.
- Build a static next-day watchlist.
- Review profitable trades and separate luck from skill.
- Update the four system components: selection, buy, sell, and position.

### Copyable Versus Non-Copyable

Copyable:

- Borrow market / sector / money-making effect.
- Refuse operation in falling markets.
- Use static selection and next-day planning.
- Classify themes by size, even if the standard must be built by review.
- Define selection, buy, sell, and position rules as a complete system.
- Audit profitable trades instead of celebrating them blindly.
- Treat mental discipline as the primary training domain.

Hard to copy:

- Judging the exact market / sector `临界点`.
- Using灵感 or感觉 without a written process.
- Active force to attract followers.
- Maintaining seven-parts mental control in fast markets.
- Distinguishing ignition from durable合力 in real time.

### Relation To Existing Frameworks

#### Youzi Growth Common Framework

This video strengthens the common hot-money path: technical forms must obey mainstream theme, money-making effect, market合力, and review discipline. It adds a concise operating priority: borrow force first, then pick stocks.

#### Dragon Leader Strategy

This video's leader definition overlaps with the dragon framework through带动性. It is less tactical than the dragon ladder framework; it does not provide board-count confirmation such as first, second, or third board.

#### Chaogu Yangjia Heart Method

The overlap is strong: emotion, crowd recognition, market-wide money flow, and discipline matter more than isolated technical prediction. Asking's `心态七分` aligns with the Yangjia emphasis on emotional control.

#### Chen Xiaoqun Mainline Core Leader

The source does not name Chen Xiaoqun, but the focus on market-recognized leaders and mainstream force overlaps with Chen's core-leader logic. Asking is more explicit about static planning and daily review.

#### Beijing Chaojia Framework

The source does not name Beijing Chaojia. The overlap is system completeness and discipline: selection, buy, sell, and position control. It does not provide Beijing-style mechanical sell markers or position formulas.

### Review Questions

1. What force am I borrowing: market, sector, or money-making effect?
2. Is the market strong enough to operate, or is this a falling phase?
3. Am I judging current strength, or am I predicting the future?
4. Is this stock a leader because it has带动性, or only because it rose first?
5. Does the theme have real market recognition?
6. Is this a broad合力 move, or only one route / one order?
7. Is buying now too comfortable for a very hot stock?
8. Was this target in my static plan, or did the intraday chart lure me in?
9. Do I have all four rules: selection, buy, sell, and position?
10. If this trade wins, how will I separate skill from luck in review?

## 4. Baozong Institutional Trend Framework

- Source file: `/Users/cuiyong/.codex/skills/youtube-investor-distiller/references/baozong-institutional-trend-framework.md`

## Baozong Institutional Trend Framework

This reference preserves the distilled framework from the YouTube video:

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5fW3Ncu9OKo`
- Title identified from NotebookLM transcript: `只赚指数不赚钱？“机构行情下”如何做才能赚钱`
- Main speaker: `保总` (Ningbo-based senior trader, per transcript)
- Host/participant: `大胡子 / 胡子哥`
- Core theme: In an institution-led A-share trend market, traditional retail dragon-head and hot-money tactics underperform. The practical response is to follow institutional winners, use ETFs or liquid growth leaders, and buy emotional pullbacks rather than chasing rallies.

Use this as a separate framework from the 摇头哥打板体系. This is a trend-following/institutional-liquidity model, not a pure limit-up or next-day premium model.

### One-Sentence Thesis

保总的核心判断是：当前市场的定价权从游资短线转向机构/ETF趋势资金，散户要停止和赢家作对，转向高流动性成长赛道、机构重仓方向和情绪回调买入的`呼吸法`。

### Market Ecology

#### Institutional Trend Market

- Market style is closer to an institution-led trend bull market, similar in spirit to 2020-style institutional grouping.
- Hot-money连板 and classic retail dragon-head tactics become difficult when institutional trend funds dominate liquidity.
- ETF inflows and large funds can concentrate liquidity in a limited set of institutional sectors.
- Small, illiquid, non-mainstream stocks can become traps because liquidity is absent even when the broad index rises.

#### Information Chain

The transcript describes a practical information hierarchy:

1. Industry insiders / industrial capital.
2. Sell-side research analysts.
3. Buy-side institutions.
4. Retail investors reading reports late.

By the time retail investors see widely circulated reports, the stock may already have moved materially. The copyable lesson is not "find insider info"; it is to understand why chasing late reports is dangerous.

### Core Rules

#### Follow The Winners

- Identify who is currently making money. In this market, the winners are institutional funds and ETF-driven sectors.
- Do not insist on hot-money methods when the market is not rewarding them.
- Avoid thin-liquidity small caps during institution-led phases.
- Prefer high-capacity, liquid names or ETFs in active institutional sectors.

#### Growth-Value Rotation

- Money can rotate from classical value or high-dividend styles into growth-value sectors when growth narratives become dominant.
- AI, robotics, and similar high-growth sectors were highlighted in the transcript as examples.
- The rule is not "always buy AI/robotics"; the rule is to follow where institutional liquidity and industry logic are converging.

#### Angle Principle

- Only consider stocks with an upward price angle, roughly `30-45 degrees`.
- Avoid flat-bottom, sideways, or downward structures.
- The angle rule is a simple visual filter for institutional trend strength and path quality.

#### Breathing Method

`吸`: Buy when the logic remains intact but the market or stock suffers an emotional pullback.

`呼`: Reduce or sell when sentiment becomes euphoric and price runs far from the trend.

Key distinction:

- `情绪杀`: The sector/stock falls because short-term emotion cools. This can be a buy zone if trend and logic remain intact.
- `逻辑杀`: The core thesis breaks. Do not average down.
- `估值杀`: Valuation framework compresses. Treat as dangerous, not a routine pullback.

### Stock / ETF Selection

Positive filters:

- Institution-led sector.
- Strong industry logic or sell-side coverage.
- High liquidity and capacity.
- Mid-cap or above; "second-line blue chips" can offer more elasticity than mega-cap leaders.
- Upward trend with clear angle.
- Trend not broken during pullbacks.

ETF alternative:

- If individual-stock selection is weak, use the relevant sector ETF to capture the sector's median return.
- ETF use reduces single-name error and information disadvantage.

Negative filters:

- Illiquid small caps.
- Old hot-money themes with weakening连板 ecology.
- Stocks with downward angle masquerading as cheap pullbacks.
- Purely report-driven late chases after a large move.
- Full-position bets on one growth name.

### Positioning

- Use diversified sector/stock allocation, described as `打地鼠`: hold several qualified directions and let the strongest one rise.
- Avoid all-in single-name bets.
- When selection confidence is low, prefer ETF or smaller position.
- In institution-led phases, patience matters because trends move more slowly than hot-money boards.

### Buy / Sell Rules

Buy:

- Market state confirms institutional trend dominance.
- Sector has ongoing institutional liquidity.
- Stock or ETF is in a valid uptrend.
- Pullback appears emotional, not thesis-breaking.
- Entry is near trend support or a controlled回档, not a late acceleration.

Sell / reduce:

- Price accelerates far above trend and sentiment becomes overheated.
- Logic or valuation framework breaks.
- Sector liquidity rotates elsewhere.
- The stock loses its upward angle and fails to repair.

### Beginner Misuse Risks

- Calling any drop a `情绪杀` when the trend has already broken.
- Buying downward or flat stocks because they look cheap.
- Reading a public report late and assuming it is still an early opportunity.
- Confusing "institutional trend" with "never sell".
- Lacking patience for slower institutional names and switching back to random short-term trades.
- Full-position concentration in one institution-style stock.
- Ignoring ETFs when individual-stock selection ability is weak.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- Classify whether the market rewards institutions or hot money.
- Prefer liquid institutional sectors during institution-led phases.
- Use ETFs when selection confidence is low.
- Apply the `30-45 degree` angle filter.
- Use the breathing method: buy emotional pullbacks, reduce emotional accelerations.
- Diversify across several qualified directions.

Hard to copy:

- Access to early industry or sell-side information.
- Judging whether a pullback is emotional vs logic/valuation damage.
- Knowing which growth narrative will persist long enough.
- Sensing institutional liquidity rotation in real time.
- Holding slow institutional trends without being shaken out.

### Trading Manual Entries

1. First classify the market: `institutional trend`, `hot-money short-term`, `mixed`, or `liquidity-poor`.
2. In institutional trend markets, stop forcing classic dragon-head and low-liquidity hot-money plays.
3. Follow the winning capital style: institutional sectors, ETFs, and liquid growth leaders.
4. Use the angle filter: no upward angle, no trade.
5. Treat emotional pullbacks as potential buy zones only when logic and trend remain intact.
6. Treat logic breaks and valuation compression as stop/avoid signals.
7. If individual stock selection is uncertain, use sector ETF.
8. Diversify across several qualified directions rather than all-in one name.
9. Reduce into emotional acceleration, especially when price is far from trend.
10. Do not chase a thesis after widely circulated public reports and large prior gains.

### Review Questions

1. Is the current market rewarding institutions, ETFs, hot money, or nobody?
2. Am I trading with the current winner, or trying to revive a losing style?
3. Is this stock/ETF in a sector with real institutional liquidity?
4. Does the chart have a valid upward `30-45 degree` angle?
5. Is this pullback emotional, or has the logic/valuation framework broken?
6. Am I buying near a controlled pullback, or chasing late acceleration?
7. If I cannot select the best stock, should I use the sector ETF instead?
8. Is my position diversified enough to survive rotation?
9. Did I sell because trend broke, or because I could not tolerate small volatility?
10. Has the information I am acting on already become widely known and priced in?

### Future Distillation Targets

When adding future videos by 保总 or similar institutional-trend traders, look for:

- More precise criteria for identifying institutional trend phases.
- Practical ETF selection rules.
- How to distinguish 情绪杀, 逻辑杀, and 估值杀.
- Exact position sizing for 打地鼠-style diversification.
- Sector rotation signals between AI, robotics, high dividend, and other themes.
- Examples where the breathing method fails.

## 5. Beijing Chaojia Seed Framework

- Source file: `/Users/cuiyong/.codex/skills/youtube-investor-distiller/references/beijing-chaojia-framework.md`

## Beijing Chaojia Seed Framework

This reference preserves the first distilled framework from the video `首次深度对话北京炒家，小白也能听懂的游资逻辑` by `A股作手`.

Use it as a seed for future technical distillation. Update it only when later videos reinforce, refine, or contradict the ideas below.

### One-Sentence Thesis

北京炒家的核心不是追求神级买点，而是用边界、过滤、仓位和机械化执行，把短线交易变成可重复的风险控制系统。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `不买`比`怎么买`更重要。
   - First remove low-quality opportunities: low-priced stocks, ST/problem companies, suspicious dealer-controlled moves, and charts with extreme destructive volatility.
   - This protects beginners from the most common tail risks before they think about entries.

2. 涨停板 is used as a certainty filter, not just an aggressive signal.
   - A limit-up stock shows market consensus under rule constraints.
   - In a weak index, a stock that can hold limit-up may reveal unusually strong support.

3. Focus on high-recognition core names.
   - Prefer companies with industry relevance, visibility, enough market cap, active stock character, and some fundamental support.
   - The hidden reason is "escape value": if the trade is wrong, a core name often offers better repair or exit opportunities.

4. Seek average system output, not heroic execution.
   - Selling early, missing a move, or failing to catch the best stock is acceptable.
   - Long-term performance comes from repeated rule-following, not one perfect trade.

5. Position sizing is psychological engineering.
   - After consecutive losses, reduce size instead of revenge trading.
   - A smaller position helps rebuild behavioral quality and confidence.

6. Selling should be mechanical.
   - Use time and price markers, such as the first 5-15 minutes after open or 1%, 3%, 5% reference points.
   - Fast selling can cause missed upside, but the same behavior reduces damage in bad markets.

7. Missing out must be managed.
   - Fear of missing out can be more dangerous than fear of loss.
   - A small tail-position in a highly recognizable core name can satisfy participation needs without breaking the system.

8. High frequency is training only if paired with review.
   - Frequent short-term trading can compress learning cycles and build market familiarity.
   - Without review and boundaries, frequency simply accelerates capital loss.

9. Survival depends on boundaries.
   - The durable edge is knowing which money not to make and when to stop.
   - Intelligence and market cleverness are secondary to behavioral limits.

### Trading System Skeleton

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- High market recognition.
- Industry or theme core status.
- Enough liquidity and market cap.
- Active historical stock character.
- Better limit-up premium and lower failed-board rate.
- Some fundamental or narrative support.

Negative filters:

- ST or companies with unresolved historical problems.
- Very low-priced stocks, especially around 1-2 RMB.
- Suspicious unexplained short-term surges.
- Dealer-like behavior.
- K-lines or intraday patterns that often produce limit-up-to-limit-down style damage.
- Financial-report periods when hidden risk is unusually high.

#### Entry

- Main mode: first-board participation at or near the moment of limit-up, seeking consensus and certainty.
- If the front-runner is missed: consider small late-day participation in a high-recognition, large-cap core name within the same theme.
- Heavy size only when the stock is the clear theme core, the limit-up is early/strong, and expected downside is controlled.

#### Exit

- Favor mechanical exits over prediction.
- Take profits on winners rather than letting every position become a psychological debate.
- Use time/price anchors.
- For possible weak-to-strong setups, avoid watching the first few minutes if that improves rule-following.

#### Position Management

- Normal mode: dispersed exposure, roughly one position around one-tenth of capital.
- Bad state: reduce position size after consecutive losses.
- Poor market: trade only the strongest one or two opportunities, or stay flat.
- Do not increase size merely to recover losses.

#### Cycle Adaptation

- Watch market temperature through premium rate, failed-board rate, and whether core stocks repair after mistakes.
- Quant-driven markets can create false weak-to-strong signals, especially near the close or auction.
- Observe post-open market consensus rather than judging solely from one apparent signal.

### Beginner Playbook

Rules a beginner can copy:

- Maintain a written `do-not-buy` list.
- Use fixed small capital for practice; do not add money just because of early wins or losses.
- Cut position size after three consecutive losing trades.
- Limit the number of simultaneous positions until execution is stable.
- Predefine sell rules before buying.
- Record every mode-break trade.
- Review whether the stock was truly a core name or just a rising name.

Misuse risks:

- Copying `打板` without copying the negative filters.
- Treating high frequency as permission to trade randomly.
- Over-diversifying with a small account and losing execution quality.
- Calling every strong move a "core" after the fact.
- Mistaking a NotebookLM summary or interview anecdote for a tested personal system.

### Copyable vs Non-Copyable

Copyable:

- Negative list discipline.
- Position-size reduction after losses.
- Mechanical sell markers.
- Core/recognition filtering.
- Daily review questions.
- Separating market temperature from personal emotion.

Hard to copy:

- Instant judgment of market consensus.
- Deep familiarity with thousands of A-share names.
- Emotional durability after extreme drawdowns.
- Detection of quant-induced false signals.
- Tolerance for selling early and missing upside over many cycles.

### Review Questions

1. Is this stock on my do-not-buy list?
2. Did I buy recognition/core status, or did I buy only because it rose?
3. If it opens sharply lower tomorrow, what is my exit plan?
4. Was my sell based on a rule or on intraday fear?
5. What is current market temperature: premium rate, failed-board rate, repair strength?
6. Does my current size match my recent win/loss state?
7. Did fear of missing out push me into a mode-break trade?
8. Is this stock truly a theme core or only a follower?
9. Did I revenge trade today?
10. If I were flat now, would I still choose this as one of the strongest one or two opportunities?

### Future Distillation Tasks

When adding future videos, look for:

- New negative filters.
- Concrete sell rules.
- How he defines `core`.
- Differences between bull, bear, and mixed markets.
- How he handles failed boards.
- How he reads volume, auction, and intraday repair.
- Rules that appear repeatedly across videos.
- Contradictions between claimed principles and examples.

## Chaogu Yangjia Complete Sentiment Risk-Reward Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `中国股市传奇人物“炒股养家”40分钟完整版心法`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VhQOyshlTzE`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Upload date shown by YouTube metadata: `2024-11-27T22:31:00-08:00`
- Duration shown by YouTube metadata: about `2488` seconds
- Captions: YouTube page exposed `0` caption tracks to the extractor; source text was imported through NotebookLM.
- NotebookLM title: `The Zen of Market Sentiment: A Trading Masterclass`
- NotebookLM URL: `https://notebooklm.google.com/notebook/cfd1896a-13e7-4959-a283-193452a55637`
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this note is based on NotebookLM's extraction from the YouTube source and public page metadata, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

炒股养家的可复制核心不是某个固定形态，而是：跳出自己的盈亏情绪，用大局观观察全市场持币者与持筹者的心理博弈，在赚钱效应和亏钱效应的轮回里，只在风险收益比极优的瞬间下重手，其余时间用空仓、轻仓和快速纠错保护复利。

### What This Video Adds

Compared with `chaogu-yangjia-heart-method-framework.md`, this video adds several higher-value layers:

1. More explicit risk/reward anchors.
   - Full-position opportunities require extremely high subjective certainty, not ordinary confidence.
   - The transcript repeatedly points to `90%` win-rate confidence, `30%-50%` upside, and `3%-5%` downside as heavy-position anchors.
   - These numbers are not accompanied by a mechanical calculation formula, so they should be treated as decision thresholds to audit, not backtest-ready signals.

2. A stronger biography and pressure layer.
   - The method is framed as the result of a long transition from value thinking and technical-pattern obsession toward market-emotion reading.
   - Early professional trading involved severe drawdown and family pressure.
   - The lesson is not "pressure creates success"; it is "a trader under pressure still needs a system that reduces emotional decisions."

3. A cleaner sell tool: empty-position thinking.
   - If the trader were currently flat and would not buy the stock now, the current holder should not defend it because of cost, face, or the hope of getting even.
   - This converts sell decisions from emotional negotiation into a present-tense buy/no-buy test.

4. A sharper warning against pure technical trading.
   - The video frames early K-line pattern trading as low edge, with a `45%` win-rate reference for pure technical board chasing.
   - Price and volume matter only after they are interpreted as crowd psychology and money behavior.

5. A clearer relation between faith and execution.
   - Asking and 职业炒手 appear as belief anchors: seeing that the method worked for prior masters helped sustain the trader through drawdowns.
   - Faith does not mean stubborn holding. It means believing in a tested process enough to execute exits, rests, and rare heavy attacks.

### Core Mental Model

#### Market Emotion Is The Center

The market is a psychological game between:

- Holders, who decide whether to sell.
- Cash holders, who decide whether to buy.
- Existing winners, whose profits create confidence and imitation.
- Existing losers, whose losses create fear, forced selling, and risk aversion.

The trader's job is to judge which side has the stronger tendency at the current moment:

- Buy tendency stronger than sell tendency: attack can be considered.
- Sell tendency stronger than buy tendency: defense, reduction, or cash is favored.
- The balance is unclear: position should be modest because edge is not obvious.

#### Money-Making Effect And Loss-Making Effect

`赚钱效应`:

- People see visible profits and become willing to chase.
- The strongest theme or leader attracts incremental demand.
- Stocks with simple public logic receive emotional premium.

`亏钱效应`:

- Failed breakouts, sharp drawdowns, and trapped positions reduce risk appetite.
- High-position stocks and weak followers lose liquidity first.
- Selling becomes contagious and can spread across sectors.

Reusable rule:

- Increase exposure only when money-making effect is expanding and the target is inside the recognized line.
- Reduce or stop trading when loss-making effect is expanding and the market cannot support follow-through.

#### Big-Picture First, Technique Second

The video consistently downgrades isolated technical patterns.

Before judging a K-line, judge:

1. Market stage.
2. Main theme or sector.
3. Policy, information, or event background.
4. Capital preference and liquidity.
5. Whether the stock is a publicly recognized carrier.
6. Whether the stock's movement can trigger immediate understanding by outside money.

Only after those questions are clear should the trader inspect entry details.

### Parameter Audit

These are the numbers extracted from NotebookLM and page metadata. They should not be used blindly.

| Parameter | What It Means | Rule Status | Caution |
|---|---|---|---|
| `90%` win-rate confidence | Threshold mentioned for full-position attack | Usable as a strict gate | No calculation method is provided; must not be invented |
| `30%-50%` expected upside | Desired upside room for a rare heavy opportunity | Usable as an audit question | Source does not define how to estimate it |
| `3%-5%` expected downside | Acceptable downside room for that same opportunity | Usable as a risk boundary | Source does not define whether this is intraday, closing, or structural risk |
| `3到5个涨停` | Heavy leader opportunity should still have meaningful limit-up room | Usable as a concept filter | Requires trader judgment about theme size and crowd recognition |
| `一年十几次重仓` | Rough frequency of rare heavy opportunities | Descriptive, not a quota | Do not force ten-plus heavy trades every year |
| `4-5次重仓必杀` | A few high-quality heavy trades plus small opportunities can build a year | Conceptual | No exact selection algorithm is given |
| `45%` win rate | Critique of pure K-line / board-chasing technical trading | Anti-rule | Do not treat pure chart familiarity as edge |
| `90多万` to `40万不到` | Early professional drawdown | Biography | Useful for risk humility, not trade sizing |
| `10万` restart capital | Public page metadata presents a "back-to-the-wall" restart story | Biography | Not a recommended minimum capital model |
| `2010年5月200万`, `9月300万`, `11月400万/500万`, `2011年初600万` | Growth milestones from extracted source/page metadata | Biography | NotebookLM and page metadata are not perfectly consistent on November 2010; do not turn this into a rule |

### Executable Rules

#### 1. Full-Position Gate

A full-position or near-full-position trade needs all of the following:

1. Market ecology supports attack.
2. Target is in the active mainstream theme.
3. The stock has broad public recognition, not private appeal.
4. Buy tendency is clearly stronger than sell tendency.
5. Expected upside is large enough, roughly `30%-50%` or `3-5` limit-ups in the source's language.
6. Expected downside is small enough, roughly `3%-5%`.
7. The trader can explain why the opportunity has around `90%` confidence.

If the last point cannot be explained, the trade is not a full-position trade.

#### 2. Empty-Position Test

For every holding, ask:

- If I were flat right now, would I buy this stock at this price?

If the answer is no:

- Sell, reduce, or stop defending the position.
- Do not use cost price, loss amount, or desire to recover as reasons to hold.

This is the most directly reusable execution rule in the video.

#### 3. Mainstream Theme Filter

A trade candidate must pass the public-recognition test:

1. Can the market immediately understand what theme this stock represents?
2. Is the theme already attracting money or about to be widely recognized?
3. Does the stock have a clear reason to be the carrier of that theme?
4. Is it a market-selected name, or only the trader's private favorite?

If the logic requires a long explanation, it is probably not a `大众情人`.

#### 4. Money-Making Effect Entry

Attack conditions:

- Profitable examples are visible in the market.
- The main line is creating positive feedback.
- The target has either leader status or low-position mainstream potential.
- Volume and price behavior show participation, not isolated self-excitement.

Do not attack only because the chart looks familiar.

#### 5. Loss-Making Effect Defense

Defense conditions:

- Failed trades are increasing.
- High-position names stop receiving premium.
- Sector resonance breaks.
- Strong stocks fall without repair.
- The market enters confirmed decline or has no credible hot spot.

Manual rule:

- In weak markets, be slower. The source's spirit is "宁愿慢三分" rather than buying the first decline.

#### 6. Weak-Market Rebound Entry

When the market is weak, the video supports waiting for sharper downside and clearer probing behavior rather than rushing:

1. Continuous decline first.
2. A large negative candle or clear emotional release.
3. Next-day probing or repair attempt.
4. Prefer a group-level oversold condition, not a lonely cheap stock.

This is not permission to catch every falling knife. It requires evidence that selling pressure has been released and buyers are testing.

#### 7. Strong-Market Selection

In a strong market:

- Chase only the strongest leader when leader premium is clear.
- Or find low-position names in the same mainstream theme before they are fully recognized.
- Avoid weak followers that need the leader to keep rising just to survive.

The key distinction:

- Leader or mainstream low-position carrier: possible opportunity.
- Random follower with no public memory: avoid.

#### 8. Sell When No Longer Bullish

The sell rule is simpler than the buy rule:

- If the original reason disappears, sell.
- If the market gives adverse information, sell or reduce.
- If the sector no longer supports the stock, sell or reduce.
- If the trader would not buy it now while flat, sell or reduce.

Do not convert a failed short-term trade into a passive medium-term hold.

#### 9. Do Not Initiate The Market

The video's posture is follower-first:

- Do not try to educate the market.
- Do not defend a logic that the market refuses to recognize.
- Do not confuse early imagination with market choice.

Good short-term trading waits until the market is willing to cooperate.

#### 10. Stable Compounding Over Hero Trades

The heavy-trade idea is not daily aggression.

Correct interpretation:

- Most days are observation, small testing, or no trade.
- A few rare moments justify heavy attack.
- The account grows through avoiding bad trades as much as capturing good ones.

Incorrect interpretation:

- "Master used heavy position, so I should all-in often."

### Anti-Rules

Do not extract these wrong lessons from the video:

1. Do not use `90%` confidence as a feeling word. If it cannot be explained, it is not 90%.
2. Do not invent a formula for `30%-50%` upside or `3%-5%` downside; the source does not give one.
3. Do not treat biography as a trading rule.
4. Do not average down because a value thesis made you emotionally attached.
5. Do not turn being trapped into "medium-term holding."
6. Do not over-focus on intraday highs and lows while ignoring market ecology.
7. Do not trade a private favorite and call it a `大众情人`.
8. Do not use pure K-line pattern familiarity as proof of edge.
9. Do not try to be the market's starter; follow market selection.
10. Do not be contrarian just for the sake of being early.
11. Do not worship news alone; news needs market recognition and money response.
12. Do not search for a hidden "dealer" as the main explanation; the video emphasizes crowd recognition and retail-style mass participation in true bull names.

### Missing Details

The source does not fully specify:

1. How to calculate buy tendency versus sell tendency.
2. How to quantify money-making effect and loss-making effect.
3. How to mechanically calculate a `90%` win-rate opportunity.
4. How to estimate `30%-50%` upside before the move.
5. How to define the exact `3%-5%` risk boundary.
6. Which specific intraday signals count as "bad market signals."
7. What exact position percentage counts as small, normal, heavy, or full.
8. How to distinguish a true mainstream low-position carrier from a random low-position follower.
9. How to measure `大众情人` status in real time.
10. How to handle modern A-share market changes versus the original trading era.

These gaps should be filled only by later source videos, real examples, or explicit backtests. Do not hallucinate them.

### Practical Review Checklist

Before buying:

1. Is the whole market in money-making effect or loss-making effect?
2. Where is the current mainstream theme?
3. Is this stock a public-recognition carrier?
4. Can outside money immediately understand the reason for the move?
5. Is buy tendency stronger than sell tendency?
6. Is this a leader, mainstream low-position carrier, or merely a follower?
7. Does the opportunity have enough upside to justify the planned size?
8. Can downside be limited near the source's `3%-5%` risk concept?
9. If I call this high certainty, what objective evidence supports it?
10. Am I following the market, or trying to start the market?

Before holding:

1. If I were flat now, would I buy this same stock?
2. Did the original reason still exist after today's price action?
3. Is the sector still supporting it?
4. Is leader premium expanding or fading?
5. Am I holding because of logic, or because I want to recover cost?

Before selling:

1. Has the market given adverse information?
2. Has money-making effect weakened?
3. Has the stock lost public-recognition premium?
4. Would a flat trader still buy it?
5. Is this sell decision delayed by ego, fear, or hope?

After the trade:

1. Was the trade inside the mainstream theme?
2. Was position size matched to real opportunity quality?
3. Did I obey the empty-position test?
4. Did I confuse technical shape with market emotion?
5. What did this trade teach about crowd psychology?

## Chaogu Yangjia Heart Method Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `"炒股养家心法"40分钟完整版`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BfnZ2gNimZo`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Core figure: `炒股养家` (top A-share hot-money trader, per NotebookLM transcript)
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

炒股养家的核心心法是：短线交易不是预测个股，而是理解市场情绪、赚钱/亏钱效应和群众心理，在大局共振时跟随主流资金，在风险扩散时迅速退场，用客观应变实现稳定复利。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. 情绪是最持久的市场规律。
   - 市场情绪的转弯过程长期不变，因为人性的贪婪和恐惧不会轻易改变。
   - 研究重点不是指标，而是人心和群体行为。

2. 赚钱效应和亏钱效应驱动资金流。
   - 买入倾向来自赚钱效应扩散。
   - 卖出倾向来自亏钱效应蔓延。
   - 仓位应该跟随效应强弱变化。

3. 看大局，做短线。
   - 同样的技术形态在不同背景下意义完全不同。
   - 个股走势要放到板块、题材、政策、资金和市场情绪中解释。

4. 做跟随者，不做发动者。
   - 小资金和普通交易者不应试图引导市场。
   - 等市场选择方向，再跟随大资金与大众共识。

5. 买入`大众情人`。
   - 优先选择市场公认、逻辑清晰、一眼能看懂概念的股票。
   - 大众情人拥有跟风盘、流动性和情绪溢价。

6. 忘记成本障碍。
   - 交易只有买入和卖出，没有套牢、解套、成本面子。
   - 如果现在空仓不会买，就不该因为持仓成本而继续拿。

7. 风险收益比决定仓位。
   - 仓位不是由兴奋程度决定，而是由胜率和盈亏比决定。
   - 只有极高胜率和极高空间比的机会，才有资格重仓。

8. 稳定复利胜过暴利冲动。
   - 职业选手的核心指标是长期稳定，而不是单次暴赚。
   - 一年真正值得大力出击的机会可能只有少数几次。

9. 板块共振胜过单兵作战。
   - 个股有随机性，板块整体动能提供更强确定性。
   - 个股强弱要由板块共振和市场认可来验证。

10. 信念支撑知行合一。
    - 短线交易会经历亏损和迷茫。
    - 真正的信念不是固执持仓，而是相信自己的情绪-周期-应变体系，并持续修正。

### Trading Philosophy

#### Market Is A Crowd Game

Core view:

- 股市是持币者和持筹者之间的心理博弈。
- 价格变化背后是群体贪婪、恐惧、犹豫、确认和跟随。
- 交易者要观察市场情绪，而不是被自己的情绪带走。

Manual rule:

- 每笔交易前先判断：我是在利用市场情绪，还是被自己的情绪利用？

#### Use Greed And Fear, Do Not Be Used By Them

When market greed expands:

- 赚钱效应扩散。
- 主线龙头和大众情人获得溢价。
- 可以提高进攻意愿，但仍需看风险收益比。

When market fear expands:

- 亏钱效应蔓延。
- 资金从高位、弱势和非主流撤离。
- 防守优先，必要时清仓休息。

#### Overall Perspective

Before judging a stock, judge:

- Current market stage.
- Main sector and theme.
- Policy or news catalyst.
- Fund flow direction.
- Board/premium/repair behavior.
- Whether the stock is a public favorite or only a private preference.

Avoid:

- Isolated chart reading.
- Buying because a single technical pattern looks familiar.
- Ignoring sector context.

#### Follow, Do Not Force

The trader's job:

- Follow the market's selected direction.
- Participate when the market recognizes the logic.
- Leave when the market no longer recognizes it.

The trader's non-job:

- Persuade the market.
- Defend personal cost.
- Predict without confirmation.

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Positive ecology:

- 赚钱效应 expands.
- Main theme has clear leadership.
- Hot stocks produce premium.
- Sector resonance appears.
- Market volume supports the opportunity.

Negative ecology:

- 亏钱效应 spreads.
- Hot stocks fail to produce premium.
- Strong stocks begin to collapse.
- Sector rotation becomes chaotic.
- Index or policy risk implies "rain is coming."

Manual classification:

1. `赚钱效应扩散`: attack.
2. `赚钱效应分歧`: select only the strongest; reduce frequency.
3. `亏钱效应蔓延`: defend or rest.
4. `转势试探`: observe new mainstream leader candidates.

#### Sector And Theme Selection

Positive filters:

- Current mainstream topic.
- Obvious public understanding.
- Sector resonance rather than isolated stock.
- Strong leading stock with follow-through.
- Catalyst recognized by both professional and retail participants.

Negative filters:

- Non-mainstream theme.
- Obscure logic that only a few people understand.
- Stock moves without sector support.
- Old theme in exhaustion.
- Trade requires persuading yourself too hard.

#### Stock Filtering

Positive stock:

- `大众情人`: recognized, liquid, easy to explain.
- Fits current market emotion.
- Has sector or policy support.
- Has visible buying inclination.
- If it rises, others immediately understand why.

Negative stock:

- Private favorite with no public resonance.
- Pure technical shape without background.
- Weak follower after leader is already mature.
- Position kept only because of cost.
- Stock that would not be bought if currently flat.

#### Entry

Allowed forms:

- Anticipatory entry when the theme is about to be recognized.
- Intraday pursuit when recognition appears.
- Limit-up participation when market consensus is confirmed.

Entry requirement:

- The stock must be in a market-recognized line.
- The opportunity must have favorable risk/reward.
- The current market ecology must support attack.

Checklist:

1. What emotion am I buying?
2. What sector or theme supports it?
3. Is it a public favorite?
4. What is the expected buyer after me?
5. What invalidates the idea?

#### Exit

Core rule:

- If no longer bullish, sell. Do not negotiate with cost.

`假设空仓法`:

- Ask: If I were flat right now, would I buy this stock at this price?
- If no, sell or reduce.
- This removes cost-price attachment and ego.

Sell signals:

- Market gives adverse signal.
- Sector resonance disappears.
- Strong stock stops receiving premium.
- Original logic no longer attracts followers.
- Rain is coming: system-level risk appears.

#### Position Management

Core idea:

- Position follows opportunity quality, not desire.

Rules:

- No opportunity: cash is a position.
- Normal opportunity: controlled size.
- Rare high-conviction opportunity: heavy attack may be justified only when win rate and risk/reward are both exceptional.
- Never add size because of damaged ego or desire to win back.

Transcript concept:

- "Good opportunity heavy attack" can happen, but only a few times a year and only under strong risk/reward conditions.

Manual guardrail:

- Beginners should treat the heavy-attack idea as aspirational, not permission to all-in.

#### Market Cycle Adaptation

Downtrend early stage:

- Consider strong-name pullback rebounds when risk/reward is favorable.

Downtrend middle stage:

- Consider oversold rebounds, but respect weak ecology.

Downtrend late / turn stage:

- Watch for new mainstream hot spots and emerging leaders.

Uptrend / hot market:

- Focus on mainstream leaders and public favorites.

Chaotic market:

- Reduce. Cash. Wait.

### Copyable Rules

1. `先看赚钱/亏钱效应，再看个股。`
   - Market ecology comes before stock selection.

2. `非主流不取，非大众情人不看。`
   - Trade what the market can understand and chase.

3. `假设空仓法。`
   - If flat-me would not buy it, holding-me should not defend it.

4. `市场错了，我就改。`
   - Judgment is provisional; adaptation is core.

5. `成本价不是理由。`
   - Cost should not control current decision.

6. `要下雨就回家。`
   - Systemic risk means rest, not heroism.

7. `仓位跟随机会等级。`
   - More conviction only when risk/reward and market ecology support it.

8. `板块共振优先。`
   - Individual strength without sector support is less reliable.

9. `得散户心者得天下。`
   - The best short-term targets are widely understandable and emotionally compelling.

10. `稳定复利优先于刺激。`
    - Survive long enough for compounding to work.

### Beginner Checklist

Before buying:

1. Is the market in赚钱效应 or亏钱效应?
2. Is this stock in the current mainstream theme?
3. Can ordinary market participants understand the story quickly?
4. Is there sector resonance?
5. Who will buy after me?
6. Is the risk/reward attractive enough for my size?
7. If I am wrong, what is the exit?
8. Am I following market choice or trying to be the engine?

While holding:

1. If I were flat, would I buy this now?
2. Is the sector still supporting it?
3. Is the market still rewarding similar stocks?
4. Is my decision distorted by cost?

After selling:

1. Did I sell because logic changed, or because I was emotional?
2. Did I respect system-level risk?
3. Was the trade based on market emotion or personal hope?

### Common Misuses

- Using `大众情人` as an excuse to chase anything popular.
- Treating heavy-attack language as permission to all-in without professional risk control.
- Ignoring the "few times a year" condition for high-conviction bets.
- Saying `市场错了我就改` but actually changing only after large loss.
- Mistaking personal conviction for market consensus.
- Using the empty-position question but answering dishonestly because of sunk cost.
- Applying strong-market chase tactics during weak-market亏钱效应.
- Believing short-term emotion rules mean long-term fundamentals do not matter at all; the framework is about short-term trading ecology.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- 赚钱/亏钱效应 tracking.
- Sector resonance filter.
- 大众情人 filter.
- 假设空仓法.
- Cost-price detachment.
- Market-stage strategy adaptation.
- Cash as defense when system risk appears.

Hard to copy:

- Deep crowd-psychology reading.
- Timing of "market recognizes concept" moment.
- Extreme emotional calm under heavy size.
- Knowing when to be slow by three minutes versus fast.
- Belief strong enough to survive large drawdowns while still remaining flexible.
- Judging rare high-conviction opportunities without self-deception.

### Relationship To Existing Frameworks

#### Liangwange Discipline Framework

Reinforced:

- Discipline and self-knowledge are essential.
- Do not let cost or emotion control trades.
- Stable compounding matters more than excitement.

Added by Yangjia:

- Stronger focus on group psychology and market emotion as the primary law.
- `假设空仓法` as a direct decision tool.
- `大众情人` and public recognition as selection criteria.

Potential tension:

- Liangwange stresses no averaging and equity-curve validation before size.
- Yangjia speaks of rare heavy attacks when odds are exceptional.
- Manual synthesis: use Liangwange as the size-control guardrail; use Yangjia to decide when a rare high-quality opportunity exists.

#### Dragon Leader Strategy

Reinforced:

- Leaders must have public recognition.
- Sector and market consensus matter more than isolated K-lines.
- Selling leaders is harder and more important than buying them.

Added by Yangjia:

- `得散户心者得天下`: public emotional recognition creates premium.
- Empty-position thinking removes cost attachment.

Potential tension:

- Dragon framework gives ladder mechanics.
- Yangjia gives emotional and crowd-psychology justification.
- Use both: ladder confirms structure, Yangjia tests whether it has crowd appeal.

#### Yaotouge Cycle Framework

Reinforced:

- Market cycle comes before stock.
- High-level behavior and money-making/loss effect drive opportunity.
- Different cycle stages require different tactics.

Added by Yangjia:

- Downtrend early/mid/late strategy framing.
- Stronger emphasis on system-level risk: if rain is coming, go home.

### Trading Manual Entries

1. Before every trade, classify market ecology:赚钱效应扩散, 分歧, 亏钱效应蔓延, or转势试探.
2. Only trade public-recognition names: non-mainstream and non-public-favorite names are excluded.
3. A stock's technical pattern is valid only inside the right sector and market background.
4. Use假设空仓法 for every holding: if flat-me would not buy it, holding-me should not defend it.
5. Cost price has no authority over sell decisions.
6. Heavy size is allowed only when market ecology, public recognition, and risk/reward all align; otherwise keep size controlled.
7. When system risk appears, cash is correct. Do not prove courage in the rain.
8. Follow the market's choice; do not try to create the market's choice.
9. Stable compounding is the professional goal.
10. Review whether each decision came from market emotion reading or personal greed/fear.

### Review Questions

1. Where is today's赚钱效应 concentrated?
2. Is亏钱效应 expanding or contracting?
3. Which theme has public recognition and sector resonance?
4. Is my stock a大众情人, or only my private favorite?
5. If I were flat now, would I buy this position?
6. Did cost price affect my decision?
7. Am I following an existing market choice or trying to predict one too early?
8. Does this trade have a favorable risk/reward worthy of its size?
9. If rain is coming, why am I still exposed?
10. Did today's action move me toward stable compounding or toward emotional stimulation?

### Future Distillation Targets

When future videos mention 炒股养家, look for:

- More concrete definitions of赚钱效应 and亏钱效应.
- How to identify大众情人 in real time.
- Exact heavy-position criteria.
- Examples of假设空仓法 in live trades.
- How to distinguish early/mid/late downtrend.
- How he handles selling a true leader.
- How his method changes with market maturity.
