## Dahuzi Liangwange Follow-Long Understand-Short Capital Aesthetic Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `神秘隐身大佬（2万哥）又来给大胡子布道了|大胡子学炒股`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Z-2VTSd5v94`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- Video upload date from YouTube structured metadata: `2025-04-27T23:04:19-07:00`
- Duration from YouTube metadata: `659` seconds
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed title, channel, duration, upload date, and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- YouTube page public caption tracks visible to browser metadata: `0`
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `The Market Logic of Trend Following and Capital Intuition`.
- Speaker identification: NotebookLM describes the mentor as an experienced investor known as `"20,000"` / `2万哥`, speaking to `大胡子`.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频把两万哥的交易观压缩成一个底层审美框架：在 A 股短线里，先承认`市场永远正确`和`钱说了算`，再用`健康曲线 / 做多排列`筛选可参与对象；真正能复制的是`跟随做多，理解做空`、`先分辨强弱和真假资金`、`认知不足就少动并轻仓训练`，而不是某个具体买点公式。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `跟随做多，理解做空`是本视频的八字总纲。
   - 两万哥强调盈利的出发点在做多，但不能只会看涨。
   - `理解做空`不是让散户去做空，而是理解风险、对手盘、趋势失败和弱势资金。

2. 市场永远正确。
   - 当个人判断和市场走势冲突时，错误通常在交易者一边。
   - 这条是纠错能力的前置条件：先尊重市场，才可能修正自己。

3. 资本市场最终是`钱`说了算。
   - 选股和强弱判断要回到资金是否真实、是否有推动力。
   - 不是看故事、情绪口号或个人幻想。

4. `健康曲线`和`做多排列`是审美过滤器。
   - 交易对象必须看起来处在做多结构里，走势要健康。
   - 但视频没有给出均线、成交量、K线组合等精确定义。

5. 追涨杀跌本身不是错。
   - 打板也是一种追涨。
   - 追涨杀跌变成贬义，是因为多数人分不清强弱，只是在情绪化追逐。

6. 分辨强弱是追涨杀跌的入场门槛。
   - 能分辨强弱，追强杀弱才是交易技术。
   - 分不清强弱，追涨杀跌就是亏损来源。

7. 跟随资金之前，必须分辨`真资金 / 假资金`。
   - NotebookLM 提取到视频明确强调要辨别真假资金。
   - 真假资金的具体判别方法被标注为未展开 / 不能讲清，不能强行补参数。

8. 认知不到位时，`少动`比多做更高级。
   - 视频将频繁操作、试图用高超做 T 扩大利润，视为容易落入主力陷阱。
   - 没有交易原则时，动作越多，暴露的错误越多。

9. 纠错能力是操手核心能力。
   - 亏损后要识别错在哪里，而不是死扛或包装成长期信仰。
   - 视频强调及时修正，但未给出具体止损百分比。

10. 轻仓是认知训练期的保护机制。
    - NotebookLM 提取到大胡子仓位从 `80%` 降到 `50%`，再降到 `30%` 的语境。
    - 这里的可复制点不是人人固定 30%，而是未悟透前必须降低仓位。

11. 复盘和向高手请教，是加速认知的训练方式。
    - 视频强调分析亏损原因、接受更高认知者点拨、反复听和复盘。
    - 但不能把高手建议变成盲从，仍要回到自己的纠错和验证。

12. 逻辑要做减法。
    - 两万哥这次不是扩展复杂战法，而是把复杂交易收敛到少数底层原则。
    - `讲多了反而乱`，对初学者尤其重要。

### Confirmed Parameters And Limits

| Parameter / Signal | Transcript Context | Executable Meaning | Do Not Overextend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `8个字` | 核心口诀：跟随做多，理解做空 | 把所有操作先归入是否顺应做多资金、是否理解风险端 | 不等于任何时候都买入 |
| `钱 / 资金` | 资本市场最终由钱决定 | 强弱判断优先看资金是否真实且有推动力 | 不等于看到放量就认定为真资金 |
| `健康曲线 / 做多排列` | 审美和选股过滤 | 只优先研究走势健康、处在做多结构的标的 | 视频未给出均线或图形公式 |
| `追涨杀跌` | 被重新定义为高级能力 | 只有能分辨强弱时，追强杀弱才成立 | 分不清强弱时严禁模仿 |
| `真资金 / 假资金` | 跟随资金的前提 | 跟随前要判断资金性质 | 具体方法未展开，不能自行补算法 |
| `80% -> 50% -> 30%` | 大胡子训练仓位变化 | 认知不足时应降仓，30%可作为训练期参考 | 不是所有人的固定仓位模型 |
| `纠错能力` | 操手核心能力 | 市场证伪后及时认错和修正 | 未给出亏损百分比或卖出公式 |

Explicitly not provided by the transcript:

- No exact definition of `健康曲线`.
- No exact definition of `做多排列`.
- No quantified rule for identifying `真资金 / 假资金`.
- No clear volume,均线,涨幅,盘口 threshold for strength.
- No fixed buy point, sell point, stop-loss percentage, or take-profit percentage.
- No objective rule for when a trader has reached `认知到位`.
- No exact rule for when `30%` training position can be increased.

### Trading System Skeleton

#### Step 1: Start From The Market Premise

Before any trade, ask:

1. Am I respecting the current market direction, or arguing with it?
2. Is the thesis based on real money choosing the stock, or on my own story?
3. Am I trying to profit from a do-long structure, or forcing action in a weak structure?

If the answers are unclear, reduce action.

#### Step 2: Filter By Capital And Aesthetic

Positive filters:

- The stock shows a recognizable `做多排列`.
- The curve looks healthy rather than chaotic.
- Money appears to be concentrated and capable of driving strength.
- The target is stronger than alternatives, not merely rising.
- The trader can explain why it is strong without relying on hearsay.

Negative filters:

- The chart is messy and lacks a do-long structure.
- The move is only a single spike without convincing capital follow-through.
- The trader cannot distinguish whether the money is real or fake.
- The buy reason is "it has already risen, so it must continue."
- The operation is motivated by anxiety, recovery pressure, or boredom.

#### Step 3: Decide Whether Action Is Allowed

Action is allowed only when:

1. The trader can explain why money is choosing the target.
2. The target fits the healthy-curve / do-long-arrangement aesthetic.
3. Strength and weakness can be distinguished.
4. The trader has a predefined correction plan if wrong.
5. Position size will not distort behavior.

If any of these are missing, the default action is `少动 / 轻仓 / 复盘`.

#### Step 4: Execute With Correction In Mind

Allowed actions:

- Follow clear do-long structures.
- Prefer stronger names over weaker names.
- Cut weak targets when the market proves them weak.
- Keep size low during the learning phase.
- Review losing trades and ask for higher-level feedback.

Forbidden actions:

- Frequent 做 T when no stable principle exists.
- Treating "I can optimize a few ticks" as a reason to overtrade.
- Chasing without strength discrimination.
- Dead-holding after the market disproves the logic.
- Increasing position because of confidence before the curve proves stability.

### Executable Rule Set

1. If the target does not show a healthy do-long structure, do not treat it as a priority candidate.
2. If you cannot distinguish strong from weak, do not use "追涨杀跌" as an execution style.
3. If a rise cannot be linked to real capital, do not assume it is tradable strength.
4. If you are in a learning / unclear phase, reduce frequency and position before seeking profit.
5. If you want to do T only to squeeze extra profit, ask whether you are actually walking into a main-force trap.
6. If the market contradicts your view, respect the market first and correct yourself second.
7. If you lose money and cannot explain why, stop expanding risk and review the cause.
8. If a senior trader's advice sounds useful, record it, replay it, and test it against your own trades instead of blindly copying.
9. If a rule cannot be stated simply, continue reducing it until it becomes executable.
10. If your account behavior worsens above a certain position size, that size has not been earned.

### Anti-Rules / Misuse Warnings

1. Do not turn `跟随做多` into "any green candle is buyable."
2. Do not turn `理解做空` into bearish guessing or fear-based selling.
3. Do not turn `健康曲线` into a made-up chart pattern without evidence from this source.
4. Do not treat normal volume expansion as proof of `真资金`.
5. Do not call every high open or fast rise `强`.
6. Do not use `追涨杀跌本身没错` to justify impulsive chasing.
7. Do not mistake frequent doing T for active risk control.
8. Do not increase from 30% training size until process quality and correction ability improve.
9. Do not treat "market is wrong" as a trading premise.
10. Do not collect more tactics if the current problem is lack of discipline and correction.

### Relationship To Nearby Frameworks

#### Compared With `liangwange-discipline-curve-framework.md`

This video is narrower and more foundational.

- The existing Liangwange framework covers discipline, equity curve, no averaging down, shape + volume, and system development.
- This video provides a more compact root phrase: `跟随做多，理解做空`.
- It also emphasizes `健康曲线 / 做多排列` as an aesthetic filter and `钱说了算` as the reduction point.

#### Compared With `dahuzi-objective-signal-real-fake-capital-framework.md`

This video names the importance of real/fake capital but does not disclose the method.

- The objective-signal framework gives a richer execution stack: auction, board ladder, watchlists, 9:35 / 14:50, L2, and objective triggers.
- This video is the principle layer: do not follow money until you can tell true from fake.
- Use the objective-signal framework when the question asks for operational detection; use this video when the question asks for the bottom philosophy.

#### Compared With `dahuzi-mystery-mentor-long-short-trap-wangwang-team-framework.md`

This video is a level shift, not a continuation of the same tactical detail.

- The long-short trap video gives tactical context: trapped chips, short control, benchmark flip, Wangwang-team window, same-class one-word boards, and pullback zones.
- This video compresses the mindset above those tactics: money decides, do-long structure matters, and the trader must first develop strength discrimination and correction ability.
- Use the long-short trap file for market-stage and reversal-window decisions; use this file for whether the trader is even qualified to act.

### Review Questions

Before entering a trade:

1. Is this a do-long arrangement, or just a price move?
2. What makes the money here real rather than noise?
3. Why is this target stronger than nearby alternatives?
4. If the market proves me wrong, what correction action is already defined?
5. Is my position size low enough that I can obey the correction?

After a loss:

1. Was the mistake in market premise, stock aesthetic, capital identification, timing, or discipline?
2. Did I trade before I could distinguish strength from weakness?
3. Did I turn doing T into overtrading?
4. Did I resist the market instead of correcting?
5. Which rule must be simplified before the next trade?

## 14. Dahuzi Market Breadth Mechanical Framework

## Dahuzi Market Breadth Mechanical Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `很多人说我频道发的视频心法都是虚的，今天的视频有交易模型|大胡子学炒股`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VHiwLASkQeQ`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Main speaker / core figure identified by NotebookLM transcript: `大胡子`
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

大胡子这套模型把短线交易改造成一套机械化流程：先用全市场涨跌家数决定能不能买、该不该砍仓，再用固定时间窗口、量价形态、10:40 条件单和三根 K 线卖出规则，压制盘中幻想、追高和亏损扩大。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `买入看大盘，卖出看个股`.
   - 买入前先看全市场生态，不让个股形态覆盖系统风险。
   - 卖出则根据持仓个股的开盘、分时、量比、K 线结构执行。

2. `3000家上涨` is the new-open gate.
   - 全市场上涨家数达到 3000 家以上，才允许考虑买入。
   - 如果没有达到，不管标的多好，都停止新开仓。

3. `3000家下跌 / 4000家下跌` are forced risk switches.
   - 下跌家数达到 3000 家，开盘无条件砍掉总仓位的一半。
   - 下跌家数达到 4000 家，无条件全仓清仓，即使个股看起来还强。

4. Time windows are discipline devices.
   - 买入只允许在 `10:40以前` 或 `14:40以后`。
   - `10:40-14:40` 之间禁止买入，只做卖出和风险处理。

5. The model protects against profit turning into loss.
   - 10:40 对盈利持仓设置条件单或分批止盈。
   - 具体回撤百分比未给出，核心是盈利票不能被幻想拖成亏损票。

6. Invalidation is tied to the buy reason.
   - 视频没有给固定百分比止损。
   - 规则是：买入理由消失时立刻止损，不能用希望替代原始逻辑。

7. The system buys structured pullbacks, not emotional surges.
   - 放量拉升不是追涨理由。
   - 更优买点是缩量回踩分时均线、开盘价上方缩量支撑，或放量拉升后的回踩确认。

8. Stock selection starts from activity.
   - 一个月内没有涨停或倍量交易的股票，不进入重点关注池。
   - 先确认曾经有资金参与，再谈具体形态。

9. The strategy includes multiple pattern modules.
   - 主要模型包括放量长上影线、一进二、横盘突破、涨停破前高。
   - 后两者在 transcript 中只提到重点关注，未给出完整硬阈值。

10. 10:40 is also a sell-decision checkpoint.
    - 对盈利票设置保护。
    - 用三根 K 线判断高点是否抬高，若没有抬高则卖出。

11. Missing a trade can be a successful trade.
    - 如果因为规则不满足而踏空牛股，仍然视为成功执行纪律。
    - 这把交易评价从单笔结果转向规则一致性。

12. Discipline is reinforced by physical constraints.
    - 复盘前先读规则。
    - 连亏三天清仓思考，违反纪律则转出部分本金到银行卡，降低继续乱做的能力。

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Core breadth rules from the transcript:

1. IF `上涨家数 >= 3000`, THEN allow the system to search for valid buy setups.
2. IF `上涨家数 < 3000`, THEN do not open new positions.
3. IF `下跌家数 >= 3000`, THEN cut total exposure by half at the open.
4. IF `下跌家数 >= 4000`, THEN clear all positions, regardless of individual-stock stories.

Manual interpretation:

- The breadth gate is the first layer of the system.
- It should override attractive chart patterns.
- It is especially useful for beginners because it turns vague market feeling into a visible switch.

#### Stock Filtering

Base filter:

- The stock should have had a limit-up move or doubled-volume trading within the past month.
- If it has no recent activity proof, skip it.

Positive features:

- Recent visible capital participation.
- Stronger pattern recognition than average stocks in the candidate pool.
- Price and volume structure that can define an invalidation point.
- Liquidity sufficient for intraday exit.

Negative filters:

- No recent limit-up or doubled-volume activity.
- Chasing a stock only because it is currently rising.
- A one-word-board / one-line-board style where normal participation and exit may be poor.
- A pattern whose buy reason cannot be written down clearly.

#### Pattern Modules

##### 放量长上影线模型

Hard conditions in the transcript:

- Background: long sideways period or upward trend.
- Shape: a long upper shadow; longer is better.
- Volume: if the upper shadow touches a prior high, current volume should exceed the volume at that prior high.
- Bonus: recent limit-up followed by pullback and visible bottom support.
- Avoid: one-line-board style structures.

Manual reading:

- The long upper shadow is not automatically bearish in this framework.
- It can indicate probing / capital testing if volume and prior trend support the interpretation.
- The next trade still needs a controlled buy point; do not chase the upper shadow day.

##### 一进二模型

Hard conditions in the transcript:

- Market cap within `100亿`.
- Had a limit-up within the past `15天`.
- Price has not broken the bottom price of that previous limit-up board.
- Volume cannot be too large, but the transcript does not provide an exact volume-ratio or turnover threshold.

Manual reading:

- The previous limit-up is treated as an anchor.
- The low of that board becomes a structural defense line.
- If price loses that anchor, the setup premise is damaged.

##### 横盘突破模型

Transcript status:

- Mentioned as a focus area.
- No exact sideways-period length, volatility range, or breakout-volume threshold was given.

Manual use:

- Treat it as a watchlist category only until further transcript evidence provides hard rules.

##### 涨停破前高模型

Transcript status:

- Mentioned as a focus area.
- No exact breakout percentage or volume confirmation threshold was given.

Manual use:

- Do not turn this into "buy any limit-up making a new high".
- Require the broader market gate and a clear exit plan.

#### Entry

Allowed time windows:

1. Before `10:40`.
2. After `14:40`.

Forbidden time window:

- From `10:40` to `14:40`, no buying. Only sell, reduce risk, or wait.

Entry structures:

- Shrinking-volume support near the intraday average line.
- Shrinking-volume support above the opening price.
- Pullback after a volume-backed upward move.
- Strong enough structure to define the exact invalidation point.

Do-not-enter rules:

- Do not buy if market breadth has not passed.
- Do not chase a stock simply because it is pulling up with volume.
- Do not buy in the middle forbidden window.
- Do not buy if the buy reason cannot be written in one sentence.

#### Exit

Open weakness:

1. IF a stock opens down, rallies, and still cannot break the intraday average, THEN sell.
2. IF it gives two failed attempts and still cannot reclaim the reference line, THEN sell.
3. IF it opens with volume-backed downside and a rebound cannot break the opening price, THEN sell.

Volume-price warning:

- IF volume ratio is extremely high, around `15以上`, but the stock is only up `1%-2%`, THEN sell immediately.

Three-candlestick rule:

- At `10:40`, observe today's K-line condition together with the previous two daily K-lines.
- IF today's high has not lifted above the prior highs in that three-candle structure, THEN treat it as lack of upward strength and sell.

Profit protection:

- At `10:40`, set conditional orders for profitable positions.
- For larger positions, use batch orders.
- The transcript does not provide an exact trigger percentage.

Invalidation:

- Exit when the original buy reason disappears.
- Do not replace a failed premise with a new story while still holding the same position.

#### Position Management

Market-breadth sizing:

- Good breadth allows trading; poor breadth blocks new buying.
- `3000家下跌` cuts exposure by half.
- `4000家下跌` clears exposure fully.

Account-state rules:

- Consecutive losses require review.
- Three straight losing days trigger clearing positions and thinking through the problem.
- If discipline is violated, withdraw a portion of capital from the securities account. The NotebookLM extraction specifically surfaced `20%` as the punishment amount.

Manual synthesis:

- This framework uses both market-state rules and behavior-state rules.
- The market tells you how much risk is allowed.
- Your recent behavior tells you whether you deserve to keep normal size.

### If/Then Trading Manual Entries

#### Market Gate

1. IF total rising stocks are below `3000`, THEN do not open any new position.
2. IF total falling stocks are above `3000`, THEN reduce total position by half.
3. IF total falling stocks are above `4000`, THEN clear all positions.
4. IF a stock looks attractive but the breadth gate fails, THEN classify the missed trade as correct discipline.

#### Buy Checklist

1. IF the stock had no limit-up or doubled-volume action in the past month, THEN skip.
2. IF current time is between `10:40` and `14:40`, THEN do not buy.
3. IF the stock is rising with volume but has not pulled back to a controllable support, THEN do not chase.
4. IF the setup is a long-upper-shadow model, THEN verify trend/background, long shadow, and volume above the prior high reference.
5. IF the setup is a one-into-two model, THEN verify market cap below `100亿`, limit-up within `15天`, and no break below the prior board bottom.
6. IF the setup is horizontal breakout or limit-up breaking prior high, THEN require additional review because the transcript did not provide complete thresholds.

#### Sell Checklist

1. IF a position is profitable at `10:40`, THEN set a conditional order or batch profit-protection order.
2. IF open weakness cannot reclaim the intraday average after one or two attempts, THEN sell.
3. IF volume ratio is above `15` while price gain is only `1%-2%`, THEN sell.
4. IF the three-candle structure shows no higher high by `10:40`, THEN sell.
5. IF the buy reason disappears, THEN stop out even if the fixed percent loss looks small.
6. IF a sold stock later turns strong again, THEN treat any re-entry as a new trade, not as regret repair.

#### Discipline Checklist

1. IF reviewing, THEN read the rules before reviewing the stocks.
2. IF two losing days appear, THEN inspect whether the system environment changed.
3. IF three losing days appear, THEN clear positions and stop to think.
4. IF a rule is violated, THEN withdraw part of trading capital and lower the ability to continue impulsive trades.
5. IF a trade was missed because rules were obeyed, THEN mark it as successful execution.

### Relationship To Existing Frameworks

#### With Dahuzi Ten-Day Line Tail-Session Framework

Mutual reinforcement:

- Both systems use fixed time windows to avoid emotional intraday trading.
- Both prefer mechanical exits and do not want the trader to improvise.
- The market-breadth model can serve as an upper-level gate for the ten-day-line model.

Potential difference:

- The ten-day-line method is a specific late-session rebound setup.
- This model is a broader operating system covering market gate, intraday entries, exits, and discipline.

Manual synthesis:

- Use breadth first, setup second.
- If breadth fails, do not take the ten-day-line trade even if the individual chart is attractive.

#### With Beijing Chaojia

Mutual reinforcement:

- `不买` is still more important than `怎么买`.
- Both systems emphasize risk control before entry excitement.
- Mechanical selling and reduced size after bad states align well.

Difference:

- Beijing Chaojia leans more toward limit-up certainty, core recognition, and market consensus.
- Dahuzi's model is more visible-rule and breadth-threshold driven.

#### With Trader Probability Discipline

Mutual reinforcement:

- Trade quality is defined by rule execution, not single-trade profit.
- Missing a nonqualified opportunity is not failure.
- Market breadth and time windows convert probability thinking into concrete buttons: buy, wait, halve, clear.

#### With Chaogu Yangjia / Sentiment Frameworks

Potential conflict:

- Yangjia-style practice reads crowd psychology and emotional extremes.
- Dahuzi's model intentionally removes much of that discretion and replaces it with hard triggers.

Manual synthesis:

- Beginners should privilege Dahuzi's hard triggers.
- Advanced traders can use sentiment interpretation only after the hard risk switches are passed.

#### With Qianniuge Cycle Framework

Mutual reinforcement:

- Both ask the trader to respect capital environment before stock picking.
- Qianniuge focuses on incremental capital, mainstream sector breathing, and volume stages.
- Dahuzi contributes a simpler whole-market breadth switch that can be checked intraday.

### Common Misuses

- Using `3000家上涨` as a bullish prediction instead of a minimum permission gate.
- Buying during the forbidden middle window because the stock "looks strong".
- Treating any long upper shadow as a bullish capital-test signal.
- Ignoring that one-into-two requires a prior limit-up anchor and no break below that board's bottom.
- Inventing exact thresholds for horizontal breakout and limit-up breaking prior high even though the transcript did not provide them.
- Setting no 10:40 profit-protection order because the trader believes a pullback is only washing.
- Calling a new story a continuation of the old buy reason.
- Keeping full size after `3000家下跌` or refusing to clear during `4000家下跌`.
- Treating physical punishment as theatrics while ignoring its actual purpose: reduce future impulse capacity.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- 3000/4000 market-breadth risk switches.
- Buy-time window: before 10:40 or after 14:40.
- No-buy window: 10:40-14:40.
- 10:40 profit-protection routine.
- Stock activity filter: recent limit-up or doubled volume.
- One-into-two hard filters: under 100亿, limit-up within 15 days, no break below board bottom.
- Three-candle sell checkpoint.
- Written rule reading before review.
- Reducing trading capital after serious discipline violation.

Hard to copy:

- Fast visual pattern recognition across many candidates.
- Knowing when "bottom support" is real rather than hindsight.
- Maintaining the emotional force to clear positions during a broad selloff.
- Treating missed winners as successful execution over many cycles.
- Calibrating conditional orders without explicit transcript thresholds.

### Review Questions

1. Did I check rising/falling-stock breadth before considering any buy?
2. Was rising breadth above 3000 when I opened a new position?
3. If falling stocks exceeded 3000, did I cut total exposure by half?
4. If falling stocks exceeded 4000, did I fully clear exposure?
5. Did I buy only before 10:40 or after 14:40?
6. Did I buy a controlled pullback or chase a volume-backed surge?
7. Did this stock have a limit-up or doubled-volume event in the past month?
8. Did I write the exact buy reason before entry?
9. At 10:40, did I protect profitable positions with conditional or batch orders?
10. Did any holding fail the intraday-average, opening-price, volume-ratio, or three-candle sell rule?
11. Did I sell when the buy reason disappeared, or did I invent a new story?
12. If I missed a winner because breadth or time-window rules failed, did I record it as correct execution?
13. Am I currently in a two-day or three-day losing state?
14. If I violated discipline, did I reduce available trading capital instead of only promising to improve?

### Future Distillation Tasks

When future Dahuzi videos appear, look for:

- Exact conditional-order trigger values around 10:40.
- More precise definitions of horizontal breakout and limit-up breaking prior highs.
- How he calibrates "volume cannot be too large" in the one-into-two model.
- Whether `3000家上涨` changes with A-share total listing count over time.
- How he handles sector leadership when breadth is good but themes are scattered.
- Whether the 10:40 / 14:40 windows are universal or model-specific.

## Dahuzi Mindset Discipline Cash Rebuild Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `大胡子学炒股：高手连线讲述情绪，人性，执行交易框架（不是2万哥）|大胡子学炒股`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xAvgamtAE5M`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- Video upload date from YouTube structured metadata: `2025-05-04T21:30:37-07:00`
- Duration from YouTube metadata: about `850-851` seconds
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed title, channel, duration, upload date, and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `Mastering Mindset and Discipline in Trading`.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频不是新增一个买点模型，而是把短线交易失败的根因压到`人性失控`：当市场出现严重亏钱效应时，最高级的动作是空仓；当交易者已被大额亏损破坏心态时，必须先把本金降到仍有痛感但可承受的金额，再用交割单、开盘预期和亏钱效应过滤重新训练执行力。

### Confirmed Parameters And Limits

These parameters were extracted through NotebookLM's transcript analysis. Treat them as context-specific operating anchors, not universal mechanical laws.

| Parameter | Context | Executable Meaning | Do Not Overextend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `15万` | 给亏损严重、资金麻木的交易者的重启本金建议 | 本金要降到既能产生痛感、又不至于让人崩溃的范围。 | 不是所有人都必须用15万；核心是心理可承受且能认真执行。 |
| `2万` | 与两万哥式小资金训练形成对照 | 对亏过上百万的人，过小本金可能无感，反而随便乱做。 | 不否定小资金训练；强调资金大小要匹配心理状态。 |
| `40几家跌停` | 严重亏钱效应 / 核心辨识度个股大面积跌停 | 这是应绝对管住手、偏空仓的红线。 | 不要泛化成任意市场都固定40家；要看是否是核心辨识度个股。 |
| `10几年` | 连线对象交易年限 | 说明交易多年不等于理解市场和能执行纪律。 | 不是经验年限越长越有效。 |
| `集合竞价及开盘阶段` | 次日验证预期、决定砍不砍 | 买入前要写出次日应有表现，开盘不兑现就处理。 | 视频没有给出`9:30-10:00`这样的固定时间窗。 |
| `每周 / 每月` | 交割单复盘频率 | 周度、月度拉交割单，找不该买、该卖没卖、扛单等错误。 | 不代表只有周末才能复盘；重点是形成闭环。 |
| `二、300开头的票` | 创业板高涨幅个股示例 | 作为当时赚钱效应或转折氛围的例子。 | 不是买入300开头股票的规则。 |

Explicitly not provided by the transcript:

- No fixed stop-loss percentage such as `-3%`, `-5%`, or `-10%`.
- No fixed position ratio such as half position, one-third position, or full position.
- No specific stock code or stock recommendation; the video explicitly avoids giving codes.
- No fixed buy price, support level, pressure level, or indicator trigger.
- No fixed take-profit level; the emphasis is on what to do when expectation fails.

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `先修人性, 再谈技术`.
   - Most traders can understand the surface of a method.
   - The difference is whether fear, greed, revenge, and回本欲望 hijack execution.

2. `空仓是主动操作`.
   - In a strong亏钱效应 environment, buying nothing is the correct trade.
   - 空仓 is not laziness; it is risk control when odds are hostile.

3. `亏钱效应是总开关`.
   - When core names are broadly limit-down, do not look for isolated clever opportunities.
   - The market is telling you that the ecology is punishing action.

4. `40几家核心跌停是红线`.
   - The transcript confirms this as a danger signal.
   - The actionable meaning is: stop trying to earn daily income from a collapsing field.

5. `本金大小要让人清醒`.
   - If the account is too large, fear and revenge dominate.
   - If the account is too small for a trader who lost huge money, the trader may become careless.
   - The useful size is the size that restores careful execution.

6. `15万是心理重建例子, 不是普适处方`.
   - It is offered to a specific damaged trader profile.
   - The general rule is: reduce capital until mistakes hurt enough to respect, but not enough to destroy.

7. `不要用回本心态交易`.
   - Trying to recover quickly is exactly when traders force low-quality trades.
   - The urge to "make daily income" is treated as a major failure mode.

8. `不及预期就砍`.
   - Whether打板 or低吸, the next day's opening response must validate the thesis.
   - If the expected path does not appear, the job is to cut, not debate with the market.

9. `交割单比感受可靠`.
   - The transcript pushes weekly/monthly delivery-order review.
   - The goal is to identify扛单, random buying, failing to sell, and subjective stock love.

10. `做T暴露人性矛盾`.
    - 做T is used as a mirror: fear of selling then rising, fear of cutting at the low, and reluctance to admit error.
    - It is not presented as a universal profit technique.

11. `地天板是转折观察项`.
    - It appears as a possible sign that亏钱效应 is exhausting and money is testing a turn.
    - It is not a standalone buy signal without broader money-making effect.

12. `职业态度来自持续盯盘和复盘`.
    - A serious trader cannot enter the market casually.
    - The process must be daily observation plus periodic account review.

### Trading Rule Table

| Rule | Trigger | Allowed Action | Forbidden Action | Failure Signal | Common Misuse |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Severe loss-effect stop | Core recognizable stocks show around `40+` limit-downs | Stay empty, reduce activity, protect cash | Force trades to recover losses | Core names stop collapsing and money-making effect returns | Treating every ordinary weak day as the same red line |
| Cash rebuild rule | Trader has suffered large losses and is emotionally numb | Reduce principal to a psychologically useful size, e.g. the transcript's `15万` example | Continue large-size revenge trading | Stable execution and respect for each trade returns | Thinking `15万` is a universal magic number |
| Anti-too-small rule | Principal is so small that mistakes feel meaningless | Increase only to the minimum size that restores seriousness | Casual gambling because "it is only small money" | Trader again treats every trade as real | Misreading small-money training as permission to be sloppy |
| Open expectation rule | A position is held or a trade is planned for next day | Define what the stock should do at auction / open | Decide after seeing price with no prior model | Actual open contradicts expected script | Mistaking personal wish for expectation |
| Fast cut rule | Opening / early path fails the prior expectation | Cut or reduce quickly | Hold because of hope, face, or回本 desire | The stock rapidly re-enters expected strong path | Replacing discipline with "maybe it comes back" |
| No revenge rule | Loss creates urge to trade for income or recovery | Stop, review, lower size, or空仓 | Add trades only to win back money | Calm system signal appears | Calling revenge trades "opportunity" |
| Delivery-order review | Weekly or monthly review window | Compare orders with K-line and written plan | Review only P/L and ignore process | Repeated same error remains | Using review to justify, not diagnose |
| Subjective-logic filter | You "like" a stock's story but market does not confirm | Wait for market-wide effect and recognizable hot spot | Buy only because logic feels good | Stock fails to get market recognition | Confusing personal research with tradable consensus |
| 做T discipline check | Holding a stock with intraday volatility | Execute only if it fits written discipline | Use 做T as excuse not to stop loss | Price leaves the assumed range / thesis breaks | Turning 做T into emotional attachment |
| 地天板 observation | After broad loss effect, a ground-to-sky reversal appears | Observe whether money is attempting a turn | Blindly buy every dramatic reversal | No broader赚钱效应 follows | Treating a spectacle as a system |
| Empty-position dignity | Market has no clear opportunity | Do nothing and keep cash | Trade because sitting still feels shameful | Clear赚钱效应 returns | Thinking no trade means falling behind |
| Process-first sizing | Trader wants to enlarge size | Require stable behavior and repeatable execution first | Add capital after one lucky win | Rule violations increase with size | Letting P/L replace process proof |

### Market Ecology

#### Severe Loss-Effect Ecology

Signs:

- Core recognizable stocks collapse broadly.
- Limit-down count reaches the transcript's `40几家` danger zone.
- Buying attempts are punished quickly.
- The trader's desire to recover becomes emotionally louder than the plan.

Action:

- Default to cash.
- Stop seeking daily income.
- Review prior mistakes instead of hunting new trades.
- Wait for broad money-making effect or a credible turn signal.

#### Transition Ecology

Potential signs:

- Loss effect begins to exhaust.
- A ground-to-sky reversal appears.
- Hot, recognizable names begin to attract money again.
- The market shows that money is testing a new direction.

Action:

- Observe first; do not mechanically chase.
- Prefer recognizable, hot names over private favorites.
- Verify that the turn is broader than one dramatic stock.

#### Money-Making Effect Ecology

Signs:

- More names allow profit after entry.
- Hot spots persist.
- Recognizable stocks respond to money.
- Opening expectations are met or exceeded.

Action:

- Only then consider action.
- Still require prior plan and early validation.
- Do not enlarge size before execution discipline is proven.

### Cash And Psychology Rebuild

The video's most important sizing contribution is not "small account" alone. It is `psychologically calibrated capital`.

#### Why Reduce Principal

Reduce when:

- The trader has suffered large losses.
- The account number causes revenge, fear, or emotional numbness.
- The trader cannot cut when the thesis fails.
- The trader keeps trying to earn back money each day.

#### Why Not Too Small

Too small can fail when:

- The trader has lost enough that tiny fluctuations feel meaningless.
- The account becomes a toy rather than a training field.
- The trader becomes casual and breaks rules because the amount "does not matter."

#### Practical Translation

Pick a training amount that meets three tests:

1. Losing one trade still hurts enough to demand respect.
2. Losing the entire training account would not destroy life or identity.
3. The amount allows real execution of the chosen strategy without forced overtrading.

In the transcript, `15万` is the suggested example for the specific caller; `2万` is discussed as possibly too small for that person's damaged psychological baseline.

### Execution Framework

#### Before Market

- Define current market state:赚钱效应,亏钱效应, or transition.
- Mark whether core recognizable names are collapsing.
- Write expected behavior for held or planned stocks.
- Decide in advance what would invalidate the trade.

#### At Auction / Open

- Compare actual opening path with written expectation.
- If不及预期, prioritize exit.
- If the market is in severe亏钱效应, do not let a single attractive stock override the environment.

#### After Trade

- Record whether the action matched the plan.
- Do not judge only by profit/loss.
- Mark the trade as: plan-following, random buy, late cut,扛单, revenge, or subjective-logic trade.

#### Weekly / Monthly

- Pull delivery orders.
- Compare every trade with the K-line and the written plan.
- Circle repeated human-nature failures:
  - would not cut;
  - bought only to回本;
  - traded during severe亏钱效应;
  - confused personal logic with market recognition;
  - treated 做T as a way to avoid admitting error.

### Copyable Rules

1. `核心股40几家跌停时, 默认空仓。`
2. `亏钱效应期不做回本交易。`
3. `本金要降到能认真执行的心理区间。`
4. `太小本金让你无感时, 也不是好训练。`
5. `买前写预期, 开盘验预期。`
6. `不及预期先砍, 不先解释。`
7. `只在赚钱效应明确时主动进攻。`
8. `地天板只作为转折观察, 不是自动买点。`
9. `做T不能变成拒绝止损的借口。`
10. `每周/月复盘交割单, 找人性错误。`
11. `亏损后先降频, 不急着下一笔。`
12. `稳定执行之前, 不放大本金。`

### Common Misuses

- Treating `15万` as a universal account prescription.
- Treating `40几家跌停` as a magic number without checking whether core recognizable stocks are collapsing.
- Using "看大局" as a vague feeling instead of checking赚钱/亏钱效应.
- Calling personal stock preference "logic" even when market does not recognize it.
- Using 做T to rationalize not selling a broken trade.
- Thinking空仓 means being weak or missing out.
- Reviewing trades only to explain outcomes, not to diagnose process failures.
- Enlarging size because one trade made money, before rule execution is stable.

### Comparison With Existing Frameworks

#### Versus Liangwange Discipline Curve Framework

Adds:

- A correction to pure tiny-account training: `2万` may be too small for someone whose psychology has been numbed by much larger losses.
- The idea of `psychologically calibrated capital`: size should restore seriousness without restoring panic.

Still consistent:

- Discipline comes before profit.
- Execution quality matters more than cleverness.
- Do not rush to recover losses.

#### Versus Dahuzi Market Breadth Mechanical Framework

Adds:

- A concrete transcript-confirmed danger marker: `40几家` core recognizable limit-downs as a cash-first red line.
- The psychological reason behind breadth defense: bad ecology activates revenge and hope, so the trader must remove himself.

Different emphasis:

- The market-breadth framework is more mechanical and time-window driven.
- This video explains why the trader fails to obey the mechanical framework.

#### Versus Dahuzi Next-Day Open Expectation Framework

Adds:

- The human-nature layer behind "not meeting expectation": cutting is hard because people fear selling low, selling wrong, and losing the chance to recover.
- The transcript does not give a `9:34` line; it only confirms auction / open-stage validation.

Still consistent:

- Write expectation first.
- Validate at the open.
- If不及预期, process quickly.

#### Versus Dragon / Limit-Up Relay Frameworks

Adds:

- Less focus on which dragon to buy, more focus on when the trader should not be allowed to buy anything.
- A cash and psychology rebuild system for damaged traders.

Still consistent:

- Recognizable hot names matter more than private favorites.
- Market-wide effect matters more than isolated logic.

### Review Questions

1. 今天市场是赚钱效应、亏钱效应，还是转折观察期？
2. 核心辨识度个股是否出现大面积跌停？
3. 跌停数量是否接近或超过`40几家`这个危险区？
4. 我今天想交易，是因为系统信号，还是因为想回本？
5. 当前本金是否让我既认真又能冷静？
6. 如果本金太小，我是否开始随便乱做？
7. 持仓明天应有什么开盘表现？
8. 如果开盘不及预期，我的第一动作是什么？
9. 我最近的交割单里，有多少笔是扛单、乱买、该卖没卖？
10. 我是否把做T当成拒绝止损的借口？
11. 我是否因为喜欢某只票的逻辑而忽略市场不认可？
12. 稳定执行还没出现前，我有没有资格放大本金？

### Future Distillation Targets

- Build a combined `cash rebuild + breadth red line + next-day expectation` checklist.
- Compare `15万心理重建` with `2万练习资金` across more大胡子连线 videos.
- Track whether later videos confirm `40几家跌停` as a repeated Dahuzi danger threshold or only this transcript's situational example.
- Separate "market ecology says don't buy" from "individual stock says buy" conflicts in future notes.

## Dahuzi Mystery Mentor Long-Short Trap And Wangwang Team Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `大胡子命真好：又有神秘大佬降临直播间|大胡子学炒股`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SxZlYLzav1E`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- Video upload date from YouTube structured metadata: `2025-04-29T23:07:33-07:00`
- Duration from YouTube metadata: `1219` seconds
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed title, channel, duration, upload date, and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- YouTube page public caption tracks visible to browser metadata: `0`
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `The Logic of Market Sentiment and Strategic Shorting`.
- Speaker identification: NotebookLM describes the source as an experienced / mysterious mentor speaking with `大胡子`; the transcript did not provide a stable public name.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频把大胡子的情绪周期框架上移到`多空主导权`层面：先判断空头是否仍有杀多动力、标杆票是否收红、汪汪队是否入场，再决定能否从防守切换到进攻；可复制部分是`标杆票收红`、`汪汪队后至少7天窗口`、`同班级一字板超过4个`、`强势票5-6点极限回踩`、`假修复不进场`、`瀑布中继不补仓`这些触发器。

### Core Logic

1. `市场本质是多空博弈`.
   - The mentor frames the market as a field where one side controls initiative.
   - The first question is not "what stock is hot" but "who is pressing whom."

2. `高位套牢盘决定空头杀多动力`.
   - After a strong prior run, high-level trapped holders create forced-cleanout demand.
   - If those holders do not cut, the market has not truly cleared.

3. `空头掌控期会杀头羊`.
   - When shorts dominate, the most recognizable bullish carrier can become the primary attack target.
   - In weak markets, being the obvious leader is not automatically safe.

4. `监管/业绩消息可成为空头王炸`.
   - The transcript describes regulatory news or performance shocks as tools that can interrupt bullish momentum and accelerate panic.
   - Do not treat news as isolated; judge whether it aligns with the current long-short power structure.

5. `假修复最危险`.
   - A stock can run above the intraday average line while staying green/red in a way that still does not reverse the market.
   - The rule is to ask whether trapped chips have cleared and whether the key benchmark has truly flipped.

6. `汪汪队是最高-priority reversal signal`.
   - NotebookLM extracted "汪汪队/国家队" as a major signal.
   - Once they act and quantitative funds cooperate, the transcript claims the window is not merely one to three days; it can last at least seven days and may form a second wave.

7. `同班级一字板是题材强度 filter`.
   - First/second-day "一字开杠" names are used to identify emerging strong themes.
   - If more than four names share the same class/attribute, they enter the candidate pool.

8. `强势票回踩必须有边界`.
   - For a genuinely strong target in the next mainline, the transcript gives 5-6 points as a key pullback reference.
   - At 7-8 or 8-9 points, observe whether there is a strong pullback / reversal response; do not treat every deep fall as a buy.

9. `量能杀不下去才可能建底`.
   - A bottom is not "fallen a lot."
   - It requires signs that sellers cannot push volume/price further.

10. `多方进攻失败要全身而退`.
    - When the bullish attempt fails, or systemic risk reappears, the mentor emphasizes full retreat rather than hope-based holding.

### Confirmed Parameters And Limits

| Parameter / Signal | Context | Executable Meaning | Do Not Overextend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `标杆票收红` | NotebookLM identified `香阳/小箱子` as a benchmark signal. | If the benchmark does not turn red/positive, do not assume market reversal. | The transcript does not provide a universal list of benchmark stocks. |
| `汪汪队/国家队` | Highest-priority reversal signal. | If this force enters and quant funds cooperate, expect a stronger window. | The transcript does not give objective identification rules for this capital. |
| `7天` | Minimum safe operation window after汪汪队 action. | Once the signal is real, do not assume the move ends in 1-3 days. | Not a guarantee that every trade in those seven days wins. |
| `同班级 > 4个一字板` | Candidate-pool filter. | More than four same-class / same-attribute one-word boards suggest a theme worth adding to watchlist. | The transcript does not define exact class granularity. |
| `5-6个点` | Strong-ticket pullback / broken-board reference. | A true strong ticket often finds support around a 5-6 point pullback. | Not for random broken boards or weak names. |
| `7-8 / 8-9个点` | Deeper observation zone. | Watch whether there is a strong recovery response in this zone. | Not automatic buy points. |
| `跌破10%` | NotebookLM's second round framed this as a caution zone. | Do not force dip-buying if pullback exceeds normal strong-ticket behavior and lacks recovery. | Not a universal stop-loss formula. |
| `2月5日` | Transcript context for a prior wave / trapped-chip calculation. | Use as historical example for how high-level trapped chips form. | Not a reusable calendar signal. |
| `上周四` | Example of a full bearish call / full retreat. | When long attack fails and systemic risk appears, retreat decisively. | Not a recurring weekday rule. |

Explicitly not provided by the transcript:

- No mathematical formula for trapped-position density.
- No objective rule to identify exactly which trades are `汪汪队/国家队`.
- No exact definition of `同班级`: industry, concept, theme, or capital line.
- No volume threshold for "量能杀不下去."
- No fixed position percentage.
- No fixed stop-loss / take-profit percentage.
- No complete stock-code list for all benchmark names mentioned by transcript.

### Trading System

#### Step 1: Determine Long-Short Control

Questions:

1. Are high-level trapped positions still unresolved?
2. Are shorts attacking recognizable bullish carriers?
3. Are regulatory / earnings shocks amplifying panic?
4. Is the benchmark still failing to close red/positive?
5. Has the market produced fake repair without real cleanout?

If most answers point to short control, the default operation is defense, not dip-buying.

#### Step 2: Identify Cleanout Completion

Possible signs:

- Extreme panic has forced trapped holders to sell.
- Selling pressure becomes ineffective.
- Volume no longer supports further killing.
- Benchmark ticket begins to flip.
- State-backed / major stabilizing force appears.

Missing: exact formula for cleanout completion.

#### Step 3: Switch To Attack Only On Strong Signals

Attack signals from the transcript:

- Benchmark ticket such as `香阳/小箱子` turns red/positive.
- `汪汪队/国家队` enters and creates a sustained environment.
- More than four same-class one-word boards appear.
- Strong candidate pulls back 5-6 points and shows recovery / support.
- The move is not a fake repair inside an unfinished short-control phase.

#### Step 4: Candidate Filtering

Positive filters:

- Belongs to the same class / theme cluster as multiple first/second-day one-word boards.
- More than four names in that class show synchronized strength.
- It has the potential to become a leading / "head" target.
- Its pullback is controlled and shows recovery.
- It appears after the higher-level control signal has improved.

Negative filters:

- Single isolated strength without class support.
- Weak broken board that is not part of a stronger theme.
- Benchmark has not flipped.
- Still in fake repair.
- Still in short-control "kill the head sheep" phase.

#### Entry

Supported entry concepts:

1. Enter after real control shift, not during short-control hope.
2. Watch same-class one-word-board cluster and add the cluster to watchlist.
3. For strong tickets, observe 5-6 point pullback support.
4. At 7-8 or 8-9 points, observe whether a recovery response appears.
5. Enter after "cannot kill further" and after fake repair risk drops.

Not provided:

- Exact intraday minute.
- Exact bid/ask order method.
- Exact volume ratio.
- Exact position size.

#### Exit

Exit / full retreat when:

- Long-side attack fails.
- Short side regains control.
- Systemic risk appears.
- Benchmark refuses to flip.
- A supposed repair remains waterline / green-red fake repair.
- The trade was a premature mid-waterfall补仓.

#### Position And Risk

The transcript has aggressive language around "博命" / pushing size when certainty is very high, but this should be treated as non-copyable unless the trader has a quantified risk system.

Copyable risk rules:

- Do not补仓 in the middle of a waterfall.
- If a补仓 point is wrong and not the bottom, stop immediately and wait for the next real bottom-building process.
- Full retreat is required when long-side logic fails.
- Do not treat a leader as safe during short-control periods.

Non-copyable or risky:

- Using leverage because the mentor says certainty is high.
- Increasing size based on physical state ("吃饱饭") without independent risk controls.

### Hard Rules

1. `如果标杆票不收红, 则不把市场视为真正翻转; 否则才允许进入多头观察。`
2. `如果汪汪队/国家队真实进场, 则把后续至少7天作为可观察做多窗口; 否则不预设行情已经安全。`
3. `如果同班级一字板超过4个, 则将该属性加入重点自选; 否则不因单票强而认定主流。`
4. `如果强势票炸板/回踩在5-6个点附近出现回拉, 则可观察接力; 否则不把随机下跌当买点。`
5. `如果跌到7-8或8-9个点, 则只观察是否有回拉动作; 否则不能机械抄底。`
6. `如果处于瀑布下跌中继, 则禁止补仓; 否则也必须等量能杀不下去。`
7. `如果多方进攻失败或空方重新掌控, 则全身而退; 否则不要用希望替代退出。`
8. `如果高位套牢盘未出清, 则所有修复都先按假修复审查; 否则再考虑反转。`
9. `如果空头掌控期开始杀头羊, 则不要把龙头辨识度当安全垫。`
10. `如果交易理由只是情绪表象, 则回到多空主导权推演; 否则容易与市场不同步。`

### Directional Principles, Not Mechanical Rules

- `高位套牢盘出清`: transcript says this can be calculated, but does not provide formula.
- `量能枯竭`: directionally important but lacks exact turnover / volume threshold.
- `假修复`: based on waterline, average-line behavior, and "uncomfortable" feel; needs further formalization.
- `汪汪队识别`: powerful but not mechanically observable from the transcript alone.
- `博命资本`: should not be copied as leverage or oversized risk.
- `逻辑是真理`: useful as a demand for causal reasoning, dangerous if turned into stubbornness.

### Common Misuses

1. Treating "leader" as safe during short-control periods.
2. Calling every bounce "repair" while the benchmark remains weak.
3. Buying a waterfall decline before cleanout and volume exhaustion.
4. Using 5-6 points as a buy point on random weak stocks.
5. Confusing 7-8 / 8-9 observation zones with automatic entries.
6. Counting four one-word boards without checking same-class / same-attribute relation.
7. Treating汪汪队 as obvious without a defined observation method.
8. Ignoring high-level trapped holders because the chart looks cheap.
9. Treating defensive sectors as safe when the whole market is being killed.
10. Using "logic truth" to refuse updating when market evidence changes.
11. Copying leverage / all-in behavior without the mentor's experience and risk structure.
12. Using a transcript-specific benchmark name without confirming the actual current benchmark.

### Relation To Existing Dahuzi Frameworks

#### Versus Dahuzi Market Breadth Mechanical Framework

Adds:

- Market breadth says whether the environment is broadly good or bad.
- This framework explains why a broad bad environment may persist: high-level trapped chips, short-side control, and forced cleanout.
- Breadth gives visible switches; this note adds the causal story behind the switches.

#### Versus Dahuzi Direct Mentor Strong Carrier Framework

Adds:

- Strong carrier framework emphasizes情绪周期,强者载体,班底/小弟.
- This video adds "shorts may target the strongest carrier" when shorts control the field.
- It also adds汪汪队 and benchmark flip as higher-level permission signals.

#### Versus Chen Xiaoqun Baotuan / First-Board Framework

Adds:

- Chen Xiaoqun first-board logic watches front-row names and group behavior.
- This video gives a numeric group filter: same-class one-word boards `>4` enter watchlist.
- It also warns that group strength must be judged under long-short control, not in isolation.

#### Versus Sangtianlu Quant Low-Frequency Window Framework

Adds:

- Sangtianlu emphasizes large market windows and low-frequency action.
- This video gives one such window:汪汪队 intervention and at least seven-day continuation.
- It also separates fake repair from real control shift.

### Review Questions

2. 高位套牢盘是否已经出清，还是仍有杀多动力？
3. 标杆票是否收红？没有收红我为什么要做多？
4. 今天是否有汪汪队/国家队级别的稳定信号？

## 17. Dahuzi Next-Day Open Expectation Time-Stop Framework

## Dahuzi Next-Day Open Expectation Time-Stop Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `高手连线大胡子分享打板心得——次日开盘涨跌解读，超预期、不及预期、核按钮|大胡子学炒股`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XZP-LbYj8SA`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- YouTube structured metadata description: `本条视频来自于2025年4月18日的连线`
- Video upload date from YouTube structured metadata: `2025-05-07T20:39:50-07:00`
- Duration from YouTube metadata: about `1701-1702` seconds
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed title, channel, duration, upload date, and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `Trading Psychology and Pre-Market Strategies for Momentum Stocks`.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频把打板接力的次日处理拆成一套`预期差算法`：前一晚先建立应有开盘，`9:25`用竞价校验超预期/不及预期，`9:30-9:34`用零轴、分时拐头和秒封确认强弱；不及预期就按时间止损处理，符合或超预期才允许持有、加仓或等待分歧后的回封。

### Confirmed Parameters And Limits

These are transcript-confirmed operating anchors extracted through NotebookLM. Treat them as short-term limit-up / consecutive-board rules, not universal trading laws.

| Parameter | Context | Executable Meaning | Do Not Overextend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `9:15-9:25` | 集合竞价 | Build and verify the day's initial plan; judge expectation gap. | Not a standalone buy signal. |
| `9:25` | 竞价结束 | Decide whether today's tone allows attack, defense, or only sell. | Must be paired with prior-night expectation. |
| `9:30-9:31` | 极强开盘 | True strongest names should秒封 / 冲天炮 in the first minute. | Not every quick rise is a valid board. |
| `9:34` | 不及预期处理线 | If the stock cannot reclaim zero axis or forms intraday double-top / turns down, sell. | This is a time-stop for this mode, not a universal stop for all stocks. |
| `开盘前5分钟` | 超短黄金窗口 | Most ultra-short decisions happen here; basic skills must be ready before market opens. | Do not improvise without plan. |
| `+6%` | 大高开 threshold | A high open above roughly +6% is treated as strong / notable. | Must compare to what should have happened. |
| `-6%以下` | 核按钮 threshold | Extreme weak open / panic open; wait for first-minute repair, then decide. | Not automatically a buyable overreaction. |
| `1/3 或 1/4` | 分仓比例 | Split capital into 3-4 parts; no full-position single-stock bet. | Do not increase just because one name looks perfect. |
| `3-4板以上` | Preferred relay zone | The speaker prefers higher-board relay and gives up first-board because first-board needs more fundamentals / content reserve. | Not permission to chase every high board. |
| `1万亿以下` | Weak liquidity | Market tends toward single-name / single-line baotuan. | Not every low-volume day has a tradable baotuan. |
| `1.5万亿-2万亿` | Better liquidity / tolerance | More room for dual mainlines and higher tolerance. | Still need leader, expectation, and board quality. |
| `+8.08%` | Example of possible不及预期 | If yesterday's stock should have opened one-line /秒封 but only opens around +8% and does not seal, it may be weak despite high open. | Do not treat `8.08%` as a universal line. |

Explicitly not provided as hard rules:

- No universal fixed percentage stop loss such as `-5%` or `-10%`.
- No current stock recommendation.
- Mentioned stock names are case examples, not buy recommendations.

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `交易看预期差, 不是只看涨跌`.
   - The core comparison is `what should happen` versus `what actually happens`.
   - A high open can still be weak if the stock should have opened stronger.

2. `前一晚必须先写出应然`.
   - Use previous board time, board quality, volume, news, and market ecology to define expected open.
   - Without prior expectation, there is no meaningful "超预期" or "不及预期".

3. `9:25是竞价分水岭`.
   - It tells whether the plan is confirmed, weakened, or invalidated.
   - The trader should already know which names are watched before this moment.

4. `9:30-9:34是执行窗口`.
   - Ultra-short trades cannot wait until the day becomes obvious.
   - The first few minutes decide whether to hold, sell, add, or stand aside.

5. `9:34不及预期就处理`.
   - If the stock cannot recover zero axis or turns down by the checkpoint, sell.
   - Do not fantasize about afternoon repair while the current script is broken.

6. `核按钮不是立刻竞价割`.
   - If it opens below `-6%`, first observe whether the first minute repairs.
   - If it cannot reclaim zero axis and turns down, exit.

7. `只做板块龙一`.
   - The transcript warns against 老三/老四 and follower stocks.
   - Followers are ruled by the leader's face and have worse readability.

8. `分仓是生存规则`.
   - The system uses `1/3` or `1/4` sizing.
   - Even a strong dragon can damage the account if sized recklessly.

9. `首板让位于3-4板以上接力`.
   - First board needs strong content / fundamental reserve.
   - This speaker's more comfortable zone is higher-board relay.

10. `安全垫来自先手`.
    - If yesterday's position has profit and today's board confirms, adding can be done against the cushion.
    - If the board fails, sell old position instead of passively raising cost.

11. `分歧回封要等雨下完`.
    - Do not buy during early unresolved storm.
    - Prefer afternoon / later confirmed reseal with enough volume after the disagreement.

12. `从左往右复盘`.
    - Judge decisions by what was knowable at the time.
    - Do not use later outcomes to rewrite the logic.

### Next-Day Expectation Gap Workflow

#### Step 1: Prior-Night Review

Write the "should open" model before the market:

1. What was yesterday's board quality?
   - 秒封 / one-line / no volume implies the next day should be very strong.
   - Late weak board or heavily disputed board lowers expectations.
2. Is there overnight news?
   - Bad news normally lowers expected open.
   - If the stock opens high despite bad news, that can be超预期.
3. What is the stock's rank?
   - Prefer board / concept leader.
   - Avoid third or fourth follower names.
4. What is market liquidity?
   - Below `1万亿`: single-line / baotuan logic.
   - `1.5万亿-2万亿`: more tolerance and possible dual mainlines.

#### Step 2: `9:25` Auction Check

Compare actual auction to expectation:

- 超预期:
  - Expected low open because of bad news, but it opens flat/high or above `+6%`.
  - Expected normal, but it opens as one-line / very strong.
- 符合预期:
  - The stock opens roughly where the prior-night model expected.
- 不及预期:
  - Expected one-line /秒封 but actual open is only high without sealing.
  - Expected strong open but it opens low or quickly weakens.
- 核按钮:
  - Opens below `-6%`, representing extreme panic or weak expectation.

#### Step 3: `9:30-9:34` Verification

Observe path, not only opening price:

- Strong confirmation:
  - `9:30-9:31` direct秒封 / 冲天炮.
  - Low open repairs within about one minute, crosses zero axis, and continues strengthening.
- Weak confirmation:
  - Cannot reclaim zero axis.
  - Intraday path turns down.
  - Forms a分时双头.
  - High open does not seal when it should have sealed.

#### Step 4: Action

- Sell:
  - If不及预期 and by `9:34` cannot reclaim zero axis / turns down / double-tops.
  - If the script has broken, do not wait for a different afternoon script.
- Hold or add:
  - If actual path meets or exceeds expectation and board confirms.
  - Add only within the `1/3` or `1/4` sizing framework.
- Buy:
  - Prefer later分歧回封 after the early storm has passed.
  - Reseal should have enough volume to digest prior high / trapped sellers.
- Stand aside:
  - If the market is in retreat or the plan is unclear at `9:25`.

### Trading Rule Table

| Rule | Trigger | Allowed Action | Forbidden Action | Failure Signal | Common Misuse |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Prior expectation rule | Before trading day | Write expected open for target names | Judging "strong/weak" without prior model | Cannot explain expected open | Calling every high open strong |
| Auction decision rule | `9:25` | Classify超预期/符合/不及预期 | Adding random new names | Auction contradicts plan | Treating auction as isolated number |
| First-minute strength rule | `9:30-9:31` | Treat direct秒封/冲天炮 as strong confirmation | Waiting too long on supposed strongest names | Strong name cannot seal fast | Calling slow grind "冲天炮" |
| `9:34` time-stop rule | 不及预期 and no zero-axis repair | Sell / clear | Hoping for afternoon comeback | Reclaims and strengthens before checkpoint | Using price stop instead of script stop |
| Nuclear-button rule | Opens below `-6%` | Observe first-minute repair; sell if no zero-axis reclaim | Immediate emotional竞价割 or blind dip buy | Cannot repair, turns down | Treating all核按钮 as opportunity |
| Leader-only rule | Selecting target | Trade only concept / board leader | Buy 老三/老四 followers | Follower depends on leader and lacks own path | Buying follower because it is cheaper |
| Split-position rule | Any buy | Use `1/3` or `1/4` | Full position single stock | No exception given | "This time is special" full-size bet |
| Higher-board focus | Choosing mode | Focus on `3-4板以上` relay | Forcing first-board trades | First-board logic requires content reserve | Treating all high boards as good |
| Safety-cushion add | Prior position has profit and today's board confirms | Add against cushion; sell old if board fails | Blindly average up before confirmation | Board breaks or不及预期 | Raising cost without exit plan |
| Disagreement-reseal rule | Early storm has passed and later reseal has volume | Board buy with defined size | Buy during unresolved early分歧 | Big money breaks board and no reseal | "Rain not over" but already entering |
| Liquidity ecology rule | Market turnover level known | Low turnover: baotuan; high turnover: tolerate multiple lines | Applying same tactic in all volume regimes | Volume cannot support the mode | Treating thresholds as exact magic lines |
| Noise isolation rule | System is defined | Ignore incompatible advice / styles | Randomly switching methods intraday | Plan changes because of outside noise | Calling distraction "learning" |

### Market Ecology

#### `1万亿以下`: Baotuan Ecology

Meaning:

- Liquidity is limited.
- Funds may concentrate into one line or one leader.
- Followers have low readability and low tolerance.

Action:

- Only look at the strongest leader.
- Reduce breadth of watchlist.
- Avoid pretending there are multiple healthy mainlines.

#### `1.5万亿-2万亿`: Higher-Tolerance Ecology

Meaning:

- Market can support more than one mainline.
- Dual-mainline trading is more possible.
- Higher-board relay has better tolerance than in weak liquidity.

Action:

- Still rank leaders.
- Allow more than one line only if money and board quality confirm.
- Do not confuse higher tolerance with no risk.

### Position Management

#### Split Position

Core rule:

- Divide capital into `1/3` or `1/4`.
- Do not full-position one stock, no matter how attractive.

Why:

- Consecutive-board trades can reverse violently.
- Account survival is more important than one perfect-looking opportunity.

#### Dynamic Safety Cushion

The transcript describes an active cushion logic:

1. Have prior position / first hand.
2. If next day confirms strength, add only after board confirmation.
3. If it breaks or fails expectation, sell the old position to preserve cushion.
4. Do not let "adding" become passive cost raising.

This is closer to dynamic risk transfer than simple averaging up.

### Copyable Rules

1. `前一晚写出明天应有开盘。`
2. `9:25只做计划内标的。`
3. `9:30-9:31看最强能否秒封。`
4. `9:34不及预期不上零轴就卖。`
5. `+6%以上才算明显大高开。`
6. `-6%以下按核按钮处理, 先看1分钟修复。`
7. `只做板块龙一, 不碰老三老四。`
8. `每笔按1/3或1/4分仓。`
9. `放弃首板, 专注3-4板以上接力。`
10. `早盘分歧不赌, 等雨下完后的回封。`
11. `回封要有足够量能消化前高套牢盘。`
12. `从左往右复盘, 不用结果倒推过程。`

### Common Misuses

- Seeing `+8%` and assuming it must be strong, while the prior model required one-line /秒封.
- Treating `9:34` as a magic number without first defining expected open.
- Buying followers because the true leader feels expensive.
- Using `1/3` or `1/4` only when uncertain, then full-positioning when excited.
- Calling every low open a核按钮 opportunity.
- Buying early分歧 before the storm has resolved.
- Using later回封 to shame an earlier rule-based sell.
- Treating `1万亿` / `2万亿` as exact mathematical borders instead of liquidity regimes.
- Copying the speaker's exercise / punishment routine without reviewing the trading error.

### Comparison With Existing Frameworks

#### Versus Dragon Leader Strategy

- `dragon-leader-strategy-framework.md` explains leader formation through board ladder,换手, and consensus.
- This video adds the next-day operating layer: how to read expectation, auction, first minutes, and failure.
- Use dragon framework to identify leaders; use this framework to process the next-day open.

#### Versus Liangwange Discipline

- `liangwange-discipline-curve-framework.md` emphasizes discipline, equity curve, and avoiding emotional drift.
- This video turns discipline into concrete time and sizing rules: `9:34`, `1/3`, `1/4`, no followers, no full position.
- Use Liangwange for account-growth discipline; use this for limit-up relay execution.

#### Versus Dahuzi Market Breadth Mechanical Model

- `dahuzi-market-breadth-mechanical-framework.md` uses market breadth and fixed windows like `10:40` / `14:40`.
- This video works earlier in the day, focusing on `9:25` and `9:34`.
- Together they form morning expectation processing plus broader daily mechanical control.

#### Versus Limit-Up Certainty

- `limit-up-certainty-framework.md` explains why打板 buys confirmed market consensus.
- This video adds what happens after the board: if next-day premium fails, the thesis fails quickly.
- Limit-up certainty covers entry philosophy; this covers next-day confirmation and failure handling.

### Review Questions

1. What did I expect this stock to open at before market?
2. At `9:25`, was it超预期, 符合预期, or不及预期?
3. Was it a board / concept leader or a follower?
4. If it was a follower, why was I watching it?
5. Did it seal by `9:30-9:31` if it was supposed to be strongest?
6. At `9:34`, was it above zero axis or failing?
7. Did I sell a broken script or fantasize about another script?
8. Was the open below `-6%`; if yes, did it repair in the first minute?
9. Did my position follow `1/3` or `1/4` sizing?
10. Was any add-on backed by a real safety cushion?
11. Did the market turnover support baotuan or dual mainlines?
12. Am I judging the trade from left to right, or from the final result backward?

### Future Distillation Targets

- Clarify whether the `9:34` checkpoint changes under different market liquidity regimes.
- Track more examples of `+8%` high-open but still不及预期.
- Compare this dynamic safety-cushion method with later Dahuzi position-control videos.
- Build a replay template for `previous-day board -> expected open -> actual auction -> first-five-minute path -> action`.

## Dahuzi Objective Signal And Real/Fake Capital Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `两万哥不敢说的真假资金 被这位大佬以另一种方式说出来|大胡子学炒股`.
