This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频的核心是把短线交易拆成一套`客观信号系统`：盘前用已知条件推演可能情境，开盘和盘中用集合竞价、连板梯队、量价、L2/大单、炸板不回等客观事实验证，盘后用复盘和备胎池维护先手；真正要识别的不是“明天谁涨”，而是哪些资金信号是真实可跟随，哪些只是主观幻觉、网红催票或收割型资金制造的噪音。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `信号 = 已知条件推演出的客观触发点`.
   - 信号不是感觉，也不是愿望。
   - 盘前只能形成预案，盘中必须让事实验证预案。

2. `主观信号是亏损之源`.
   - “我觉得跌多了要涨”“我觉得大资金会拉”都不是交易信号。
   - 只要动作不是由客观事实触发，就应标记为主观交易。

3. `普通版看广度，远航版看深度`.
   - 同花顺普通版用于观察全市场涨跌幅、即时异动和盘面广度。
   - 同花顺远航版用于精细管理自选、连板梯队、二板以上观察池和首板精选池。

4. `连板梯队是短线资金风向标`.
   - 最热点的钱通常在连板票上。
   - 二板以上必须全部观察，三板以上用于判断情绪强弱、退潮与续强。

5. `弱转强只能由市场确认`.
   - 昨天弱、今天可能强，只是预案。
   - 需要集合竞价、开盘溢价、量价配合和封板/承接行为确认。

6. `炸板不回、高开低走是卖出信号`.
   - 当盘面给出弱势证据，不能靠“还能拉起来”对抗事实。
   - 短线纪律是尊重卖出信号，而不是保护昨天的看法。

7. `织布机只看关键节点`.
   - 对资金无明显进出的横盘票，中间波动不值得反复操作。
   - 主要处理窗口是 `9:35前` 和 `14:50后`。

8. `卖出不代表删除`.
   - 逻辑没有破坏但暂时卖出的票，应进入备胎池 / 待启动池。
   - 如果后续向上变盘，先手来自持续跟踪，而不是临时全市场再找。

9. `真假资金要分开处理`.
   - 真资金通常表现为连板梯队溢价、盘中量价验证、关键节点放量变盘。
   - 假资金/噪音常表现为主观幻觉、网红催票、评论区狂热、套利砸盘或诱多大单。

10. `手续费不是核心矛盾`.
    - 该卖不卖、该调不调，通常比手续费更伤。
    - 但这条不能变成乱动的借口：忽略手续费，只针对客观信号触发的动作。

11. `盘后复盘是盘中反应速度的来源`.
    - 盘中看似快速，其实来自盘前/盘后的观察池、逻辑推演和预案。
    - 复盘不是总结情绪，而是把明天可能出现的信号写成动作。

### Objective Signal Table

| Signal | Source / Location | Trigger | Action | Invalid / Disabled When | Common Misuse |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 已知条件推演 | 盘后/盘前全局逻辑 | 结合消息、题材、个股位置推演可能结果 | 写出次日买入/卖出信号点 | 盘中走势与推演完全背离 | 把推演当预测，盘中强行交易 |
| 集合竞价信号 | 9:15-9:25 盘头交易界面 | 撮合价、量能、托单/压单验证预案 | 验证弱转强或开盘溢价 | 遇到高开砸盘、量化/收割型资金 | 只看高低开，不看资金属性 |
| 首板筛选信号 | 同花顺普通版/远航版 | 全市场首板中筛出逻辑最强者 | 精选约 10-15 个加入观察池 | 题材整体走弱或封单极差 | 全选或全不选，缺乏审美过滤 |
| 二板以上全观察 | 同花顺远航版自选栏 | 市场所有二板及以上标的 | 必须全部加入观察栏 | 断板且无反抽逻辑 | 凭个人喜好删除不喜欢的票 |
| 三板以上情绪 | 连板梯队最高标 | 高位票开盘溢价、盘中走势、修复能力 | 判断续强、走弱或退潮 | 权重强拉造成情绪误判 | 忽略高位负反馈，强做中位股 |
| 弱转强信号 | 集合竞价/盘中表现 | 昨日弱势今日高开、承接、缩量封板或转强 | 盘中验证后跟进 | 高开后随即被爆头 | 把“跌多了想涨”当弱转强 |
| 高开低走 / 炸板不回 | 盘中即时走势 | 冲高回落、炸板后不能快速回封 | 卖出或降风险 | 极少数深 V 长腿，需后续确认 | 幻想还能拉回，对抗盘面 |
| 织布机信号 | 盘中分时图 | 横盘不动、资金无明显进出 | 盘中不动，仅节点处理 | 突然出现真实大资金拉升 | 反复做 T 浪费注意力和成本 |
| 9:35 前节点 | 盘初交易时段 | 冷门票成交量最集中的真实反应窗口 | 处理持仓或核心买卖 | 逻辑突变、封死涨跌停 | 错过后在震荡区乱动 |
| 14:50 后节点 | 尾盘交易时段 | 收盘前最后 10 分钟确认去留 | 最终调仓、确认留存 | 尾盘非理性抢筹/砸盘 | 提前预测收盘价而早动 |
| 备胎池 / 待启动池 | 独立自选板块 | 已卖出但逻辑未破、等待方向选择 | 每日扫视，向上变盘时杀回 | 跌破关键支撑，方向向下 | 卖完就删，起飞时反应变慢 |
| 网红催票信号 | 抖音、雪球、评论区 | 某标的被大规模推荐或人声鼎沸 | 标记风险，原则上不接盘 | 极少数真正产业趋势核心 | 跟着热闹盘接飞刀 |
| 大单 / L2 信号 | 同花顺普通版 | 异常大笔成交或单向挂单 | 辅助验证资金攻击欲望 | 诱多挂单、对倒单 | 迷信单笔大单，忽略整体量价 |
| 手续费心态 | 个人账户管理 | 信号触发但犹豫成本 | 该调就调，该卖就卖 | 资金太小且极高频交易时要另算 | 为省手续费错过明确动作 |

### Real vs Fake Capital

#### Real Capital Signals

- `连板梯队溢价`
  - 核心连板票在竞价和开盘后的溢价，代表主流热钱方向。
  - Use it to judge whether market emotion rewards continuation.

- `盘中客观验证`
  - 盘前预案在盘中得到量价、承接、封板、回封或放量配合。
  - This is where a hypothesis becomes a tradable signal.

- `待启动池变盘`
  - 织布机 / 逻辑未破标的在关键节点放量向上选择方向.
  - Because it was tracked beforehand, response speed can beat临时选股.

- `连板全观察`
  - 二板以上全部纳入观察，减少主观筛掉真正资金选择的风险。

#### Fake Capital / Noise Signals

- `主观幻觉`
  - “我觉得跌多了会涨”“我觉得大游资会拉”“昨天有利好所以今天一定涨”。
  - Treat as no signal.

- `网红吹票资金`
  - 抖音老师、评论区、雪球短时狂热形成的跟风盘。
  - If no objective market confirmation appears, do not become exit liquidity.

- `套利/收割型资金`
  - 高开吸引跟风后快速砸盘。
  - The transcript explicitly flags awareness of strong砸盘 / 收割 attributes in some资金类型.

- `单笔大单幻觉`
  - A single L2 print or large order is not enough.
  - It must fit the whole volume-price structure and timing.

Manual rule:

- Follow only signals that become objective on the screen. Do not follow stories that only exist in the head or on social media.

### Five-Stage Execution Flow

#### 1. 盘前: 预案期

Actions:

- Collect post-market and overnight signals: announcements, comments, industry logic, discussion heat.
- Use known conditions to infer possible signal outcomes.
- Build tomorrow's watchlists:
  - 同花顺普通版: broad market and whole-field movement.
  - 远航版: all two-board-plus names and selected first-board names.
  - 备胎池: sold-but-not-dead names waiting for direction choice.

Output:

- For each candidate, write:
  - What signal would confirm it?
  - What signal would invalidate it?
  - What action happens if either appears?

#### 2. 9:15-9:35: 验证期

Actions:

- Observe auction price, volume, support/resistance orders, and opening premium.
- Verify weak-to-strong plans.
- For cold / 织布机 names, treat this as one of the few meaningful action windows.

Do not:

- Buy just because it opens high.
- Assume weak-to-strong before the market proves it.
- Ignore who may be selling into the open.

#### 3. 盘中: 监控期

Actions:

- Watch high-board, two-board and first-board ecology.
- Track whether today's money is in continuation, retreat, or fake excitement.
- If objective sell signals appear, execute.
- Ignore meaningless micro-movement in 织布机 names unless a real large-order / volume-price signal appears.

Do not:

- Turn a pre-market hypothesis into a stubborn intraday belief.
- Reinterpret sell signals because of yesterday's logic.
- Treat social-media heat as capital confirmation.

#### 4. 14:50-收盘: 决断期

Actions:

- Make final decisions on 织布机 / uncertain holdings.
- Reduce or handle names that failed to seal or had weak sealing quality.
- Decide which sold names move to the待启动池.

Do not:

- Guess the closing price too early.
- Chase irrational late-session noise without confirmation.

#### 5. 盘后: 复盘期

Actions:

- Review whether trades followed objective signals or subjective feelings.
- Compare plans versus actual screen evidence.
- Update:
  - two-board-plus full observation pool,
  - first-board selected pool,
  - wait-to-start / backup pool,
  - risk list of social-media crowded names.

Output:

- Tomorrow's if/then action sheet.

### Trading System

#### Market Emotion / Money-Making Effect

Primary indicators:

- 三板及以上高位票的溢价 and intraday path.
- 二板票的承接 and promotion rate.
- 首板数量 and seal quality.
- 炸板不回 / 高开低走 frequency.
- Whether weak-to-strong attempts are rewarded or punished.

Manual interpretation:

- If high-board premiums and repairs improve, continuation ecology is alive.
- If high-board names are爆头,炸板不回, or no longer produce premium, the market may be retreating.
- If only social-media heat exists but the board ladder does not confirm, treat it as noise.

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- Appears in the proper objective pool: all two-board-plus or selected first-board.
- Has clear reason from盘前推演.
- Receives real auction / intraday confirmation.
- Fits the current emotion state.
- If sold before, remains in backup pool only when logic is not broken.

Negative filters:

- Chosen only because of a personal feeling.
- Driven mainly by网红催票.
- Opens high but immediately faces dump pressure.
- 织布机 name being traded outside meaningful nodes.
- One large order without full volume-price confirmation.

#### Entry

Allowed entries:

- Weak-to-strong after objective auction and intraday confirmation.
- Continuation trade when连板梯队溢价 and market emotion support it.
- Backup-pool re-entry when a logic-intact name chooses upward direction at a key node.
- Intraday large-order / volume-price signal only when it aligns with the written plan.

Entry guardrails:

- No entry from pure prediction.
- No entry from social-media excitement.
- No entry because of FOMO after deleting a sold stock from tracking.
- No entry during meaningless middle-session noise in a织布机.

#### Exit

Exit when:

- High open fails and price walks down.
- Board breaks and cannot quickly reseal.
- Objective sell signal contradicts yesterday's logic.
- Funding source appears to be exit-liquidity / harvest-style money.
- The candidate violates the prewritten invalidation condition.

Do not exit only because:

- A tiny cost or fee feels annoying.
- Social media says the stock is dead without screen confirmation.
- Price wiggles inside a known织布机 state before action nodes.

#### Position And Loss Handling

This transcript is less about exact sizing and more about decision integrity. Synthesize with existing discipline frameworks:

- Use Liangwange: equity curve and personality fit decide size.
- Use Dahuzi market-breadth model: breadth gates and fixed windows prevent overtrading.
- Use Trader Probability: if/then plans, expected payoff and invalidation must be written before action.
- Use Beijing Chaojia: respect筹码面 and mechanical selling when the screen goes against you.

After a loss:

- Classify whether it was:
  - signal invalidation,
  - fake-capital trap,
  - social-media influence,
  - premature weak-to-strong assumption,
  - failure to execute sell signal,
  - backup pool tracking failure.

### If/Then Rulebook

1. If a trade idea cannot be tied to an objective signal, then it is not a trade.
2. If二板以上 appears, then add it to the full observation pool; do not filter by personal taste.
3. If首板数量 is large, then select the strongest 10-15 by logic, seal quality, and theme fit.
4. If三板以上 names have poor premium and weak paths, then reduce continuation expectation.
5. If a weak-to-strong thesis is not confirmed by auction / opening / intraday evidence, then do not act.
6. If high open becomes high-open-low-walk, then respect the sell signal.
7. If a board breaks and cannot reseal quickly, then treat the original bullish thesis as damaged.
8. If a stock enters织布机 mode, then avoid middle-session operations unless a real large-order signal appears.
9. If a cold /织布机 name reaches 9:35前 or 14:50后 with clear direction, then make the planned decision.
10. If a sold stock's logic remains intact, then move it to backup pool, not the trash.
11. If a backup-pool stock chooses upward direction with volume at a key node, then consider it as a new signal, not regret repair.
12. If social media is crowded but market confirmation is absent, then treat the crowd as risk.
13. If L2 shows a large order, then require supporting volume-price context before acting.
14. If手续费 is the only reason not to execute a valid sell or swap, then ignore the fee and follow the signal.
15. If a loss occurred, then identify which objective signal was ignored or which fake signal was believed.

### Copyable Practices

- Keep separate watchlists:
  - `二板以上全观察`
  - `首板精选`
  - `呼吸战法待启动 / 备胎池`
  - `社交媒体拥挤风险`
- Before market open, write if/then plans for every candidate.
- During 9:15-9:35, validate instead of predict.
- During the middle session, separate real new signals from noise.
- Around 14:50, make final decisions on uncertain /织布机 names.
- After close, log each trade as objective-signal trade or subjective trade.
- Track which social-media crowded names created fake strength or exit liquidity.
- Review all sold names for whether they belong in backup pool.

### Easy Misuses

1. Calling every plan a signal.
   - A plan becomes a signal only after objective screen evidence appears.

2. Treating high open as weak-to-strong.
   - High open can be a trap if dump pressure appears immediately.

3. Deleting sold stocks too soon.
   - If logic is intact, deletion destroys future response speed.

4. Trading织布机 out of boredom.
   - Most of the middle path is noise; use key nodes.

5. Overtrusting L2.
   - Single prints and orders can be fake or incomplete without structure.

6. Thinking网红热度 equals real money.
   - It may mean the exit-liquidity crowd has arrived.

7. Using手续费 as a reason to avoid discipline.
   - Fees matter less than holding a broken signal.

8. Treating this as pure discretion.
   - The point is not faster intuition; it is a prewritten signal-action map.

### Alignment With Existing Frameworks

#### Reinforces Liangwange

- Both reject 主观幻想 and emphasize discipline.
- Liangwange says shape + volume and equity curve; this video operationalizes that into screen signals, watchlists and key time nodes.
- Both warn against outsourcing judgment to online personalities.

#### Reinforces Dahuzi Mechanical Models

- Both convert trading into visible triggers and time windows.
- This video's `9:35前 / 14:50后` complements Dahuzi's broader `10:40 / 14:40` discipline devices.
- Dahuzi breadth model controls when to trade; this signal framework controls what to watch and how to validate.

#### Reinforces Trader Probability Discipline

- A signal table is an if/then trading plan.
- It reduces ruin-risk behavior by forcing invalidation and action before emotions rise.
- Missed valid signals and followed fake signals should both be reviewed.

#### Reinforces Chaogu Yangjia

- Yangjia says observe crowd emotion rather than be used by it.
- This video gives concrete proxies:连板梯队、三板以上溢价、社交媒体拥挤、炸板不回.

#### Reinforces Dragon / Limit-Up Frameworks

- 连板梯队 and next-day premium are used as market-confirmed strength measures.
- The video adds workflow: all two-board-plus names must be tracked, while first boards can be filtered.

#### Reinforces Beijing Chaojia

- Mechanical selling, respect for筹码面 and caution toward砸盘属性 align with the Beijing Chaojia risk-control layer.
- Potential tension: the video mentions some资金 as收割/砸盘 risks, while Beijing Chaojia's own framework emphasizes disciplined risk control rather than personality labels. Resolve by watching actual screen behavior, not relying on labels alone.

### Non-Copyable Edges

- Fast推演 from known conditions.
- Taste to pick 10-15 useful names from 30 first boards.
- Experienced judgment of which social-media heat is real catalyst versus exit crowd.
- Intraday speed reading L2 and order-book behavior.
- Emotional ability to sell immediately when screen evidence invalidates a loved idea.

Copy the signal system; do not pretend to copy the veteran's reflexes.

### Review Questions

1. Did every action today come from an objective signal, or from my feeling?
2. Which premarket hypotheses were confirmed by screen evidence?
3. Which hypotheses were invalidated, and did I still trade them?
4. Did I keep all two-board-plus names in the observation pool?
5. Did I filter first boards by logic and seal quality, or randomly?
6. What did the three-board-plus names say about market emotion?
7. Did I mistake a high open for true weak-to-strong?
8. Did any holding give a high-open-low-walk or炸板不回 sell signal?
9. Did I trade a织布机 in the middle session for no reason?
10. Which sold names should enter the backup pool?
11. Did social-media heat make me buy exit liquidity?
12. Did I overread a single L2 large order without volume-price structure?
13. Did手续费影响 a signal-based action?
14. Which loss came from believing fake capital?
15. What exact if/then plan should be written for tomorrow?

### Future Distillation Directions

- Find more videos from this anonymous 大胡子 guest and extract exact examples of `信号`.
- Build a reusable daily template for `二板以上全观察 / 首板精选 / 备胎池`.
- Compare 9:35 and 14:50 nodes with Dahuzi's 10:40 / 14:40 timing rules.
- Track whether `网红催票` is always negative or only negative when the board ladder fails to confirm.
- Add concrete thresholds if future videos define volume, L2, or auction criteria more precisely.

## Dahuzi Ten-Day Line Tail-Session Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `既然你们更喜欢实战技法，继续安排|大胡子学炒股`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pwCKV1E6mR4`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Core figure identified by NotebookLM transcript: `大胡子`, described as a Suzhou mathematics / Olympiad teacher with about six years of trading experience.
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

大胡子这套方法不是追涨、打板或题材推演，而是把短线回调做成一台概率机器：只在有资金参与过的上升趋势股里，等缩量阴线回踩十日线，尾盘 14:50 附近分仓买入，次日 10:00 前按规则止盈或止损，用大量样本和机械执行吃短线反弹概率。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `买阴卖阳` is the core posture.
   - Buy after a controlled pullback, not after emotional acceleration.
   - The setup tries to avoid becoming the late buyer of a visible rise.

2. The strategy buys a rebound probability, not a long-term thesis.
   - The planned holding window is very short.
   - If the next morning does not confirm the rebound, the trade thesis is weakened.

3. The ten-day moving average is the operating line.
   - The method avoids the five-day line because it is too aggressive.
   - It also avoids broken ten-day-line trades because the support premise has failed.

4. Volume must show two layers: past strong participation and current quiet pullback.
   - A prior heavy-volume bullish candle proves that money once cared.
   - A current low-volume bearish candle suggests reduced selling pressure, not panic distribution.

5. `14:50` is a discipline device.
   - Waiting until the late session reduces intraday noise.
   - The trader sees most of the day's volume and price structure before deciding.

6. `次日 10:00` is the main exit discipline.
   - The edge is expected morning rebound premium.
   - Holding past the planned window easily turns a rebound trade into a hope trade.

7. Diversification is part of the edge.
   - The transcript describes splitting capital across many stocks, such as ten positions.
   - This treats each trade as a sample in a probability distribution rather than a heroic single bet.

8. Do not do intraday T.
   - The speaker treats T-trading as too difficult and easy to reverse.
   - The manual version should protect the simple loop: late buy, next-morning sell, review.

9. Do not stare at the whole trading day.
   - The useful windows are mainly early morning and late session.
   - Midday noise is treated as a source of emotional mistakes.

10. Stop loss is not negotiable.
    - Breaking the ten-day line by about 3% is treated as forced exit.
    - A weak open that cannot reclaim the intraday average within the observation window is also failure.

11. Review intensity is the hidden moat.
    - The method sounds simple, but selecting the best candidates requires scanning and reviewing many charts.
    - The speaker's edge includes years of repetition, not only the visible formula.

12. This is a bottom-up technical/probability system.
    - It can ignore sector logic more than other frameworks, but that is also a risk.
    - When broad liquidity or mainstream sector context is poor, the same chart pattern may deserve lower size or no trade.

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Positive environment:

- Broad market is not in violent downside.
- There are enough active stocks with visible volume history.
- Morning rebounds and short-term repair are being rewarded.
- Ten-day-line pullbacks are not being broken frequently.

Negative environment:

- Broad market or mainstream sectors are collapsing.
- Many formerly strong stocks break moving-average support with volume.
- Morning rebounds fail quickly and turn into intraday selloffs.
- Liquidity is too thin to support high-frequency next-day exits.

Manual synthesis:

- The transcript emphasizes individual stock shape more than sector cycle.
- For safer use, wrap the method with Qianniuge/Yangjia-style market ecology: only trade larger size when market volume, mainstream breathing, or赚钱效应 support repair trades.

#### Stock Filtering

Required positive filters:

- Prior `爆量大阳` or obvious heavy-volume bullish participation.
- Current pullback is a bearish candle or weak-looking day, not a chase candle.
- Current pullback volume is clearly smaller than the prior heavy-volume candle.
- Price is near the ten-day moving average, ideally close enough that support and stop can be defined.
- Moving averages are still in an upward structure: short and medium lines are not rolling into a clear downtrend.
- The stock has enough liquidity to enter late day and exit next morning.

Negative filters:

- Current bearish candle is also heavy volume.
- Price has already broken the ten-day line decisively.
- The ten-day, twenty-day, and thirty-day lines are turning down together.
- The stock is an obvious late-stage high-position collapse.
- The prior volume came from a one-day random spike with no follow-through.
- The trade requires holding because of a story rather than because of the rebound setup.

#### Operational Volume Rules

The transcript gives a visual rule, not a complete indicator formula. Use these as testable manual defaults, not as source-literal claims:

1. `爆量大阳`
   - Prefer a bullish candle whose volume is at least 1.8x to 2.5x the recent 20-day average volume, or visually a new volume platform.
   - The candle should mark meaningful participation, not only a tiny stock's accidental spike.

2. `分层量能`
   - The prior participation volume should look like a higher layer than the earlier quiet base.
   - The current pullback volume should fall back to a lower layer, ideally below 50% to 70% of the prior explosive candle's volume.

3. `缩量深跌`
   - A down day of roughly 4% to 7% with notably small volume can be ideal if the ten-day line remains structurally valid.
   - A large down day with expanding volume is a sell-pressure warning, not a buy signal.

4. `禁止爆量阴线`
   - If the pullback day is a large-volume bearish candle, assume possible distribution and skip.

#### Entry

Core entry:

- At around `14:50`, buy only if the stock is a shrinking-volume bearish pullback near the ten-day line and the moving-average structure is still upward.

Entry checklist:

1. Did the stock previously show a heavy-volume bullish participation candle?
2. Is today's candle bearish or weak, rather than a chase candle?
3. Is today's volume clearly smaller than the earlier participation volume?
4. Is price near the ten-day moving average and not meaningfully below it?
5. Are the ten-day, twenty-day, and thirty-day lines still supportive rather than downward?
6. Is it late enough in the session that intraday volume and price are mostly known?
7. Is position size small enough to obey tomorrow morning's exit?

Manual rule:

- If the setup cannot be written as `爆量大阳后缩量阴线回踩十日线`, skip.

#### Exit

Primary exit:

- Sell most or all of the position before or around `10:00` the next trading day unless the stock shows exceptional strength such as a clear limit-up continuation.

Sell signals before 10:00:

- Rebounds into the five-day moving average and cannot break through.
- Early spike turns down and breaks the intraday average / yellow line.
- Price falls below the intraday average and cannot reclaim it within about 15 minutes.
- Profit shrinks back to about 1.5% to 2% after an early spike and price breaks the intraday average.
- Low open below the ten-day line followed by direct selling pressure.

Stop-loss signals:

- Price breaks the ten-day line by around 3%.
- Open is weak, price sells off directly, and no quick recovery appears.
- The trade loses the defined support premise before the expected rebound window.

#### Position Management

Core idea:

- The system uses分仓 to let statistics work.

Rules:

- Treat each stock as one sample, not a destiny trade.
- Avoid oversized single-name concentration.
- If using a small account, reduce the number of positions only to the level where fees and attention remain manageable; do not turn the method into one-stock gambling.
- After a losing streak, reduce total exposure and check whether market ecology has changed.
- Do not add to a failed position to improve cost.

#### Loss Handling

After a loss, classify:

1. Did I buy too early instead of waiting until late session?
2. Did I buy a downtrend and call it a ten-day-line pullback?
3. Was the pullback actually heavy-volume distribution?
4. Did I ignore the ten-day-line stop?
5. Did I hold past 10:00 because I wanted a better price?
6. Did I concentrate too much in one stock?
7. Did the whole market stop rewarding morning rebounds?

Rule:

- A failed rebound is information. It is not an invitation to do T, average down, or become a medium-term holder.

### If/Then Trading Manual Entries

#### Buy Setup

1. IF a stock previously printed a clear heavy-volume bullish candle AND the current day is a low-volume bearish pullback, THEN it can enter the candidate pool.
2. IF price is near the ten-day moving average AND the medium moving averages remain upward, THEN the support premise is valid.
3. IF the signal appears before 14:40, THEN wait; do not front-run the late-session rule.
4. IF at 14:50 the stock is still near the ten-day line, still shrinking in volume, and not breaking down, THEN buy only one predefined unit.
5. IF the pullback candle is heavy-volume bearish, THEN skip.
6. IF price has broken the ten-day line decisively, THEN skip.

#### Next-Morning Sell

1. IF time reaches about 10:00 and the stock is not clearly limit-up or exceptionally strong, THEN exit according to plan.
2. IF morning price spikes and then breaks the intraday average, THEN sell and lock the remaining profit.
3. IF price rallies into the five-day moving average but cannot break through, THEN treat the rebound as failed and exit.
4. IF profit compresses back to roughly 1.5% to 2% after a spike and the intraday average breaks, THEN sell.

#### Gap-Down / Weak Open

1. IF price opens below the ten-day line and sells down directly, THEN exit mechanically.
2. IF price opens weak but does not hit hard stop, THEN observe up to about 15 minutes.
3. IF within 15 minutes price cannot reclaim the intraday average, THEN sell.
4. IF within 15 minutes price reclaims the intraday average and rises above the open, THEN it may be held briefly, but still under the morning-exit plan.
5. IF price breaks the ten-day line by about 3%, THEN forced stop-loss triggers.

### Beginner Checklist

Before buying:

1. Is this a trend pullback, not a downtrend bottom-fishing trade?
2. Did a prior heavy-volume bullish candle prove money once participated?
3. Is today's pullback volume truly smaller?
4. Is today's candle bearish / weak, not a chase candle?
5. Is price near the ten-day moving average?
6. Is it late-session enough to avoid guessing?
7. What is tomorrow morning's exit rule?
8. How much will I lose if the ten-day stop triggers?
9. Am I diversifying enough for this to be a sample-based method?
10. Am I entering because the checklist passed, or because I want action?

After selling:

1. Did I exit by 10:00 or invent a reason to wait?
2. Did the trade work because of the setup or because of luck?
3. Did I obey the intraday-average failure rule?
4. Did I treat a stop as information or as personal insult?
5. Did today's candidates as a group behave well or badly?

### Common Misuses

- Buying the first bearish candle without waiting for the ten-day-line area.
- Calling any drop a `缩量阴线` while ignoring trend damage.
- Ignoring sector or market collapse because the chart shape looks familiar.
- Using one or two winners as proof and increasing size too early.
- Concentrating in one stock while claiming to run a probability system.
- Holding past the planned morning window because of hope.
- Doing intraday T and destroying the method's simplicity.
- Treating the speaker's reported performance as an input to position size.
- Taking the provisional volume thresholds as magic constants instead of review parameters.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- Late-session entry discipline.
- Ten-day-line support filter.
- Prior heavy-volume / current low-volume comparison.
- Next-morning time-based exit.
- Intraday-average failure exit.
- Distributed sample-based position management.
- Loss classification checklist.

Hard to copy:

- Years of chart memory from reviewing hundreds of stocks per day.
- Fast judgment of which candidate is the strongest among many similar pullbacks.
- Emotional comfort with many small samples.
- The speaker's mathematical temperament and tolerance for mechanical repetition.
- Any proprietary indicator code mentioned but not fully disclosed in transcript.

### Relationship To Existing Frameworks

#### Trader Probability Discipline

Reinforced:

- This is a large-sample probability system, not a prediction system.
- Every entry and exit can be written as if/then rules.
- Position size must allow repeated execution through losses.

Added by Dahuzi:

- A concrete technical setup: heavy-volume bullish participation followed by low-volume ten-day-line bearish pullback.
- A concrete time window: 14:50 buy, next day around 10:00 sell.

Manual synthesis:

- Use Trader Probability for expectancy tracking and ruin-risk control.
- Use Dahuzi for one named setup to track as a repeatable sample.

#### Beijing Chaojia

Reinforced:

- Mechanical selling protects the trader from fantasy.
- 分仓 and survival boundaries matter more than heroic precision.
- Short holding windows require strong execution quality.

Difference:

- Beijing Chaojia buys visible consensus through limit-up / core recognition.
- Dahuzi buys controlled weakness after prior participation and seeks morning rebound.

Manual synthesis:

- Beijing is the consensus-confirmation route.
- Dahuzi is the pullback-rebound route.
- Both need predefined exits and size control.

#### Liangwange

Reinforced:

- Discipline is the floor.
- Shape and volume are the basic language.
- Failed short-term trades should not become averaging-down positions.

Added by Dahuzi:

- More concrete tactical criteria for shape and volume.
- More precise daily operating windows.

Potential tension:

- Liangwange emphasizes understanding why funds choose a stock.
- Dahuzi can become too chart-only if the trader ignores market or sector context.

#### Qianniuge

Reinforced:

- Volume tells whether money is present.
- Pullback buying only works when the underlying line still has life.
- Non-mainstream or logic-failed losses should not be averaged down.

Potential tension:

- Qianniuge starts from broad-market volume and capital source.
- Dahuzi states a much more bottom-up stock-shape approach and may ignore sector logic.

Manual synthesis:

- Use Qianniuge to decide whether the market is rewarding repair / rebound.
- Use Dahuzi only after the environment permits short-term rebound trades.

#### Chaogu Yangjia

Reinforced:

- Cost price should not control selling.
- Stable compounding beats stimulation.
- Market ecology should affect aggression.

Difference:

- Yangjia emphasizes crowd psychology, mainstream, and大众情人.
- Dahuzi emphasizes mechanical technical structure and next-morning repair.

Manual synthesis:

- If the Dahuzi setup is also in a recognized mainstream line, quality improves.
- If it is an isolated private favorite, size should be reduced or the trade skipped.

#### Limit-Up Certainty

Potential conflict:

- Limit-up logic pays for confirmation and consensus at the strongest price.
- Dahuzi logic buys controlled weakness and avoids追涨.

Manual synthesis:

- Do not mix entry logic.
- A board trade's failure rule is next-day premium failure.
- A Dahuzi trade's failure rule is ten-day-line / next-morning rebound failure.

### Review Questions

1. Did today's candidate have prior heavy-volume bullish participation?
2. Was today's pullback low-volume, or did hidden distribution appear?
3. Was price near the ten-day line or did I buy too high / too early?
4. Did I wait until late session before entering?
5. Was the moving-average structure still upward?
6. Did I buy a trend pullback or a falling knife?
7. What exact condition will make me sell before 10:00 tomorrow?
8. Did I obey the 10:00 exit, or did I negotiate?
9. Did I diversify enough for the method's probability logic?
10. Did market ecology support morning rebound trades today?
11. How did all candidates from the same scan behave the next morning?
12. Which loss category appeared: selection error, timing error, volume error, market error, or execution error?

### Future Distillation Targets

When future videos mention 大胡子, 十日线, 买阴卖阳, 尾盘买入, or similar rebound systems, look for:

- Exact formulas behind any self-built volume indicator.
- Whether he uses market index or sector filters despite saying he focuses on individual charts.
- The minimum liquidity / turnover requirement for candidates.
- How many positions are optimal for small accounts after costs.
- Whether the 14:50 and 10:00 rules have exceptions.
- Historical sample statistics: win rate, average win, average loss, max drawdown.
- How he changes the method during extreme bull, bear, and quant-driven markets.
- Examples of invalid patterns that look similar but should be skipped.

## 20. Dragon Leader Strategy Framework

## Dragon Leader Strategy Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `龙头战法精髓，值得反复观看十遍`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Sn4YvxWARw0`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Main speaker: not explicitly identified in NotebookLM transcript; likely the channel presenter.
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

龙头不是提前主观指定的股票，而是市场在热点板块中通过涨停梯队、二进三确认、换手承接、封板质量和赚钱效应共同选出来的最强合力载体。

### Core Principles

1. 短线本质是`强弱`与`合力`。
   - 龙头战法不是只看K线或指标，而是读开盘后最强资金流向。
   - 龙头必须获得全市场资金认可，而不是个别交易者主观看好。

2. 龙头是`选美`出来的。
   - 首板阶段通常只能发现候选者。
   - 龙头要经过板块内部优胜劣汰，不能靠盘前主观预设。

3. 首板看发现，二板看确定，三板出龙头相。
   - 首板：热点爆发，多股涨停，信息噪音很大。
   - 二板：筛掉一日游，进入龙头种子阶段。
   - 三板：开始汇聚市场目光，具备明显龙头相。

4. 四板、五板是分歧考验。
   - 板块内部开始分化。
   - 能通过换手胜出的，才可能从板块龙头晋级为市场总龙头。

5. 换手决定高位生命力。
   - 高位继续走强需要筹码不断上移。
   - 健康换手能消化低位获利盘，让新资金接力。

6. 缩量一致是隐患。
   - 缩量板看似强，但低位获利盘没有被充分换手。
   - 一旦分歧打开，容易出现踩踏式砸盘。

7. 情绪决定战法生死。
   - 市场没有赚钱效应时，龙头战法应该降频甚至休息。
   - 连板高度下降、封板率下降、次日无溢价时，强行做龙头容易被反杀。

8. 跟风股只适合隔日套利，不适合当龙头抱。
   - 跟风股通常是畏高资金的补涨选择。
   - 龙头一波动，跟风股可能更快跌停。

9. 仓位是主动权。
   - 即使看好，也不要全仓单只。
   - 留出仓位才能在盘中处理弱转强、卡位、补救和防守。

10. 只跟随，不主观判断。
    - 可以有盘前预期，但必须以集合竞价和盘中实际表现修正。
    - 市场不给确认，就空仓或降仓。

### Leader Confirmation Ladder

#### 首板: Discovery

Meaning:

- Hot theme may be emerging.
- Many stocks can board together.
- 龙头 cannot be confirmed yet.

Action:

- Classify theme and candidate pool.
- Avoid overconfidence.
- Record first-board strength,封板质量, and relation to sector.

Risk:

- Many first boards are one-day stories.
- Buying all first boards is noise trading.

#### 二板: Bridge / First Confirmation

Meaning:

- Candidate survives the first筛选.
- Funds start choosing from the first-board pool.
- The stock becomes a possible dragon seed.

Action:

- Compare same-sector candidates.
- Watch order of board,封板质量, volume, and whether it带动板块.
- 二进三 becomes the key follow-up setup.

Risk:

- A second board can still be a follower.
- It must be judged against sector梯队, not alone.

#### 三板: Dragon Appearance

Meaning:

- The stock begins to show `龙头相`.
- Market visibility rises sharply.
- It can attract attention beyond its own sector.

Action:

- Study whether it is the strongest among same-theme names.
- Watch whether money-making effect expands.
- This is where serious attention begins, but not permission for reckless size.

Risk:

- A three-board stock in a weak market can still fail quickly.
- Need broader market and theme support.

#### 四五板: Divergence And Promotion

Meaning:

- High-level divergence tests who truly owns market consensus.
- Healthy换手 can upgrade a sector leader into a broader market leader.

Action:

- Prefer high-level换手板 over pure缩量加速.
- Watch whether costs move upward and selling pressure is digested.
- If it opens weak or fails expectation, execute quickly.

Risk:

- 缩量加速 after several boards can hide huge profit-taking pressure.
- Once broken, downside can be violent.

### Key Concepts

#### 孙悟空 / 猪八戒 / 唐僧

The transcript uses a journey metaphor for sector梯队:

- `唐僧`: the faith/mission holder, corresponding to the true dragon or direction anchor.
- `孙悟空`: a card-positioning or path-clearing stock, often testing danger first and helping identify direction.
- `猪八戒/沙和尚`: assistant/follower stocks, validating sector heat but not necessarily carrying the main leadership.

Manual use:

- Do not mistake assistants for the true leader.
- A strong sector often has hierarchy: leader, card-position stock, assist stocks, and late followers.

#### 换手健康 vs 缩量一致

Healthy换手:

- Turnover is sufficient.
- Intraday path may look like small teeth or repeated digestion.
- New cost basis moves upward.
- Selling pressure is released while price remains strong.

Dangerous缩量:

- Looks powerful because nobody sells.
- Low-level holders keep large profit.
- If disagreement appears, there may be little承接.
- High-level缩量 after many boards can become a踩踏 setup.

#### 封板质量

Signals to inspect:

- Was the board decisive?
- Did it seal repeatedly or fail repeatedly?
- Was volume sufficient?
- Did sector peers respond?
- Did the board create next-day premium?

#### 市场共识 / 赚钱效应

The setup is stronger when:

- 连板高度 expands.
- Board traders receive next-day premium.
- Sector ladder is complete.
- Failed boards can repair.
- The leader drives followers rather than being isolated.

The setup weakens when:

- 连板高度 contracts.
- High-level names produce large loss effect.
- Next-day premiums vanish.
- Sector ladder breaks.
- Followers collapse when leader hesitates.

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Classify the day:

- `情绪上升`: height expands, premium improves, leaders repair, sector ladder complete.
- `情绪分歧`: leaders split, high-level换手 decides survival, position size must be controlled.
- `情绪退潮`: height falls, failed boards punish, leaders cannot continue, rest or reduce.

Rules:

- 龙头战法 depends on情绪 and赚钱效应.
- If the market only supports three boards and stops, do not force high-level continuation.
- When environment is wrong, low-frequency observation beats forced execution.

#### Sector Selection

Find:

- Strongest main theme.
- Highest board count inside that theme.
- Complete ladder: leader, second line, assist stocks, followers.
- Whether the theme is collective or just one isolated stock.

Avoid:

- Non-mainstream themes.
- A single stock with no sector response.
- Late followers after the leader has already exhausted height.

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- Second board or above.
- In the strongest theme.
- Has带动性 toward sector peers.
- Strong封板质量.
- Healthy换手 rather than blind缩量.
- Next-day premium is likely in current environment.

Negative filters:

- Pure follower with no主动性.
- Late補涨 after dragon height is already obvious.
- High-level缩量 with huge unexchanged profit.
- Weak封板, repeated炸板, no sector response.
- 龙头 has already weakened while follower is still being chased.

#### Entry

Preferred setups:

- 二进三 confirmation when the theme is strong and candidate shows dragon appearance.
- Weak-to-strong after a stock opens below expectation but then absorbs selling and turns strong.
- High-level换手 board that digests profit-taking and still seals decisively.

Avoid:

- Buying first boards and declaring them dragon too early.
- Buying followers because the true leader feels too high.
- Chasing when market emotion is already retreating.
- Full-position betting on a single confirmation.

#### Exit

Exit or reduce when:

- It should open high but opens weak.
- A缩量 high board opens and cannot reseal.
- Sector ladder breaks.
- 龙头 loses带动性.
- Next-day premium fails.
- Your position is not the true leader and leader weakens.

Principle:

- 不及预期即处理. Do not turn dragon tactics into passive holding.

#### Position Management

Transcript rule:

- For accounts under around 1M RMB, hold at most 3-5 names.
- Keep about 50% capital as tactical reserve to preserve initiative.

Manual adaptation:

- Beginners should be even more conservative.
- Do not all-in one high-level board.
- Reserve cash is not laziness; it is operational flexibility.

#### Daily Preparation And Review

Before open:

1. List all second-board and above stocks.
2. Classify each by sector/theme.
3. Mark current highest board height.
4. Identify main theme and complete ladder.
5. Prepare response plans for leader high-open, low-open, weak-to-strong, and failure.

After close:

1. Count board rate and failed-board rate.
2. Count next-day premiums.
3. Review whether leader drove the sector.
4. Identify card-position stocks and assist stocks.
5. Update tomorrow's high-level risk and low-level opportunities.

### Copyable Rules

1. Do not call a first board a dragon.
   - First board is discovery, not confirmation.

2. Focus on二进三.
   - The second-to-third-board transition is the key dragon confirmation gate.

3. Only trade the main theme.
   - Dragon tactics require audience and liquidity.

4. Prefer换手 over high-level缩量.
   - Healthy exchange is safer than hidden profit-pressure.

5. Avoid followers unless the plan is explicit next-day arbitrage.
   - Followers are not leaders.

6. Track height, board rate, and premium every day.
   - Dragon tactics are cycle-dependent.

7. Keep reserve capital.
   - Position flexibility is part of the system.

8. If expectation fails, exit quickly.
   - 龙头战法 cannot survive by hope.

### Beginner Checklist

Before buying:

1. Is this stock second board or above?
2. Is it in the strongest theme?
3. Is it leader, card-position, assist, or follower?
4. Has it shown带动性?
5. Is today's market emotion rising, diverging, or retreating?
6. Is the board healthy换手 or dangerous缩量?
7. What would count as不及预期 tomorrow?
8. Is my position small enough that I can exit without hesitation?
9. Am I buying because it is the leader, or because the leader is too high?
10. Do I still have cash for the next decision?

### Common Misuses

- Treating a first board as confirmed dragon.
- Buying a follower and holding it like a leader.
- Forcing dragon tactics during emotional retreat.
- Worshiping缩量 acceleration and ignoring trapped profit pressure.
- Ignoring sector ladder and only staring at one stock.
- Using full position because the stock "has dragon appearance."
- Missing the true leader, then buying a weak补涨 for comfort.
- Failing to prepare next-day plans before the open.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- Board-ladder classification.
- 二进三 focus.
- Main-theme filtering.
- High-level换手 preference.
- 50% reserve-capital principle.
- 不及预期 exit discipline.
- Daily board-rate and premium review.

Hard to copy:

- Instant sense of strongest market合力.
- Fast execution during weak-to-strong transitions.
- Distinguishing true card-position from noise in real time.
- Emotional tolerance for high-level leaders.
- Knowing when a leader has market-wide consensus rather than only sector heat.

### Relationship To Existing Frameworks

#### Beijing Chaojia

Reinforced:

- Recognition/core status matters.
- Risk control and mechanical exit are essential.
- Avoid random low-quality boards.

Difference:

- Beijing Chaojia seed framework emphasizes first-board probability distribution.
- This video says first board cannot confirm dragon and shifts attention to二板/三板 confirmation.

Manual synthesis:

- Use Beijing Chaojia for first-board risk control.
- Use this dragon framework when the task is to identify the emerging leader from the board ladder.

#### Yaotouge

Reinforced:

- Cycle and emotion come before individual stock.
- 二进三 is important.
- High-level leader behavior controls market ecology.
- Weak markets require rest or reduced size.

Added:

- More explicit ladder language: first board, second board, third board, fourth/fifth board.
- Strong emphasis on换手 versus缩量.
- Sector-role metaphor: leader, card-position, assist, follower.

#### Limit-Up Certainty Framework

Reinforced:

- Limit-up is a consensus event.
- Next-day premium is the key short-term edge.
- Board quality and market temperature matter.

Added:

- Limit-up alone is not enough; the board's location in the ladder matters.
- High-level boards require换手 health, not only封板 certainty.

### Trading Manual Entries

1. 龙头 is selected by market合力, not predicted by me.
2. 首板 is discovery; 二板 is筛选; 三板 shows龙头相; 四五板 test换手 and consensus.
3. Every day, classify all second-board and above names by sector ladder.
4. Only main-theme leaders deserve serious attention; followers are at most套利 objects.
5. Healthy换手 beats high-level缩量 acceleration.
6. If board height and next-day premium contract, dragon tactics must reduce or stop.
7. Keep reserve cash; 50% initiative is part of the system.
8. If a position is不及预期, handle it immediately.
9. Before the open, prepare plans for high-open, low-open, weak-to-strong, failure, and sector break.
10. Review whether the trade was leader logic, card-position logic, assist logic, or follower comfort-buying.

### Review Questions

1. What was today's highest board height?
2. Did board height expand or contract compared with yesterday?
3. What was today's board rate and failed-board rate?
4. Which theme had the most complete ladder?
5. Which stock was the true leader, and which were card-position or assist stocks?
6. Did the third-board candidate show dragon appearance?
7. Was the high-level board healthy换手 or dangerous缩量?
8. Did the leader drive the sector, or was it isolated?
9. Did yesterday's board trades receive premium today?
10. If tomorrow's leader opens weak, what is my plan for every related holding?

### Future Distillation Targets

When future videos discuss 龙头战法, look for:

- Concrete thresholds for turnover rate and封板质量.
- Exact weak-to-strong entry timing.
- How to rank multiple二进三 candidates.
- How to define and trade卡位股.
- How to handle high-level leader断板.
- Whether 50% reserve capital changes by account size.
- Specific next-day auction rules.

## 21. Huadong Da Daodan Limit-Up Growth Framework

- Source file: `/Users/cuiyong/.codex/skills/youtube-investor-distiller/references/huadong-da-daodan-limit-up-growth-framework.md`

## Huadong Da Daodan Limit-Up Growth Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `实盘炒股大赛冠军华东大导弹，分享悟道经历`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VDbNRbNhEec`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Core figure identified by NotebookLM transcript: `华东大导弹`, a professional short-term trader and real-money trading contest champion.
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

华东大导弹这条视频的核心不是“满仓单挑”的刺激故事，而是一个亏损者通过回溯交割单、砍掉 80% 垃圾交易、专精打板/扫板模式、建立市场审美和控手纪律，最终把小资金优势转成高弹性收益的成长框架；普通人可复制的是筛选、复盘和风控，不可直接复制高手的大仓位和临场速度。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. 能力重于本金。
   - If the trader has a real edge, small capital can compound.
   - If no edge exists, larger capital only increases damage.

2. 绝对禁止杠杆。
   - The transcript treats leverage, borrowed money, and pressure capital as the path to irreversible collapse.
   - Free capital is psychologically cleaner and easier to cut.

3. Use the `20/80` trade audit.
   - Roughly 20% of trades produce most profits; the remaining 80% often create noise, drawdown, and psychological debt.
   - The main work is deleting weak modes, not adding more tactics.

4. `等` is an edge.
   - Short-term trading is not constant action.
   - The trader waits for the market to present a familiar, high-quality setup.

5. Be a market follower.
   - The transcript praises being a `墙头草`: adapt to market choice, not personal opinion.
   - This aligns with hot-money logic: follow current attention, sector heat, and leader behavior.

6. `审美` means probabilistic quality, not personal taste.
   - A beautiful trade is one that historically has a high chance of making money for this trader.
   - It includes sector support, leader quality, visible money, and clean execution.

7. Physical self-control is a real rule.
   - When hesitation appears, remove hands from the keyboard and do not press confirm.
   - This turns vague discipline into a concrete interrupt.

8. Drawdown destroys compounding.
   - Losing 50% requires 100% to recover.
   - Avoiding catastrophic losses matters more than maximizing every good day.

9. Full-time trading is an earned state.
   - If a person cannot profit before going full time, changing the work environment will not magically create edge.
   - Professional conditions help only after the system and discipline already exist.

10. Professional tools are useful only after a system exists.
    - Computer, multi-screen, Level 2,涨跌速, and盘口 can improve execution.
    - They do not replace market understanding or review.

11. Family and life stability are risk-control assets.
    - Emotional and financial desperation leads to leverage, revenge, and one-shot thinking.
    - The trader must protect the final support system, not sacrifice it to the market.

12. The legend is not the method.
    - One-year tenfold and champion labels are evidence of a path, not trade instructions.
    - Extract process, not identity worship.

### Growth Path From Loss To Specialization

#### Stage 1: Random And Borrowed Conviction

Failure patterns:

- Following friends' stock tips or online claims.
- Believing external voices without a personal system.
- Confusing bull-market luck with skill.
- Taking trades that cannot be reviewed into a stable pattern.

Manual rule:

- Any trade that cannot be classified after the close is not a system trade.

#### Stage 2: Leverage And Recovery Thinking

Failure patterns:

- Adding leverage near a market top.
- Using borrowed, credit, family, or pressure money.
- Trying to win back losses in one trade.
- Treating a large loss as an insult to be avenged.

Manual rule:

- If the capital has repayment pressure or family-use pressure, it is banned from trading.

#### Stage 3: Indicator Worship

Failure patterns:

- Searching for formulas, special bars, AI stock-picking, and fixed technical signals.
- Mistaking descriptive tools for a profitable system.
- Changing indicators after each loss.

Manual rule:

- Indicators may describe candidates, but they do not replace sector heat, leader status, market phase, and trade audit.

#### Stage 4: Trade-Audit Breakthrough

Core shift:

- Review historical trades.
- Identify which mode actually made money.
- Identify which mode fits temperament.
- Delete the modes that caused most losses.

Transcript example:

- NotebookLM identified that he found打板 worked better for him while low吸 did not fit his personality.

Manual rule:

- Keep only the mode that has both reviewed positive contribution and emotional executability.

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Positive environment:

- Recent limit-up stocks are clustered in clear sectors.
- There are current hotspots among the top four or five market attention lines.
- Leaders show visible follow-through or strong board behavior.
- The market rewards acceleration and next-day premium.
- Sector stage still has upward动力 and空间.

Negative environment:

- No clear hot sector.
- Limit-up distribution is scattered.
- Leaders fail quickly or produce large negative feedback.
- Board trades have poor next-day premium.
- The trader is forcing trades because of boredom, recovery desire, or external opinion.

Manual classification:

1. `无审美`: no trade.
2. `观察期`: watch leaders, record boards, do not force.
3. `试错期`: small size only when sector and leader are credible.
4. `爆发期`: aggression only after market, setup, and personal execution record align.

#### Stock Filtering: The `审美` Checklist

Use `审美` as a scoring system, not a vague feeling.

Required dimensions:

1. `板块`
   - The stock belongs to one of the current high-attention sectors.
   - Sector peers confirm attention; it is not an isolated move.

2. `龙头 / 识别度`
   - The stock is a leader or credible leader candidate.
   - It can attract attention from active short-term traders.
   - It has带动性 or clear representative status in its line.

3. `市场阶段`
   - The sector still has upward momentum and room.
   - Avoid late exhaustion, chaotic rotation, and post-climax weakness.

4. `图形 / 量能`
   - Recent strength is supported by volume and recognizable price action.
   - The shape is clean enough to review later as a repeatable setup.

5. `盘口 / 涨速`
   - Level 2,涨跌速, and order flow show real money sweeping, not only a slow decorative rise.
   - The trader can see whether资金 is actively taking supply.

6. `个人模式匹配`
   - The trade belongs to the trader's proven mode, not a temporary imitation.
   - If hesitation appears, skip.

Negative filters:

- No sector support.
- Not a leader or credible core.
- Board appears in a weak or scattered market.
- Trader cannot explain why funds choose it.
- Trade is taken only because someone online mentioned it.
- Trade is taken to recover a previous loss.

#### Entry: Sweep Board Versus Queue Board

Transcript-confirmed tendency:

- The speaker prefers `扫板` over passively queueing when he sees money rapidly pushing upward and believes queueing may not fill.

Sweep-board conditions:

- Stock is in a supported hot sector.
- It has strong leader/core qualities.
- Money starts sweeping from about the mid-high intraday area toward the board.
- The stock is small or active enough that waiting in queue may miss the trade.
- The trader has already prepared the stock in review; it is not a surprise impulse.

Queue-board / wait conditions:

- The trader is unsure whether it is a true leader.
- Sector support is unclear.
- Board quality is not decisive.
- There is hesitation.
- Market temperature is weak.

Beginner guardrail:

- For a small account, `扫板` is banned unless the stock passes sector, leader, volume, board-quality, and next-day-premium checks.
- If the trader says "maybe" while entering, hands leave keyboard.

#### Exit And Stop

Transcript-confirmed:

- Control drawdown because large losses destroy compounding.
- Do not repeat known catastrophic errors such as天地板 experience.
- Follow the market and change view quickly when market feedback changes.
- No exact numerical stop was confirmed in the NotebookLM transcript output.

Manual inference:

- If the board fails to seal, the打板 thesis is impaired.
- If next-day premium fails and the stock opens/acts weak, handle quickly.
- If sector heat disappears, leader status is lost, or similar stocks produce negative feedback, reduce or exit.
- Use a personal numeric risk rule from the Trader Probability framework; do not invent confidence-based holding.

Manual rule:

- Failed board, failed premium, failed sector, or failed leader status means exit logic is active. Do not hold because of cost.

#### Position Management

Transcript warning:

- The speaker's full-position / single-leader behavior belongs to an expert context and is dangerous for ordinary users.

Small-account adapted ladder:

1. `空仓`
   - No clear hot sector.
   - No high-quality leader.
   - Recent trades show rule-breaking.
   - Equity curve is unstable.

2. `试错仓`
   - Setup appears but personal execution record is still unproven.
   - Use small fixed size and record the result.

3. `标准仓`
   - Setup belongs to the proven 20% mode.
   - Sector, leader, and board-quality checks pass.
   - Exit plan is written before entry.

4. `进攻仓`
   - Only after stable curve, clean execution, positive recent review, and strong market temperature.
   - Still no leverage, no borrowed money, no recovery trade.

Forbidden:

- Leverage, borrowed money, credit-card funds, family emergency funds.
- Increasing size after loss.
- Full-position trades before the mode has been audited.
- Concentration when the trader cannot execute the stop.

### 20% Advantage Trade Audit

Use this after every trading period, preferably weekly and monthly.

#### Required Fields

Record each trade with:

- Date.
- Stock.
- Sector / theme.
- Market temperature.
- Setup type:打板, 扫板, 排板, 低吸, 半路, other.
- Leader classification: leader, credible leader candidate, follower, noise.
- Entry reason.
- Exit reason.
- Profit/loss percent.
- Maximum adverse excursion.
- Whether the trade was planned.
- Whether hesitation occurred.
- Whether external opinion influenced the trade.
- Whether the trade passed审美 checklist.
- Whether the result was a valid system win/loss or behavior error.

#### Sorting Rules

1. Group trades by setup type.
2. Calculate total profit contribution by setup.
3. Calculate total drawdown contribution by setup.
4. Identify which setup produced most profits with tolerable drawdown.
5. Identify which setup repeatedly caused losses, hesitation, or revenge.
6. Delete the losing modes from the active plan for the next cycle.
7. Improve the one or two strongest modes instead of adding more.

Manual classifications:

- `优势交易`: planned,审美 passed, setup belongs to proven mode, loss if any is controlled.
- `垃圾交易`: unplanned, caused by boredom/recovery/external opinion, no sector or leader support, or belongs to a historically losing mode.
- `行为亏损`: result came from breaking rules.
- `系统亏损`: result came from a valid setup that failed within accepted risk.

### If/Then Trading Manual Entries

1. IF capital is borrowed, leveraged, or family-critical, THEN it cannot enter the account.
2. IF there is no clear hot sector or leader, THEN stay flat.
3. IF the stock is not in one of the current high-attention sectors, THEN skip the board.
4. IF a board trade has no sector support, THEN treat it as a random board and skip.
5. IF the stock is not a leader or credible leader candidate, THEN do not concentrate.
6. IF the setup is not one of the reviewed 20% advantage trades, THEN skip or use only tiny test size.
7. IF hesitation appears while entering, THEN hands leave keyboard and no confirmation is pressed.
8. IF a trade is taken because of a big V, news panic, or friend recommendation, THEN classify it as invalid unless it independently passes the system.
9. IF a large loss or天地板 occurs, THEN stop trading and review before any new position.
10. IF a board fails to seal or next-day premium fails, THEN exit according to the failed-thesis rule.
11. IF the trader wants to go full time, THEN first require stable profitability outside full-time conditions and a professional operating setup.
12. IF recent equity curve is unstable, THEN do not increase capital or size.

### Beginner Playbook

Copyable rules:

- Start from small money and fixed size.
- Use only free cash.
- Build a written list of allowed setups.
- Review all trades for 20/80 contribution.
- Watch recent limit-up distribution to learn market preference.
- Trade only when sector, leader, and market temperature align.
- Use physical interruption to stop hesitation trades.
- Treat cash as the default state.

Misuse risks:

- Copying full-position single-stock concentration.
- Treating扫板 as permission to chase any fast-rising stock.
- Calling subjective attraction `审美`.
- Using professional tools to overtrade faster.
- Mistaking a lucky leader trade for悟道.
- Going full time before proving profitability.
- Using leverage because the account is small.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- 20/80 trade audit.
- No leverage / no borrowed capital.
- Sector and leader checklist.
- Physical hands-off rule during hesitation.
- Small-account sizing ladder.
- Deleting historically losing modes.
- Reviewing recent limit-up distribution.

Hard to copy:

- Intraday speed at the board.
- Level 2 reading under pressure.
- Courage to buy true leaders without buying followers.
- Emotional resilience after deep drawdown.
- Knowing when full-position concentration is truly justified.
- The luck of catching a market-selected super leader.

### Relationship To Existing Frameworks

#### Limit-Up Certainty

Reinforced:

- 打板 is paying for visible market consensus.
- Board trades need sector, volume, recognition, and market temperature.
- A failed premium weakens the thesis.

Added by Huadong Da Daodan:

- Stronger emphasis on扫板 when money is actively pushing and queueing may not fill.
- More autobiographical warning about leverage, drawdown, and deleting weak modes.

Manual synthesis:

- Use Limit-Up Certainty to define what a valid board is.
- Use Huadong's framework to decide whether this board belongs to the trader's proven 20% mode.

#### Beijing Chaojia

Reinforced:

- Boundaries and risk control must wrap all short-term aggression.
- Core/recognition filtering matters.
- Mechanical exits and no-revenge behavior protect survival.

Potential tension:

- Beijing emphasizes average output and diversified practice.
- Huadong describes concentrated full-position aggression.

Manual synthesis:

- Beginners should use Beijing's sizing and negative filters as the safety wrapper.
- Treat Huadong's concentration as expert context, not default instruction.

#### Liangwange

Reinforced:

- A strategy must fit personality.
- Equity curve validates whether size can grow.
- Discipline and no revenge are foundational.

Added:

- A concrete 20/80 trade-audit path for finding the mode that fits the trader.
- The physical self-control rule: hands off keyboard.

#### Trader Probability Discipline

Reinforced:

- Positive expectancy must be discovered through records, not claimed.
- Position size must not destroy the ability to keep trading.
- A valid setup can lose; behavior errors must be separated from system losses.

Manual synthesis:

- Huadong supplies the autobiographical method discovery.
- Trader Probability supplies the math and ruin-risk guardrail.

#### Youzi Growth Common

Reinforced:

- Mainstream, leader, volume, and plan-first execution are common hot-money growth patterns.
- Small-account growth depends on specialization and review.
- Weak markets require rest.

Added:

- More explicit deletion of non-advantage modes.
- More direct leverage ban and professional-environment warning.

#### Chaogu Yangjia

Reinforced:

- Follow the market, not personal conviction.
- Public recognition and sector resonance matter.
- Heavy attack is justified only under exceptional risk/reward and market ecology.

Potential tension:

- Yangjia frames stable compounding as the professional target.
- Huadong's story can seduce beginners into chasing tenfold speed.

Manual synthesis:

- Use Yangjia's stable-compounding standard to restrain the emotional pull of this video.

### Review Questions

1. Which three to five sectors received the strongest attention today?
2. Which stock was the true leader, and did it drive peers?
3. Did my trade pass the审美 checklist or only feel exciting?
4. Was my entry planned before the board action, or created during the rush?
5. Did I sweep because money was truly sweeping, or because I feared missing out?
6. Did I use any leverage, borrowed funds, or pressure capital?
7. Did hesitation appear, and did I obey the hands-off rule?
8. Which setup type produced today's profit or loss?
9. Was the loss a system loss or behavior loss?
10. Which 20% of my trades produced most profits this month?
11. Which 80% created drawdown, distraction, or emotional debt?
12. Does my equity curve justify increasing size?
13. If I were not trying to recover money, would I still take this trade?
14. Did I confuse professional full-position aggression with my current skill level?
15. What exact evidence would make me exit tomorrow?

### Future Distillation Targets

When future videos mention 华东大导弹, contest champions, small-account tenfold stories, or扫板, look for:

- Specific next-day sell rules after successful or failed扫板.
- Exact board-quality criteria: order-book strength, turnover, seal amount, and timing.
- How he ranks leaders inside a hot sector.
- Whether full-position use depends on account size, market phase, or recent equity curve.
- Detailed examples of trades deleted by the 20/80 audit.
- How he distinguishes lucky super-leader capture from repeatable edge.
- Whether he has numerical drawdown limits before stopping.

## 22. Liangwange Discipline And Equity-Curve Framework

- Source file: `/Users/cuiyong/.codex/skills/youtube-investor-distiller/references/liangwange-discipline-curve-framework.md`

## Liangwange Discipline And Equity-Curve Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `良心#两万哥9月15日深夜再次连线大胡子`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qDbFrn52MHA`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Main speaker: `两万哥` (anonymous trader, per NotebookLM transcript)
- Counterparty/host: `大胡子`
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

两万哥的核心不是给一个神奇买点，而是强调交易者必须先建立纪律和逻辑一致性，再找到适合自己性格的模式，最后用收益曲线验证是否具备放大资金的资格。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. 纪律是地基。
   - 没有执行力和纪律，短期利润会被市场拿回去。
   - 纪律不是高级阶段才需要，而是初级、中级、高级都绕不开的生存条件。

2. 接受亏损和踏空，才可能稳定盈利。
   - 亏损和踏空不是失败，而是交易系统的日常成本。
   - 不能接受亏损的人，会陷入补仓、复仇交易和频繁换模式。

3. 仓位决定心态，心态反过来决定动作。
   - 仓位过大时，交易者会恐惧、犹豫、变形。
   - 不是先有强心态再上大仓位，而是先用小仓位训练能承受的行为质量。

4. `有道无数`和`有数无道`都不完整。
   - 只有抽象认知而没有具体方法，落不了地。
   - 只有技术指标、战法、模式而没有底层逻辑，迟早撞墙。
   - 稳定交易需要`道`和`数`互相支撑。

5. 顺势的本质是`跟随做多，理解做空`。
   - 两万哥把自己的系统概括为极端的追涨杀跌。
   - 这里的追涨杀跌不是情绪化追逐，而是承认资金流向和市场选择。

6. 收益曲线是唯一诚实的老师。
   - 曲线没有稳定向上前，不要加初始本金。
   - 健康曲线不是直线，而是小亏试错和大赚推进后的波动上升。

7. 复杂指标让位于`形态+量`。
   - 两万哥强调不看复杂技术指标，主要看形态和成交量。
   - 形态表达市场结构，量表达资金态度。

8. 短线亏损后严禁补仓摊平。
   - 短线的亏损往往意味着逻辑被市场否定。
   - 用补仓摊低成本，是把短线认错机制改造成被动持有。

9. 板块热度和情绪是个股爆发的土壤。
   - 个股强势通常来自板块刺激、热点情绪或龙头带动。
   - 只看个股形态而不理解资金为何选择它，容易把随机波动当逻辑。

10. 小资金阶段的价值在于心路训练。
    - 即使有资金后盾，也应从小资金开始。
    - 交易者必须亲自经历亏损、踏空、犹豫、反思、修正这一段路。

11. 高手匿名和反流量，本身就是重要信号。
    - 两万哥强调警惕 AI 假冒、收费社群、冒名直播等流量诈骗。
    - 真方法不在别人包装出来的人设里，而在自己的复盘和行为校正里。

### Trading System Skeleton

#### Development Path

1. `纪律阶段`
   - 先解决能不能按规则做。
   - 不报复交易，不乱补仓，不因为踏空而追。

2. `模式阶段`
   - 找到适合自己性格的模式，例如连板、半路、反弹、趋势等。
   - 模式必须让自己能执行，而不是看起来最赚钱。

3. `盈亏比阶段`
   - 在胜率基础上优化盈亏比。
   - 用小亏试水温，等真正机会出现时让利润覆盖试错成本。

4. `曲线验证阶段`
   - 用收益曲线验证模式是否可持续。
   - 曲线稳定之前，不谈扩大本金。

#### Market Ecology

Positive environment:

- 市场存在清晰资金方向。
- 板块热度能持续。
- 强势股背后有可解释的资金选择。
- 情绪和量能支持起爆。

Negative environment:

- 随机轮动，看不懂资金选择。
- 只靠个股单点异动。
- 情绪退潮但仍想强行赚钱。
- 自己看不懂涨停原因，却想参与。

#### Stock / Pattern Filtering

Positive filters:

- 能解释为什么资金今天选择它。
- 属于当下较强板块或情绪龙头。
- 形态健康，量能配合。
- 买点靠近起爆点，节省时间成本。
- 模式和自己的性格、执行能力匹配。

Negative filters:

- 涨停原因看不懂。
- 只因为别人说好或盘中涨得快。
- 形态和量能不支持。
- 自己已经情绪化、急着回本。
- 模式不熟却临时切换。

#### Entry

Core idea:

- Buy only when the logic is understandable, the capital direction is visible, and the pattern belongs to your trained model.
- The entry should save time cost by being close to the start of acceleration, not merely cheap.

Before entry:

- What is the board/sector/hotspot logic?
- What does volume say about money?
- Does this match my own model?
- If wrong tomorrow, where do I admit wrong?

#### Exit

Exit when:

- Original logic disappears.
- Market does not recognize the idea.
- Shape and volume no longer support the trade.
- The position becomes a passive hope trade.
- The trader is tempted to average down.

Important rule:

- A short-term loss is information. Do not turn it into a long-term position through补仓.

#### Position Management

Core idea:

- Position size should be earned by equity-curve stability, not by confidence in a single trade.

Rules:

- Use small money to train.
- Do not add capital when the curve is not stable.
- Do not increase position after emotional loss.
- Keep size below the level that causes fear and distorted execution.
- When environment is unclear, use small positions to test or stand aside.

#### Loss Handling

After a loss:

- Do not revenge trade.
- Do not force immediate recovery.
- Ask whether it was a logic error, selection error, timing error, cycle error, or execution error.
- If the loss came from mode drift, reduce size and return to the original model.
- If several losses appear in a row, protect behavior quality before trying to protect profit.

### Copyable Rules

1. `看不懂原因的涨停，与我无关。`
   - If you cannot explain why funds chose it, skip it.

2. `短线亏损不补仓。`
   - A failed short-term trade is an invalidated thesis, not a discount.

3. `曲线不稳定，不加本金。`
   - Equity curve comes before account size.

4. `形态+量优先于复杂指标。`
   - Use shape to read structure and volume to read money.

5. `亏损和踏空都是成本。`
   - Accept them explicitly so they do not become revenge behavior.

6. `模式必须适合性格。`
   - A strategy you cannot execute under stress is not your strategy.

7. `先小亏试水，再等大机会。`
   - The goal is not every trade winning; the goal is small controlled losses and occasional large wins.

8. `资金说了算。`
   - Respect market recognition instead of arguing with it.

### Beginner Checklist

Before trading:

1. Can I explain the stock's reason in one sentence?
2. Is this stock tied to a strong board, sector, or sentiment line?
3. Do shape and volume support the trade?
4. Is this inside my trained model, or am I changing style today?
5. What is the exact invalidation point?
6. Is my position small enough that I can execute the exit?
7. Am I trading to make money or to recover face after a loss?

After trading:

1. Did the trade follow logic or rely on luck?
2. Did I accept loss or try to average down?
3. Did I revenge trade?
4. Did I change models impulsively?
5. Did my equity curve justify my position size?

### Common Misuses

- Hearing `追涨杀跌` and turning it into emotional chasing.
- Using `形态+量` as a vague phrase without defining what shape and volume are valid.
- Saying `资金说了算` after the fact, but not identifying the资金选择 before entry.
- Treating a lucky涨停 as personal悟道.
- Adding capital after a few wins while the curve is still unstable.
- Calling every loss "试错" without reviewing why it happened.
- Refusing to sell because補仓 can lower the average cost.
- Consuming高手 content endlessly while avoiding personal复盘.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- No averaging down in short-term trades.
- Equity-curve validation before capital increase.
- One-sentence logic test before entry.
- Shape and volume as first-level filters.
- Loss classification review.
- Small-money training period.

Hard to copy:

- Instant market feel from years of experience.
- Emotional strength after large losses.
- Knowing when a pattern fits one's personality.
- Seeing why funds choose a stock before the crowd articulates it.
- Extreme depth of nightly reflection and persistence.

### Relationship To Yaotouge Framework

Reinforced:

- Both emphasize discipline and execution.
- Both reject impulsive half-way chasing.
- Both treat market recognition and资金流向 as more important than personal opinion.
- Both imply that weak environments require smaller size or no trade.

Added by Liangwange:

- Equity curve as the permission system for scaling capital.
- Explicit no-averaging-down rule for short-term trades.
- Stronger emphasis on psychology, self-reflection, and trading identity.
- The `道/数` distinction: tactics must be grounded in a deeper logic.

Potential tension:

- Yaotouge provides more concrete tactical filters such as 9:50, 三层仓, recent multi-board, top turnover names.
- Liangwange warns against over-worshiping tactics without understanding.
- For the trading manual, use Yaotouge for candidate selection and timing, and Liangwange for behavioral guardrails and capital-scaling permission.

### Anti-Scam And Source-Integrity Notes

The transcript warns against:

- AI-generated impersonations.
- Accounts using `两万哥`'s reputation to sell courses, groups, livestreams, or tips.
- Mistaking popularity or traffic for trading skill.

Manual rule:

- Do not pay for, follow, or copy any account claiming to be `两万哥` unless independently verified. Even then, treat all stock tips as untrusted until they pass your own system.

### Trading Manual Entries

1. Discipline is the floor. No discipline, no size.
2. If I cannot explain the logic, I do not trade it.
3. Shape and volume are the basic language of short-term funds.
4. Short-term loss means admit wrong; do not average down.
5. Loss and missed opportunity are normal costs, not insults to be avenged.
6. My model must fit my personality and execution speed.
7. Equity curve stability is required before adding capital.
8. A healthy curve is not a straight line; it is controlled drawdown plus larger opportunity capture.
9. Tactics without logic are `有数无道`; logic without executable tactics is `有道无数`.
10. Do not outsource judgment to online persona, AI clones, or paid groups.

### Review Questions

1. Was today's profit supported by logic, or was it luck?
2. Did I understand why funds chose this stock?
3. Was the trade inside my model?
4. Did shape and volume confirm the idea?
5. Did I trade after a loss because I wanted revenge?
6. Did I accept the loss or try to average down?
7. Did my position size create fear or distorted behavior?
8. Does my equity curve justify the amount of capital I am using?
9. Did I switch models because of impatience?
10. Did I do a real review, or only look at the chart and move on?

### Future Distillation Targets

When future videos mention 两万哥, look for:

- Concrete definitions of valid shape and valid volume.
- How he defines a suitable personal model.
- Whether he gives exact position-sizing thresholds.
- How he classifies trend, board, half-way, and rebound modes.
- How he identifies sector heat and emotional leaders.
- More specific equity-curve standards before capital scaling.
- Examples where he admits wrong and exits.
