## 28. Tuixuechaogu Xiaoming Lower-Episode Retracement And Position Framework

- Source file: `/Users/cuiyong/.codex/skills/youtube-investor-distiller/references/tuixuechaogu-xiaoming-lower-retracement-position-framework.md`

## Tuixuechaogu Xiaoming Lower-Episode Retracement And Position Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `退学炒股从四处借钱凑1万炒股到股市赚取千万，只因“我和小明”（下集）`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uSI7Lcga6-A`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- Video upload date from YouTube structured metadata: `2025-05-02T02:03:29-07:00`
- Duration from YouTube metadata: `1491` seconds
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed title, channel, duration, upload date, and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- YouTube page public caption tracks visible to browser metadata: `0`
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `Mastering the Inner Game of Stock Trading`.
- NotebookLM prompt status: two extraction rounds completed with transcript citations; a third validation prompt failed twice with NotebookLM's temporary "trouble responding" message, so validation below is synthesized from the successful rounds plus the existing upper-episode framework.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

下集把上集的`小明人格过滤器`继续推进成仓位和执行纪律：真正可复制的不是退学炒股的传奇收益，而是用动态`10%回撤线`决定全仓/分仓，用空仓等待确定性，用卖点保护主动权，用“操作对错”和“盈利结果”分离来压住小明的后悔、贪婪、牛市幻觉和乱买冲动。

### Relationship To The Upper Episode

Upper episode (`Vsan6X6DtBk`) emphasized:

- `小明` as the internal enemy: laziness, FOMO, blind dip-buying, post-loss capital injection, and emotional trading.
- No borrowing and no adding capital after losses.
- Two-account psychological separation.
- Expectation-gap management.
- Off-hours study and post-trade emotional reset.

This lower episode adds or strengthens:

- A dynamic `10%` retracement line as a position-mode switch.
- The psychological cost of full-position trading: anxiety, expectation, poor sleep, and adrenaline.
- A concrete definition of active empty position: watching the market with trading software open, ready to buy, but finding no valid target.
- The idea that bull-market thinking is poison for short-term traders.
- The rule that old holdings preserve sell-side optionality; selling before the system sell point just to buy a new target sacrifices initiative.
- A sharper distinction between `system-correct operation` and `market-given profit`.
- A warning that "certainty" is not a technical-indicator formula in this transcript.

### Confirmed Parameters And Limits

| Parameter | Context | Executable Meaning | Do Not Overextend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `10%` / `10个点` | NotebookLM extracted a dynamic retracement line based on the account's highest capital point. | When capital is above the retracement line, the trader may attack more aggressively; when capital touches the line, switch to defensive分仓. | Not a universal stop-loss percentage for each stock. |
| `资金最高点` | The retracement line moves with the account high-water mark. | Use the account curve, not one trade's emotional pain, to decide position mode. | The transcript does not provide a full formula beyond around 10% from the high point. |
| `回撤线之上` | State before breaching the line. | Full-position trading is allowed only when account state is still above the line and the setup is highly certain. | Not permission to full-position any exciting target. |
| `触及回撤线` | State after account decline reaches the dynamic line. | Must switch to分仓/defense to prevent large drawdown. | The transcript does not define how long to stay defensive after recovery. |
| `两个票` | Short-term execution capacity boundary. | Holding/trading two stocks is presented as manageable and useful for mental stability. | Not a recommendation to always hold exactly two stocks. |
| `半仓` / `坚决半仓` | Later-stage preference for calmer execution. | Use half-position or split-position when full-position pressure destroys objectivity. | The transcript does not define an exact half-position formula for every setup. |
| `涨停40多家` | Example of a market that appears active but may have few valid targets after filtering. | Count the quality of candidates after removing non-actionable names, not just total涨停 count. | Not a fixed breadth threshold. |
| `未开板次新` / `庄股` | Categories to remove from the apparent opportunity set. | Exclude names where the apparent strength is not realistically tradable or not cleanly determined. | The transcript does not give a full identification checklist. |
| `月赚30%` | Bull-market / expectation example mentioned in NotebookLM extraction. | Treat inflated return targets as a warning sign of牛市思维. | Not a target to pursue. |
| `年底翻倍` / `25万` / `5个多月` | Example of setting smaller reachable targets. | Use smaller goals to restrain urgent, chaotic trading. | Not a universal performance benchmark. |

Explicitly not provided by the transcript:

- No concrete stock-picking formula such as exact moving averages, volume ratio, turnover, or order-book thresholds.
- No exact sell-point formula, only the instruction to follow the system's pre-set sell point.
- No per-trade stop-loss percentage.
- No universal position percentages beyond full/half/split-position logic.
- No objective market-cycle threshold beyond examples such as filtering a day with many涨停 but few valid candidates.
- No specific stock recommendation.

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `控制内心是交易系统的前置条件`.
   - NotebookLM summarized the transcript as saying an uncontrolled mind is unlikely to succeed in the market.
   - This continues the upper episode's `小明人格过滤器`: first filter the trader, then filter stocks.

2. `确定性是买入的唯一标准, 不是打板或低吸的形式`.
   - The transcript does not tell the user to worship a technique label.
   - If打板/低吸/半路 are just forms, the executable rule is to wait for the most certain point inside one's system.

3. `空仓是主动能力, 不是躲懒`.
   - Executable empty position means watching the market with the trading interface ready, but not finding a valid target.
   - The psychological standard is that active空仓 should feel more comfortable than forcing a trade.

4. `分仓的第一功能是保护客观心态`.
   - The transcript treats full-position anxiety as a real execution cost.
   - Split positions reduce not only account risk but also expectation, insomnia, and adrenaline-driven decisions.

5. `动态10%回撤线是仓位模式开关`.
   - Above the line: offensive mode can exist, subject to setup certainty.
   - Touch the line: switch to分仓/defensive mode.
   - This turns a vague "control drawdown" instruction into an account-level circuit breaker.

6. `主动权高于新机会`.
   - If an existing holding has not reached the system sell point, selling it early to chase a new name sacrifices the right to sell at any time.
   - The new buy creates fresh T+1/passive exposure while the old position still had exit optionality.

7. `卖错后的盲目纠错是小明行为`.
   - Buying back just because a sold stock rises is not a new setup.
   - It must be evaluated as an entirely new trade; the previous ownership gives it no bonus.

8. `牛市思维是短线毒药`.
   - Bull-market thinking inflates expected return, makes traders overestimate market strength, and encourages random switching.
   - Short-term certainty is not automatically higher just because the index is bullish.

9. `操作只有对错, 盈利交给市场`.
   - The system judges whether the operation was correct.
   - Profit is an outcome assigned by the market, so a correct non-profitable trade may still be the lowest-risk action.

10. `大赚后比亏损后更要警醒`.
    - The transcript highlights post-profit confidence inflation.
    - After rapid gains, the next risk is lower standards, random buying, and giving profit back.

11. `格局不是失败借口`.
    - The transcript links large achievement to格局, but also warns against using feelings or情怀 to excuse losses.
    - Practical meaning: accept the wrong trade, cut illusion, and keep responsibility.

12. `了解自己比了解别人更能防亏`.
    - Knowing others can help find how money is made.
    - Knowing one's own weakness tells where money is lost.

### Trading Rule Table

| Rule | Trigger | Allowed Action | Forbidden Action | Parameter | Failure Signal |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Dynamic retracement switch | Account is above or touches high-water retracement line | Above line: may attack; at line: switch to分仓/defense | Keep full-position after reaching the defensive line | Around `10%` from account high | Drawdown mode ignored because the trader wants to win back quickly |
| Full-position permission | Setup is extremely certain and account is above retracement line | Full-position only when both account state and setup quality pass | Full-position because of greed, comeback desire, or "I want to爆发" | No exact setup formula provided | Sleep/anxiety/adrenaline become part of the decision |
| Half/split-position sanity | Certainty is high but not top-tier, or full-position pressure affects objectivity | Use half-position/split-position; two stocks can be manageable | Treat分仓 as permission to buy weak names | `两个票`, `半仓` mentioned; exact ratios not given | Both positions are impulsive, so分仓 becomes disguised overtrading |
| Active empty-position rule | No candidate remains after filtering the apparent market opportunities | Keep watching, software ready, no trade | Buy just because the market has many涨停 or others are making money | Example: `涨停40多家`, remove未开板次新/庄股 | Empty position feels like deprivation rather than strength |
| No withdrawal-rebuy rule | A target requires repeated order cancellation / chasing to get filled | Skip the trade | Repeatedly撤单重买 because "it must be bought" | No time/price formula | The entry process itself proves the point is not clean |
| No blind correction rule | Sold stock rises after sale | Reassess from zero as a new setup | Buy back only because it was previously sold | No numeric parameter | The trade reason is regret rather than current certainty |
| Sell-point initiative rule | Existing holding has not reached system sell point, while a new target appears | Keep existing holding until its sell point, unless system says otherwise | Sell old target early just to chase new target | Sell-point formula not provided | Trader gives up a position with exit optionality for a new passive position |
| Post-profit warning rule | Capital grows quickly or confidence inflates | Tighten standards, consider shrinking risk, review objectively | Random buying, inflated return expectation, "I am enlightened" behavior | No fixed cooling-off period | Profit turns into loosened discipline |
| Bull-market poison rule | Trader starts thinking "牛市来了" and raises return expectations | Evaluate each setup by short-term certainty | Monthly-return fantasy, over-switching, lower standards | Example `月赚30%`; no cycle formula | Market label replaces setup quality |
| Operation/result separation | Reviewing any trade | Judge execution by system compliance | Call a correct loss "wrong" or a lucky violation "right" | No numeric parameter | Review becomes P/L worship |
| Xiaoming pre-trade filter | Urge arises from envy, regret, FOMO, fear, laziness, or self-proof | Stop and label the impulse | Let emotion write the trading logic | No numeric parameter | "Certainty" becomes a feeling |
| Family/responsibility boundary | Trading pressure threatens family responsibility or debt risk | Reduce risk, do not drag family into the loss | Borrow, leave debt, gamble for redemption | No new debt amount provided | Hero story is used to justify dangerous behavior |

### Execution System

#### Market View

- The market can show a false appearance of prosperity.
- A day with many涨停 may still offer few tradable, high-certainty targets after filtering out未开板次新 and庄股.
- Future stock supply and capital diversion can make old潜伏 modes less effective.
- Short-term trading should focus more on strong names when strength is truly concentrated, but strength must be clean and tradable.

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- The target is inside one's pre-defined competence circle.
- The target appears at a known, system-defined point.
- The opportunity remains valid after filtering out non-actionable strength.
- The trader can explain the setup without regret, envy, or bull-market fantasy.

Negative filters:

- The only reason is that the stock was sold earlier and then rose.
- The order requires repeated撤单重买.
- The stock is bought because the day "looks hot".
- The trader is trying to convert anxiety into action.
- The target is a weak name being fantasized into a strong one.

#### Entry

Supported by the transcript:

- Buy only at the highest-certainty point inside the system.
- If no such point exists, stay actively empty.
- If the order process itself becomes messy, skip.
- Do not equate打板/低吸 labels with certainty.

Not provided:

- Specific intraday time window.
- Specific price threshold.
- Specific volume threshold.
- Specific board-seal formula.
- Specific industry or theme checklist.

#### Exit

Supported by the transcript:

- Follow the system's pre-set sell point.
- Do not delay selling after the sell point because of hope.
- Do not sell before the sell point merely to chase a new target.
- Protect the initiative of an existing position until the system says otherwise.

Not provided:

- Exact sell signal formula.
- Exact stop-loss percentage.
- Exact take-profit percentage.

#### Position

Account-level mode:

1. Calculate/update the high-water account level.
2. Treat a retracement of around `10%` from that high as the defensive line.
3. Above the line: full-position is allowed only for extremely high-certainty setups.
4. At or below the line: switch to分仓/defensive execution.

Psychological mode:

- If full-position causes poor sleep, obsessive expectation, or adrenaline-driven thinking, use half/split-position.
- Two short-term positions are presented as manageable.
- Split-position should reduce emotional pressure, not increase the number of random trades.

#### Empty Position

Executable definition:

- Trading software open.
- Market being watched.
- Ready to place an order immediately.
- No stock passes the current system filter.

This is not:

- Avoiding the market because of fear.
- Closing the app to escape pressure.
- Buying a token position to relieve loneliness.

#### Loss And Profit Handling

After losses:

- Cut illusion.
- Reassess whether the trade was a Xiaoming trade.
- Do not blindly correct by buying back.
- Do not use loss pain to justify bigger size.

After profits:

- Treat confidence inflation as a danger signal.
- Tighten standards rather than loosening them.
- Avoid monthly-return fantasy and bull-market overconfidence.
- Keep judging operations by process, not recent P/L.

### Copyable Rules

1. `如果账户从最高点回撤约10%, 则从攻击模式切换到分仓防守; 否则也只有在高确定性点才允许攻击。`
2. `如果买点需要反复撤单重买, 则放弃; 否则只在清晰点位执行。`
3. `如果卖出的股票上涨, 则按新标的重新评估; 否则不得因为后悔而买回。`
4. `如果持仓未到系统卖点, 则保留主动权; 否则不要为了新机会提前卖旧股。`
5. `如果市场看似火热但过滤后无高确定标的, 则主动空仓; 否则只买系统内标的。`
6. `如果全仓影响睡眠和客观判断, 则半仓/分仓; 否则全仓也必须受回撤线和确定性双重约束。`
7. `如果大赚后自信心膨胀, 则提高警戒; 否则容易乱买回撤。`
8. `如果脑中出现牛市收益幻想, 则回到单笔确定性; 否则牛市标签会替代交易系统。`
9. `如果一笔交易按系统做对但没赚钱, 则记录为正确执行; 否则会被结果反向训练。`
10. `如果盈利来自违反系统的幸运交易, 则不能强化; 否则小明会把侥幸升级成规则。`
11. `如果交易动机来自羡慕、后悔、恐惧或证明自己, 则停止; 否则这不是确定性。`
12. `如果交易压力开始牵连家庭责任或债务风险, 则降风险; 否则传奇故事会变成危险借口。`

### Common Misuses

- Mistaking `10%回撤线` for a per-stock stop loss instead of an account-level position-mode line.
- Treating "above the retracement line" as permission to full-position every strong-looking target.
- Calling subjective confidence "certainty" when the transcript gives no objective technical formula.
- Thinking空仓 means not watching or not doing work.
- Buying a weak or messy target because the day has many涨停.
- Repeatedly撤单重买 and calling that decisiveness.
- Buying back a sold stock because of regret and calling it correction.
- Selling an old holding before the system sell point just to chase novelty.
- Treating bull-market atmosphere as a substitute for setup quality.
- Using split-position to justify buying two bad trades instead of one.
- Using "格局" or情怀 to excuse failure to cut illusion.
- Treating a lucky profitable violation as proof that the system should be loosened.

### Copyable vs Non-Copyable

Copyable:

- Account-level retracement line.
- Switching from offensive to defensive position mode after drawdown.
- Active empty-position definition.
- The no-withdrawal-rebuy rule.
- The old-position initiative rule.
- Separating process correctness from market profit.
- Post-profit self-warning.
- Xiaoming pre-trade impulse labeling.

Not directly copyable:

- The legendary path from borrowed small capital to huge wealth.
- Any personal tolerance for full-position pressure.
- The exact intuitive judgment of "maximum certainty."
- The speaker's personal格局, emotional constitution, and experience base.
- The specific historical market context in which the examples occurred.

### Integration With Upper-Episode Framework

The combined `我和小明` framework now has four layers:

1. `Personality filter`: is this me executing a plan, or小明 seeking relief?
2. `Market filter`: after removing fake/non-actionable strength, is there a truly valid target?
3. `Position filter`: is the account above the dynamic retracement line, and can the mind stay objective?
4. `Review filter`: was the operation system-correct, regardless of immediate P/L?

The lower episode resolves one previous open question:

- Upper episode had `2个账户` as psychological separation. Lower episode adds a more explicit account-level retracement switch and two-stock/half-position mental-stability logic.

The lower episode does not resolve:

- Exact objective selection criteria for the highest-certainty target.
- Exact sell-point formula.
- Exact technical thresholds for identifying庄股 or non-actionable strength.

### Review Questions

1. 今天的买点是不是系统里最确定的点，还是我主观觉得确定？
2. 我是否因为市场有很多涨停就降低了标准？
3. 过滤掉未开板次新、庄股、不可交易强势后，还剩几个真正可做标的？
4. 我是否处在主动空仓状态：看盘、可下单、但无合格标的？
5. 我今天有没有为了买到某股反复撤单重买？
6. 我是否因为卖飞而把旧股票加了感情分？
7. 我是否为了新机会提前卖掉了没到卖点的旧持仓？
8. 我的账户距离最高点回撤线还有多少？现在应该攻击还是防守？
9. 全仓是否影响了睡眠、期待、焦虑或客观判断？
10. 分仓是否真的降低压力，还是让我买了两个不该买的标的？
11. 我是否因为牛市想象提高了月收益预期？
12. 今天我复盘的是操作对错，还是只看赚没赚钱？
13. 大赚后我的标准有没有变松？
14. 这笔交易是我在执行系统，还是小明在证明自己？

### Future Distillation Targets

- Search future退学炒股 material for concrete definitions of "maximum certainty."
- Verify whether `10%回撤线` appears elsewhere as a stable rule or only this transcript's example.
- Build a combined upper/lower `我和小明` one-page pre-trade checklist.
- Compare this dynamic retracement rule with两万哥收益曲线纪律 and Dahuzi本金/空仓 discipline.

## Tuixuechaogu Xiaoming Psychology Two-Account Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `退学炒股从四处借钱凑1万炒股到股市赚取千万，只因“我和小明”（上集）`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Vsan6X6DtBk`
- Channel shown by YouTube metadata / NotebookLM transcript: `A股作手`
- Video upload date from YouTube structured metadata: `2025-04-29T01:32:45-07:00`
- Duration from YouTube metadata: about `1155-1156` seconds
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-20 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: this analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection. The YouTube page metadata exposed title, channel, duration, upload date, and description, but no public caption tracks were visible through the page object.
- NotebookLM import status: successful `1 source`; notebook title: `The Trader's Mind: Master Your Spirit to Conquer the Market`.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频的核心不是“1万到千万”的传奇，而是把失败交易人格化为`小明`：懒惰、不复盘、盲目低吸、踏空乱买、盈利后膨胀、亏损后加钱，都要被识别为“小明指令”；可复制部分是用双账户分仓、预期落差管理、模式纯化、盘外学习和情绪清零来压制这个内在敌人。

### Confirmed Parameters And Limits

These parameters were extracted through NotebookLM's transcript analysis. Treat them as context, not universal formulas.

| Parameter | Context | Executable Meaning | Do Not Overextend |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `1万` | 退学炒股在破产后东拼西凑的重启本金 | 极端小资金起点说明资金弹性和心态压力。 | 不是建议借钱拿1万入市。 |
| `3000元` | 当时可选择工作的月薪参照 | 用于比较职业炒股的机会成本。 | 不是交易目标或收益标准。 |
| `月赚30%` | 以1万元本金对照3000元月薪推导出的个人职业化目标 | 说明他当时为什么选择继续交易。 | 不是普通交易者应追求的月收益率。 |
| `千万` | 视频标题/叙事中的最终结果规模 | 是长期复利和个人经历的结果。 | 不是短期目标，也不是可直接复制的收益承诺。 |
| `2个账户` | 文稿明确提到的分仓操作方式 | 物理隔离资金，降低单账户回撤和空仓焦虑。 | 没给出两个账户之间的具体资金比例。 |
| `100股` | 描述“哪怕买100股也要持仓”的空仓焦虑 | 用来识别无法空仓的心理强迫。 | 不是建议用100股试仓。 |
| `90%亏损` | 极端亏损麻木案例 | 说明缓慢亏损会让人逐渐适应痛苦。 | 不是止损阈值。 |
| `100%心理概率` | 错过目标股大涨后主观认为“它本该必涨” | 解释痛苦来自预期落差。 | 不是客观胜率。 |
| `4个多小时` | A股每日交易时长 | 盘中只是职业交易的一部分，盘外才是主要积累时间。 | 不代表只看盘4小时就够。 |
| `4小时睡眠` | 达芬奇分段睡眠法示例 | 作为利用失眠/盘外时间思考学习的例子。 | 不建议机械缩短睡眠。 |
| `1年` | 普通人掌握基础技术点的大致说法 | 基础技术不是最大瓶颈，执行和性格才是。 | 不是悟道时间表。 |
| `3年多` | 作者前期碌碌无为的摸索时长 | 说明长期原地打转通常是性格/纪律问题。 | 不是成长必经时长。 |

Explicitly not provided by the transcript:

- No fixed stop-loss percentage such as `-3%`, `-5%`, or `-10%`.
- No specific stock code or stock recommendation.
- No fixed position ratio such as `50%` or `1/3` for each account.
- No fixed buy-price formula or technical-indicator parameter.
- No fixed take-profit level.

### Highest-Value Principles

1. `小明是内在敌人的名字`.
   - 小明代表懒惰、随手交易、没主见、踏空冲动、赌性和不复盘。
   - 把错误人格化后，交易者更容易在冲动发生时识别它。

2. `传奇不能复制, 行为约束可以复制`.
   - 借钱凑1万、最终做到千万，是个人极端路径。
   - 可复制的是他如何对抗性格缺陷，而不是复制借钱重启。

3. `低吸被列为小明式诱惑`.
   - 文稿把盲目低吸归入“小明”喜欢的亏损模式。
   - 重点不是否定所有低吸，而是反对为了空间和幻想做不确定买点。

4. `打板是模式纯化的载体`.
   - 视频强调主推打板和“懂的点”。
   - 可执行含义是只做自己有确认点的模式，放弃模糊诱惑。

5. `两账户分仓有心理功能`.
   - 分仓不仅降低单票/单账户回撤，也缓解“必须持仓”的空仓焦虑。
   - 它让交易者有结构地接受没机会时不动。

6. `痛苦来自现实与期望的落差`.
   - NotebookLM最终校验显示，文字稿更准确地讲的是“落差”，不应硬写成数学公式。
   - 当主观成功概率被抬到100%，现实不兑现时痛苦最深。

7. `大盘差时先调低预期`.
   - 市场环境恶劣时，主动把赚钱预期降到很低甚至为零。
   - 这不是消极，而是保护心态不被落差击穿。

8. `借钱短线是禁令`.
   - 债务压力会让短线动作变形。
   - 盈利后也可能因为“我证明了自己”而继续加杠杆或追加资金。

9. `亏损后不能加钱`.
   - 大亏或连败后，正确动作是反思逻辑和降频。
   - 加钱常常是在逃避“我错了”的事实。

10. `短线买卖逻辑必须一致`.
    - 短线被套后不能改口成中线。
    - 中线标的也不能因为一天波动就短线化处理。

11. `职业化不是多交易`.
    - 成为职业交易者后，时间更多不等于出手更多。
    - 盘外复盘、政策、行业、金融理论学习才是职业化的核心。

12. `每笔交易后情绪清零`.
    - 盈利后的膨胀和亏损后的沮丧都会污染下一笔交易。
    - 目标是以平淡状态进入下一次判断。

### Trading Rule Table

| Rule | Trigger | Allowed Action | Forbidden Action | Failure Signal | Common Misuse |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Xiaoming rejection rule | 想偷懒、不复盘、随手低吸、踏空乱买 | 标记为“小明指令”，停止执行 | 给冲动找逻辑包装 | 交易理由来自情绪而非计划 | 把“小明”当口号，不做实际拦截 |
| Mode-purity rule | 计划交易前 | 只做自己懂的确认点/模式 | 低吸、半路、打板来回切换 | 买点无法写清楚 | 把所有涨停相关动作都叫打板 |
| Two-account rule | 空仓焦虑或单账户波动影响心态 | 用两个账户物理分仓 | 满仓一把梭或靠100股缓解焦虑 | 两账户也被同时乱买 | 以为分账户等于风控完成 |
| Expectation-gap rule | 市场暴跌或错过大涨股 | 主动调低心理预期，接受现实 | 把错过后的“本该涨”当事实 | 主观概率被拉到100% | 把落差理论硬改成数学公式 |
| No-borrowing rule | 想靠借钱翻身或加速 | 拒绝借钱做短线 | 负债操作、配资、借钱加仓 | 压力影响买卖 | 把退学炒股的1万起步误读为借钱推荐 |
| Loss response rule | 大亏、连败、账户失控 | 停手、复盘、降频 | 加钱、借钱、摊平、急着回本 | 找不到新验证方向 | 认为亏损只是运气不好 |
| Logic consistency rule | 持仓浮亏或剧烈波动 | 按原买入逻辑处理 | 短线被套改中线，中线震荡改短线 | 买卖理由不一致 | 用“格局”掩盖止损失败 |
| Off-hours work rule | 收盘后或假期 | 学政策、行业、经济金融理论，复盘标的 | 发呆、娱乐化耗时间 | 次日无计划 | 以为职业交易只等于全天看盘 |
| Emotional reset rule | 每笔交易结束后 | 清零兴奋/懊恼再判断下一笔 | 带着上一笔盈亏情绪继续做 | 盈利后放宽标准，亏损后急躁 | 把情绪清零理解成不复盘 |
| No-target no-trade rule | 市场火爆但无符合逻辑标的 | 空仓或等待 | 因踏空焦虑随机买 | 买入不在复盘计划内 | 认为别人赚钱就必须参与 |
| Pain review rule | 情绪痛苦明显时 | 问：现实与期望哪里错配？ | 把痛苦转成赌性 | 期望值持续失真 | 用心理安慰代替交易修正 |
| Legendary-story filter | 听到1万到千万、改写命运等叙事 | 只提取规则，不复制经历 | 模仿借钱、辞职、少睡、极端路径 | 行为风险上升 | 把励志故事当操作系统 |

### Trading System

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- Belongs to a pre-defined mode and a known confirmation point.
- Comes from prior review, not intraday impulse.
- Can be explained without using hope, envy, or fear of missing out.
- Matches the trader's own competence circle.

Negative filters:

- Bought because the market is hot and others are making money.
- Bought because it has fallen and feels cheap.
- Bought because the trader wants to hold at least `100股` to feel involved.
- Bought only to recover a prior loss.

#### Entry

The transcript supports only high-level entry discipline:

- Prefer the speaker's purified打板 mode / known point.
- Do not act before the point appears.
- Do not use low吸 as a vague replacement for a clear setup.
- Do not borrow or add capital to force opportunity.

It does not provide:

- A specific board-time filter.
- A specific price.
- A specific volume threshold.
- A detailed order-placement formula.

#### Exit

Exit discipline is framed as logic consistency:

- If the reason for buying was short-term, do not turn it into medium-term holding after a loss.
- If the reason for buying was medium-term, do not sell only because of one short-term fluctuation.
- If the theory is wrong, admit it and cut.
- Do not wait for imaginary big money to rescue the position.

#### Position

The strongest position lesson is psychological separation:

- Split capital into `2个账户`.
- Use the separation to lower pressure and reduce all-in behavior.
- The transcript does not define exact per-account percentages.
- Avoid the `100股` compulsive-holding behavior; that is a symptom of inability to stay empty.

#### Loss Handling

After a loss:

1. Do not add capital.
2. Do not borrow money.
3. Do not say it was just bad luck until reviewing the process.
4. Identify whether the trade was a Xiaoming trade.
5. Reduce frequency until a verified direction returns.

### Copyable Rules

1. `给每个冲动贴标签: 这是我, 还是小明?`
2. `只做自己懂的点, 点不到不出手。`
3. `不因踏空而买, 不因别人赚钱而买。`
4. `低吸如果只是贪便宜, 一律归入小明模式。`
5. `短线买入不能被套后改中线。`
6. `亏损后先复盘, 不加钱。`
7. `短线交易不借钱。`
8. `用两个账户做物理分仓, 不用满仓赌命。`
9. `空仓焦虑出现时, 不用100股安慰自己。`
10. `大盘差时主动降低赚钱预期。`
11. `收盘后研究政策、行业、经济金融理论和交割结果。`
12. `每笔交易结束后情绪清零, 再做下一笔。`

### Common Misuses

- Copying the `1万到千万` story and ignoring the anti-borrowing warning.
- Treating `月赚30%` as a target instead of a specific历史语境下的机会成本计算.
- Calling any two-account setup "risk control" while both accounts are still used impulsively.
- Using "小明" as a joke instead of a hard stop for bad behavior.
- Thinking打板 means chasing every涨停附近的票.
- Over-mathematizing the pain formula instead of reviewing expectation mismatch.
- Treating `4小时睡眠` as a productivity hack; it is not a trading rule.
- Confusing no-trade discipline with laziness.

### Comparison With Existing Frameworks

#### Versus Beijing Chaojia / Limit-Up Discipline

Adds:

- A psychological filter before the trade: identify whether the urge is a Xiaoming impulse.
- Less focus on board mechanics, more focus on whether the trader deserves to press the button.

#### Versus Huadong Da Daodan Small-Capital Growth

Adds:

- A clearer account of inner-personality management.
- Huadong emphasizes removing垃圾交易 and specializing in打板/扫板; this note explains how impulse and self-story sabotage that specialization.

#### Versus Liangwange Discipline Framework

Adds:

- A named internal enemy: Xiaoming.
- A two-account psychological separation method rather than only curve/discipline thinking.

#### Versus Dahuzi Mindset / Cash Rebuild

Adds:

- Dahuzi focuses on空仓,本金校准, and亏钱效应.
- This video focuses on expectation pain, personality labeling, and avoiding post-loss capital injection.

#### Versus Chen Xiaoqun Mainline Core

Adds:

- Chen Xiaoqun frameworks filter by主线/核心/容错率.
- This video filters the trader's own impulse before discussing which stock is core.

#### Versus Chaogu Yangjia Heart Method

Adds:

- Yangjia emphasizes大众情人,赚钱效应, and market consensus.
- This note adds a self-regulation mechanism: if the impulse comes from envy, laziness, or missing out, do not trade even if the market is hot.

### Review Questions

1. 这笔交易是我的计划，还是小明的冲动？
2. 我今天是否因为懒得复盘而临时买入？
3. 我是否把低吸当成贪便宜和幻想空间？
4. 这笔买入是否在我真正懂的确认点上？
5. 我是否因为别人赚钱而产生踏空焦虑？
6. 我是否为了缓解空仓焦虑而买了哪怕`100股`？
7. 我是否把短线亏损改口成中线持有？
8. 亏损后我第一反应是复盘，还是加钱/借钱/摊平？
9. 两个账户是否真的降低风险，还是同步乱买？
10. 当前痛苦来自市场事实，还是我把预期设得太高？
11. 收盘后我有没有研究政策、行业、经济金融理论和交易记录？
12. 我能否带着平淡情绪进入下一笔交易？

### Future Distillation Targets

- Distill the lower episode / follow-up if available, especially whether it adds concrete打板 execution rules.
- Compare `小明人格过滤器` with other trader self-control devices such as两万哥曲线纪律 and Dahuzi空仓红线.
- Build a checklist that separates `market setup valid` from `trader state valid`.
- Track whether future退学炒股 content confirms low吸 as a rejected mode or only rejects盲目低吸.

## 30. Yaotouge Seed Framework

- Source file: `/Users/cuiyong/.codex/skills/youtube-investor-distiller/references/yaotouge-framework.md`

## Yaotouge Seed Framework

This reference preserves the distilled framework from the YouTube video:

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=n9mSIRjIb68`
- Title identified from NotebookLM transcript: `大佬摇头哥连线大胡子 讲的干货比两万哥实在又细致`
- Main speaker: `摇头哥`
- Host/other participant: `大胡子`
- Core theme: A-share short-term trading cycle logic, three trading roles, and using market freeze-point transitions to find short-term opportunities.

Use this as a seed framework for future distillation. Treat it as trading-process research, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

摇头哥的框架是用高位龙头的赚钱/亏钱效应判断资金是`吸血`还是`回血`，再在周期切换点选择低位首板、二进三或高位龙头三类生态位，并用三层仓、条件单和次日早盘去弱留强来控制执行风险。

### Three Trading Roles

1. `低位首板`
   - Best when high-level leaders fail and capital begins leaving high-level names.
   - The opportunity is not that the low stock is already famous, but that market liquidity needs a new place to go.
   - Timing matters: in weak/uncertain periods, avoid impulsive early entries; wait for confirmation after about `9:50` or even afternoon seal confirmation.

2. `二进三 / 中位切换`
   - Best after old mid-level strong stocks complete broad补跌.
   - The key is not simply the second board itself; it is whether old-topic risk has cleared enough for new-topic capital to form.
   - In freeze-point markets, slower confirmation can be better than the fastest morning board.

3. `高位龙头`
   - Works only when the theme is logically large and persistent enough.
   - Requires exceptional tolerance because entries may happen at uncomfortable high levels.
   - It is less copyable for beginners because it depends on judgment of theme scale, time expectation, and crowd consensus.

### Market Cycle Logic

#### Absorbing vs Releasing Capital

- `吸血`: A high-level leader is so strong that it attracts market attention and liquidity away from other names. During this state, random low-level stocks often have poor odds.
- `回血`: When the high-level leader breaks down and produces loss effect, trapped capital and fresh speculative capital may rotate back to low-level opportunities.

#### Freeze-Point Transition

- A freeze point is not simply "the market is bad"; it is a point where loss effect has become obvious enough that capital may seek new direction.
- The opportunity often appears after high-level and mid-level risk has released, not while traders are still refusing to admit the old cycle is over.
- Use high-level leader behavior, recent multi-board performance, failed-board rate, and next-day repair strength to classify the state.

### Copyable Rules

#### Environment Rules

- If the highest market leader drops sharply or falls below roughly `-5%`, treat it as a possible cycle turning point.
- If old mid-level stocks are broadly补跌, pause old-topic接力 and watch for new-topic二进三.
- Use a `recent multi-board` indicator or equivalent watchlist to track whether money-making effect is expanding or loss effect is spreading.
- Do not judge market health only by index color; short-term traders should watch连板 ecology and high-level feedback.

#### Entry Rules

- Avoid half-way chasing around `5%-6%` just because of fear of missing out.
- Prefer limit-up confirmation or preplanned conditional orders to reduce emotional execution.
- For low-level first boards in uncertain markets, prefer entries after the earliest volatility window, often after about `9:50`.
- In freeze-point states, afternoon seal confirmation may be more valuable than being fastest in the morning.
- For二进三, require evidence that the old-cycle mid-level補跌 is mostly complete.

#### Exit Rules

- Next day, use the first few minutes as a strength check.
- If the position is weaker than the previous day, execute `去弱` quickly rather than hoping for an afternoon rescue.
- If an individual stock炸板 but its sector index remains strong, it may still have a next-day exit window.
- If both the stock and sector weaken, prioritize immediate exit.

#### Position Rules

- Use `三层仓`: a single stock should generally not exceed about one third of capital.
- First day can be diversified across 2-3 candidates in a theme or setup.
- Next day, sell weaker names and concentrate only into the strongest one when strength is confirmed.
- Do not use full-position single-stock bets as a beginner.

#### Execution Rules

- Use conditional orders when possible to reduce impulsive chasing.
- Build dynamic watchlists or filters for recent boards, main-board stocks, stocks that did not limit-up yesterday, and names matching the setup's moving-average or behavior profile.
- Keep the tool layer subordinate to the cycle judgment; filters find candidates, they do not define the market state.

### Beginner Misuse Risks

- Mistaking every high-level drop for a buy signal in low-level stocks. The signal needs loss-effect release plus new direction formation.
- Buying at `5%-6%` because "it may limit up" instead of waiting for confirmation.
- Treating `二进三` as a mechanical pattern while ignoring whether old mid-level risk has cleared.
- Over-learning the high-level leader strategy before building emotional tolerance and loss-control rules.
- Watching the index while ignoring连板赚钱效应.
- Using conditions/orders without a valid candidate pool.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- Classify market ecology through high-level leader behavior.
- Track recent multi-board and loss-effect indicators.
- Wait for post-`9:50` or afternoon confirmation in uncertain states.
- Avoid half-way chasing; use confirmation or conditional orders.
- Apply三层仓 and next-day去弱留强.
- Review sector index behavior when a position炸板.

Hard to copy:

- Judging whether a high-level theme is big enough to keep extending.
- Having the courage and timing to buy a high-level leader at 4-to-5 or similarly uncomfortable stages.
- Sensing in real time whether market capital is truly吸血 or回血.
- Selecting the best one from a large number of first-board candidates.
- Converting a broad macro/theme narrative into a practical short-term holding window.

### Trading Manual Entries

1. Before trading, decide current state: `高位吸血`, `高位崩塌回血`, `中位补跌`, `新题材试错`, or `主升`.
2. If high-level leader loss effect is severe, stop high-level接力 and scan low-level first-board opportunities.
3. If old mid-level stocks are still补跌, avoid new mid-level接力 until risk release is clearer.
4. In weak or freeze-point markets, do not rush before `9:50`; wait for stronger confirmation.
5. Do not buy half-way because of FOMO. Either use limit-up confirmation or stand aside.
6. Use三层仓: no single name above roughly 33% in normal conditions.
7. If holding multiple candidates, use next-day strength to `去弱留强`.
8. If next-day first five minutes are weak, exit weak names quickly.
9. If a stock炸板, check the sector index before deciding whether there is still an exit window.
10. Review every trade against cycle state first, individual stock logic second.

### Review Questions

1. Is the current high-level leader absorbing or releasing capital?
2. Did the highest leader produce a clear loss effect today?
3. Are old mid-level strong stocks still补跌?
4. Is the new candidate a true new direction or only an old-cycle tail?
5. Did I enter before `9:50` out of impatience?
6. Did I buy half-way instead of waiting for limit-up/confirmation?
7. Did I use三层仓, or did I over-concentrate?
8. Which position was strongest in the first five minutes the next day?
9. If a stock炸板, did the sector index support a repair/exit window?
10. Was this trade selected by cycle logic, or by seeing a stock rise?

### Addendum: Small-Account Limit-Up Model

Source video:

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xx1yPACxlWQ`
- Title identified from NotebookLM transcript: `摇头哥实名挑战北京炒家，分享实战干货|大胡子学炒股`
- Main speaker: `摇头哥`
- Core theme: Small accounts can compound through a disciplined limit-up model built around market strength, turnover leadership, popular-stock memory, post-9:50 confirmation, and next-day high exits.

#### Thesis

This video sharpens the execution side of 摇头哥's framework: do not read broad index color first; read whether the highest-turnover and most popular stocks are making money. When large-volume popular names show strength, small accounts can use limit-up confirmation and fast next-day exits to capture short-term premium.

#### Market Strength Rules

- Use the previous day's top five stocks by turnover as the market thermometer.
- If at least `3/5` of those high-turnover names continue to strengthen today, short-term money-making effect is probably active.
- If `4/5` are strong, the environment may justify more aggressive size, but only inside the defined model.
- Watch whether stocks with turnover above about `10B RMB` can continue to board or trend. Large-volume winners indicate big-money participation.
- Do not rely only on index red/green. A red index with strong high-turnover leaders can still support short-term trading; a green index with weak leaders can be deceptive.

#### Candidate Pool Rules

- Prefer popular stocks with memory:
  - Names with two consecutive limit-ups within the last 10 trading days.
  - Names with four consecutive limit-ups within the last 90 trading days.
- Prefer large-volume names when they are in the active direction.
- Build a dynamic watchlist for the top turnover names and historical popular names rather than manually staring at random K-lines.
- Avoid stocks that are only rising intraday without prior popularity, volume battlefield, or confirmed market attention.

#### Entry Rules

- Avoid the first 20 minutes of emotional surge. Early fast boards can carry heavy half-way chasing pressure.
- Use the `9:50 rule`: after about `9:50`, look for stocks that have digested early volatility, consolidated or washed, and then seal limit-up through market consensus.
- Prefer buying at limit-up confirmation to buying at `5%-6%` out of fear of missing out.
- Treat the seal as evidence of合力, not as a guarantee. The setup still requires market strength and candidate-pool quality.

#### Exit Rules

- This model earns next-day premium, not dreams of unlimited continuation.
- Next-day strength or pull-up is normally a sell window.
- Do not turn a premium-capture model into a long-hold fantasy.
- Fast selling may miss later gains, but it preserves turnover and reduces drawdown.

#### Position and Account-Growth Rules

- Small accounts should use flexibility and turnover as advantages.
- Push size only when environment and setup both confirm: high-turnover leaders strong, candidate has popular-stock memory, and entry happens after confirmation.
- In weak environments, shrink or skip. The transcript gives less detail on loss recovery, so do not infer revenge-size rules.
- The edge is not "small account can gamble harder"; it is "small account can enter/exit quickly without liquidity burden."

#### Beginner Misuse Risks

- Buying any stock after `9:50` and calling it the 9:50 rule.
- Treating turnover above `10B` as bullish by itself while ignoring whether large money is winning.
- Holding next-day winners too long and destroying the premium-capture logic.
- Confusing "popular-stock memory" with old exhausted topics.
- Using the model when the top turnover names are weak.
- Chasing early boards because of FOMO, which is exactly what the rule tries to avoid.

#### Trading Manual Entries

1. Before the open, list yesterday's top five by turnover.
2. During the day, classify how many of those five are strong. `3/5` is a basic permission signal; `4/5` is stronger.
3. Build a candidate pool from high-turnover names and popular-stock-memory names.
4. Wait until after about `9:50` unless the market is in an exceptional consensus state.
5. Do not buy at `5%-6%` just because it looks like it might board.
6. Prefer limit-up confirmation after early volatility has been digested.
7. The next-day plan is to sell into strength and protect premium.
8. If the environment thermometer fails, do not force the pattern.
9. Track whether the model's wins come from market strength or from random luck.
10. Keep the setup narrow enough that missed trades are acceptable.

#### Review Questions

1. What were yesterday's top five turnover stocks?
2. How many of those five strengthened today?
3. Did any `10B+` turnover name produce clear money-making effect?
4. Does my candidate have popular-stock memory: 10-day two-board or 90-day four-board history?
5. Did I enter after `9:50`, or did I chase the first emotional surge?
6. Did I buy at confirmation, or did I buy halfway from FOMO?
7. Was the board supported by broader hot-money direction?
8. Did I sell next-day strength according to the premium-capture plan?
9. Did I overstay because I fantasized about continuation?
10. If the top-turnover thermometer was weak, why did I trade at all?

### Future Distillation Targets

When adding future videos, look for:

- How 摇头哥 defines `冰点`.
- Specific filters behind the first-board candidate pool.
- Exact use of `最近多板` or equivalent indicators.
- Criteria for deciding old mid-level補跌 is complete.
- High-level leader theme-size evaluation.
- Whether the `三层仓` rule changes by account size or cycle.
- Concrete examples where he breaks or modifies these rules.

## 31. Youzi Growth Common Framework

## Youzi Growth Common Framework

This reference distills the YouTube video `中国游资成长经历语录心法｜｜炒股养家｜著名刺客｜退学炒股｜陈小群｜`.

- URL: `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=n4B35wq---k`
- Channel: `A股作手`
- Core figures identified by NotebookLM transcript: `著名刺客`, `小鳄鱼`, and other A-share hot-money / retail-trader representatives such as `炒股养家`, `退学炒股`, `陈小群`, `赵老哥`, and related names.
- Source access: imported into NotebookLM on 2026-06-19 via the user's logged-in browser state.
- NotebookLM limitation: the analysis is based on the YouTube text transcript imported by NotebookLM, not independent audio/video inspection.

This is an educational trading-method note, not investment advice.

### One-Sentence Thesis

这条视频的核心不是游资传奇，而是游资成长的共性：小资金要脱离随机交易，围绕`主流题材`、`市场合力`、`龙头股`、`成交量`和`复盘计划`建立超短系统；弱势空仓，强势只做核心，错了快速纠偏。

### Highest-Value Principles

1. 脱离主流，技术再漂亮也没有用。
   - KD、均线、数浪、形态都必须服从主流题材和市场合力。
   - 小资金做大的路径不是寻找冷门低估，而是参与全市场正在认可的方向。

2. 市场合力比单一主力更重要。
   - 短线真正的阻力和动力来自大量散户与活跃资金的共同选择。
   - 研究重点不是猜某个庄家，而是判断资金是否形成共识。

3. 成交量是短线行情的许可系统。
   - 有量代表资金愿意进场，有流动性才能承载短线接力。
   - 地量或缩量冷场时，强行交易通常是在降低胜率。

4. 弱势不操作是最高级操作。
   - 下跌通道和无量环境中，即使个股看似符合模式，也应降低或放弃。
   - 空仓不是错过机会，而是保留下一次主流机会的主动权。

5. 龙头通常在一轮下跌或风险释放后诞生。
   - 真正龙头不是随便涨起来的票，而是在市场转折点带动关联股的合力载体。
   - 龙头必须有带动性：它涨，板块跟；它弱，板块受影响。

6. 计划内操作优先于盘中灵感。
   - 静态选股和深度复盘能提高确定性。
   - 盘中随意追涨，即使偶尔赚钱，也会破坏系统。

7. 低吸核心股可能比追涨更稳定。
   - 追涨依赖临场直觉和速度。
   - 低吸龙头阴线、支撑位或二次启动点，更依赖复盘计划和理性判断。
   - This does not mean buying weak followers because they are cheap.

8. 大局观决定小买点是否有效。
   - 指数、题材、情绪、量能、消息和板块轮动要一起看。
   - 单个买点脱离大局，容易变成随机交易。

9. 小钱要博，大钱要稳。
   - 小资金的优势是进出灵活、学习反馈快。
   - 资金变大后，流动性、回撤控制和稳定性比单次暴利更重要。
   - Beginner adaptation: do not copy `天天满仓` before the market filter and personal discipline are proven.

10. 简单纯粹胜过模式堆叠。
    - 只做自己看得懂、能复盘、能重复的模式。
    - 放弃不属于自己的机会，是减少错误的核心。

11. 恐高症会让交易者买到跟风股。
    - 不敢买真龙头，转而买便宜跟风，是短线常见亏损路径。
    - 龙头贵但强，跟风便宜但弱；短线看的是合力强弱，不是价格舒适感。

12. 午间拉高和计划外冲动要谨慎。
    - NotebookLM answer noted the transcript warned against some lunch-time pull-ups because they can be诱多 or出货.
    - Manual adaptation: lunch-time or low-liquidity pull-ups need stronger confirmation, not automatic追涨.

### Core Figures And Copyable Methods

#### 著名刺客

Core mode:

- 龙头打板.
- 只做强势主流中的核心，强调最高价打板和强转强节点.

Copyable parts:

- Only participate when the stock is in the strongest mainstream direction.
- Watch `强转强`: a strong stock survives disagreement and returns to strength.
- Treat limit-up as market consensus, not as a magic signal.
- Use high turnover and broad recognition as proof of battlefield quality.

Hard to copy:

- The courage and speed to buy the true leader when most people feel it is too high.
- Intraday reflexes at critical board moments.
- Tolerance for high volatility after entry.

Beginner guardrail:

- Do not copy "only at highest price" before you can classify market emotion, theme strength, and leader identity.

#### 小鳄鱼

Core mode:

- 大局观下的主流热点交易.
- 看大做小: first understand index, theme, news catalyst, liquidity, and emotion; then choose the stock.

Copyable parts:

- Start from market environment and mainstream theme, not from isolated K-line beauty.
- Prefer simple and pure personal patterns.
- Keep trading psychologically comfortable enough to execute.
- Use market rhythm rather than forcing constant action.

Hard to copy:

- Cross-market and cross-cycle judgment.
- Knowing when a theme is becoming the main line rather than a one-day story.
- Maintaining calm after large gains or losses.

#### Other Youzi Growth Examples

The transcript also references other hot-money representatives and growth stories. Treat these as evidence of repeated patterns, not as role models to worship.

Repeated pattern:

- Early losses and disorder.
- Discovery that random technical trading does not scale.
- Shift toward mainstream themes, leading stocks, volume, and emotional cycle.
- Stronger review process.
- More rigid execution and quicker correction.

Manual use:

- Extract the process, not the legend.
- A trader's wealth number is not a trading rule.

### Trading System

#### Market Ecology

Positive environment:

- Market volume expands.
- Mainstream theme is clear.
- Hot stocks produce next-day premium.
- Leaders drive followers.
- Strong stocks repair after disagreement.
- Money-making effect appears in recognizable places.

Negative environment:

- Index or short-term ecology is in a downtrend.
- Volume shrinks.
- Leaders fail to create premium.
- Follow-up stocks collapse quickly.
- Rotation is chaotic and not anchored by a main theme.
- The trader cannot explain why funds would continue to buy.

Manual classification:

1. `强势主流`: active attack allowed inside core names.
2. `分歧检验`: only leaders or planned low吸; reduce random trades.
3. `弱势无量`: rest; cash is correct.
4. `转折试探`: watch first放量大阳线, second start, and leader带动性.

#### Theme Selection

Positive filters:

- The theme is easy for the market to understand.
- It has multiple stocks moving together.
- It has a clear leader or leader candidate.
- It is supported by volume, news, policy, or strong emotional memory.
- It can attract both retail attention and active capital.

Negative filters:

- One isolated stock with no板块效应.
- Obscure logic that requires long explanation.
- Old theme at exhaustion stage.
- Theme strength exists only in hindsight.
- The trader chooses it because the true main line feels too high.

#### Stock Filtering

Positive filters:

- 龙头 or credible leader candidate.
- Strongest stock in the strongest theme.
- High volume and liquidity.
- First large-volume positive candle at a relative low area, or second launch after digestion.
- Clear带动性 toward sector peers.
- Strong board quality or visible support at planned low吸 area.

Negative filters:

- Cheap follower bought because the leader is too high.
- Weak stock in a strong sector.
- Stock moving without volume.
- Lunch-time pull-up with weak context.
- A plan formed only after seeing intraday rise.
- A trade outside the prepared watchlist.

#### Entry

Allowed entry forms:

1. `龙头打板`
   - Use when market emotion, theme, and leader identity are clear.
   - The board must represent合力, not only a price reaching 10%.

2. `强转强`
   - Use when a strong stock absorbs disagreement and returns to strength.
   - Best when market ecology supports continuation.

3. `核心低吸 / DC`
   - Use when the target is a confirmed or highly credible leader.
   - Entry around planned negative-line support, second bottom, or second launch.
   - Do not low吸 weak followers.

4. `转折首根放量大阳线`
   - Use when a stock appears at a market or theme turning point and volume confirms new attention.
   - Requires sector and market context.

#### Exit

Exit or reduce when:

- The trade was not in the plan.
- Next-day behavior fails expectation.
- The stock loses leader status.
- Sector no longer supports it.
- Volume disappears or turns into distribution.
- Market enters weak/no-volume state.
- The position only survives because the trader hopes to recover.

Manual rule:

- A plan-break trade should be corrected quickly, even if it later might have worked.

#### Position Management

Principles:

- Position follows market volume and opportunity quality.
- Strong environment plus core target allows larger participation.
- Weak environment demands cash or minimal testing.
- Small account flexibility is an advantage only if exits can be executed.

Guardrails:

- `小钱要博` is not permission for beginners to all-in randomly.
- Before using larger size, the trader needs:
  - A stable reviewed model.
  - A clear environment thermometer.
  - A planned exit.
  - No revenge or recovery motive.

#### Review Process / Static Selection

Daily work:

- Review around 100 core or active stocks if possible.
- Classify main themes and leader candidates.
- Record first boards, second boards, high-volume names, and popular-stock memory.
- Identify whether market volume expands or contracts.
- Prepare next-day plans before the open.

Static selection checklist:

1. What is the current mainstream theme?
2. Which stocks are the first to strengthen?
3. Which leader actually drives the sector?
4. Which candidates are only followers?
5. Where are planned entries if the market confirms?
6. What behavior invalidates the plan?

### Copyable Rules

1. `先主流，后个股。`
   - If the stock is not tied to a mainstream theme, skip it.

2. `有量才有短线战场。`
   - If volume is shrinking and leaders have no premium, reduce or rest.

3. `只做龙头或核心，不买便宜跟风安慰自己。`
   - Fear of height should not push capital into weaker names.

4. `弱势不操作。`
   - Downtrend plus low volume means cash.

5. `计划内交易优先。`
   - Trades must come from review and static selection, not intraday impulse.

6. `低吸只低吸核心。`
   - Low吸 is a leader/support tactic, not a bargain-hunting habit.

7. `盘中随意交易，次日先纠错。`
   - Process violation must be punished before it becomes a habit.

8. `午间拉高不自动追。`
   - Require context, volume, and leader status.

9. `小资金靠灵活和复盘，不靠无脑满仓。`
   - Speed is useful only inside a validated model.

10. `大局观先于买点。`
    - A good technical entry in the wrong ecology is still a bad trade.

### Beginner Checklist

Before buying:

1. Is market volume expanding or shrinking?
2. Is there a clear mainstream theme?
3. Is this stock leader, credible leader candidate, follower, or noise?
4. Does the target drive its sector?
5. Is the entry from my static plan?
6. Am I buying because it is strong, or because the true strong stock feels too high?
7. Is this a打板, 强转强, 核心低吸, or转折放量 setup?
8. What exact behavior tomorrow would make me exit?
9. Is my position size earned by my system and current market, or by excitement?
10. Would I still take this trade if I could not watch the P&L for five minutes?

After trading:

1. Did I follow the prepared plan?
2. Did volume and theme support the result?
3. Did I buy a leader or a comfort follower?
4. Did I correct plan-break trades quickly?
5. Did I trade because of主流合力 or because of a random K-line?

### Common Misuses

- Treating every famous-trader quote as a rule without context.
- Using `主流题材` as a vague label for anything rising.
- Buying followers because the true leader feels expensive.
- Calling random intraday追涨 `强转强`.
- Low吸ing weak stocks and pretending it is leader DC.
- Copying `小资金满仓` without market filter, exit rule, or reviewed model.
- Turning static selection into a giant watchlist with no priority.
- Ignoring weak market ecology because a single stock looks strong.
- Over-trading lunch-time pull-ups.
- Worshiping游资 stories instead of reviewing one's own trades.

### Copyable vs Hard To Copy

Copyable:

- 主流题材 filter.
- Volume/environment thermometer.
- Leader/follower classification.
- Static selection and daily review.
- Plan-first entry discipline.
- Low吸 only on core names.
- Weak-market rest rule.
- Fast correction of plan-break trades.

Hard to copy:

- Intraday courage to buy true leaders near the highest price.
- The feel for when a strong stock is truly强转强.
- Large-size emotional tolerance.
- Early recognition of a theme before it becomes obvious.
- Knowing when to shift from aggressive small-account compounding to large-account stability.

### Relationship To Existing Frameworks

#### Dragon Leader Strategy

Reinforced:

- Leaders are selected by market合力.
- Followers are dangerous comfort buys.
- 龙头需要带动性 and sector ladder support.

Added:

- More explicit small-account growth language.
- Stronger emphasis on static selection and volume as permission.

- Use this framework to decide whether the whole market has volume and主流合力 to justify action.

#### Chaogu Yangjia Heart Method

Reinforced:

- 主流, 大众理解, 情绪, and赚钱效应 matter more than isolated technicals.
- Cost and ego should not control decisions.

Added:

- More emphasis on volume and static stock review.

Potential tension:

- This video's `小钱要博` can be misread as constant aggression.

#### Beijing Chaojia

- Recognizable core names and liquidity matter.
- Random boards and low-quality names are not worth copying.

Difference:

- This video focuses more on主流合力, leader courage, and static selection.

Manual synthesis:

- Use this framework for identifying the mainstream battlefield.

#### Yaotouge

Reinforced:

- Cycle and high-level feedback come before individual stock.
- 二进三, leaders, and money-making effect matter.
- Weak or uncertain states require later confirmation and lower impulse.

Added:

- More general游资成长 lesson: simplify, specialize, and repeat.

#### Liangwange

Reinforced:

- Discipline, plan execution, and no revenge behavior.
- Shape and volume are more important than complex indicators.
- A system must fit the trader's personality.

Potential tension:

- Liangwange warns that no stable equity curve means no capital increase.
- This video's small-account aggression should be allowed only after behavior discipline is proven.

Manual synthesis:

- Use Liangwange as the psychological and curve-validation guardrail.
- Use this framework as the opportunity-selection layer.

### Trading Manual Entries

1. Before trading, classify market volume: expanding, neutral, shrinking, or dead.
2. Identify the mainstream theme before selecting any stock.
3. Only trade leader, credible leader candidate, or planned core low吸; avoid comfort followers.
4. A stock without sector带动性 is not a true short-term core.
5. If the market is weak and volume is shrinking, rest.
6. All entries must belong to one of the named setups: 龙头打板, 强转强, 核心低吸/DC, or转折首根放量大阳线.
7. If a trade was not in the plan, correct it quickly regardless of current profit/loss.
8. Do not chase lunch-time pull-ups without clear market, theme, volume, and leader confirmation.
9. Small-account aggression is allowed only when environment, setup, and execution are all confirmed.
10. Review enough active names to know the battlefield, but trade only the few that match the system.

### Review Questions

1. Did market volume expand or shrink today?
2. What was the clearest mainstream theme?
3. Which stock was the true leader, and what did it drive?
4. Which stocks were only followers or comfort substitutes?
5. Did my trade come from static selection or intraday impulse?
6. Was my entry打板, 强转强, 核心低吸/DC, or转折放量?
7. Did I skip a true leader because of恐高 and buy a weaker stock?
8. Did any lunch-time pull-up tempt me into a low-quality trade?
9. If tomorrow the market weakens, what is my exit plan?
10. Did today's action improve my system, or only feed excitement?

### Future Distillation Targets

When future videos mention 著名刺客, 小鳄鱼, 退学炒股, 陈小群, or游资成长心法, look for:

- More exact definitions of主流题材.
- How 著名刺客 distinguishes strong-to-strong from fake strength.

- Exact low吸/DC examples on leaders.
- Whether `小资金满仓` is tied to market stage or personal curve.
- Specific static selection workflow and watchlist size.
- Concrete rules for lunch-time pull-ups and when they are valid.
